# @molecule/app-ui-angular

Angular UI components for molecule.dev.

Provides standalone Angular components at parity with `@molecule/app-ui-react`:
every React export exists here under the same name, with the same props, the
same rendered DOM (ClassMap classes, attributes, inline styles, text) and the
same behaviour. The shared conformance kit
(`@molecule/app-framework-conformance`) proves the parity.

React-named exports include `Button`, `Card` (+ `CardHeader`, `CardTitle`,
`CardDescription`, `CardContent`, `CardFooter`), `Input`, `Select`,
`Textarea`, `Checkbox`, `RadioGroup`, `Switch`, `Accordion`, `Alert`,
`Avatar`, `Badge`, `EmptyState`, `Icon`, `Label`, `Progress`, `Separator`,
`Skeleton`, `Spinner`, `Tabs`, `Table`, `Pagination`, `Tooltip`, `Modal`,
`Toast` (+ `ToastContainer`, `ToastProvider`), `ConfirmDialog`,
`PromptDialog`, `Dropdown` (+ `DropdownLabel`, `DropdownSeparator`),
`Form`, `FormField`, `LanguagePicker`, `PageHeader`, `PageShell`,
`ResponsiveAppShell` (+ its `TopBar`, `Sidebar`, `Drawer`, `Content`
parts), `SidebarUserCard`, `UserMenu`, `UserMenuPopover` (+ its `Trigger`,
`Panel`, `SignOut` parts), `AuthGuard`, `ConfirmButton`,
`OptimisticToggleButton`, `ThemeToggle`, and the hooks `useToast`,
`usePanelClose`, `useIsDesktop` and `useUserMenuPopoverClose`.

> **Auto-generated, AI-first package reference** for the [molecule.dev](https://molecule.dev) ecosystem.
> It is written to be read by coding agents as much as by people, and is generated from this
> package's source — edit `src/index.ts` JSDoc, not this file.

## Quick Start

```ts
import { Component } from '@angular/core'
import { setClassMap } from '@molecule/app-ui'
import { classMap } from '@molecule/app-ui-tailwind'
import { Button, Input } from '@molecule/app-ui-angular'

// Once at startup, before the first component renders:
setClassMap(classMap)

@Component({
  selector: 'app-save-panel',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [Button, Input],
  template: `
    <input mol-input placeholder="Name" [value]="name" (change)="onName($event)" />
    <button mol-button color="primary" [loading]="saving" (click)="save()">Save</button>
  `,
})
class SavePanel {
  name = ''
  saving = false
  onName(event: Event): void {
    this.name = (event.target as HTMLInputElement).value
  }
  save(): void {}
}
```

## Type

`framework`

## Installation

```bash
npm install @molecule/app-ui-angular @angular/common @angular/core @angular/platform-browser @molecule/app-angular @molecule/app-i18n @molecule/app-icons @molecule/app-ui
```

## API

### Interfaces

#### `CSSProperties`

Framework-agnostic CSS properties.
Mirrors React.CSSProperties but without React dependency.

```typescript
interface CSSProperties {
  [key: string]: string | number | undefined
}
```

#### `BaseProps`

Base props shared by all components.

```typescript
interface BaseProps {
  /**
   * Additional CSS class name(s).
   */
  className?: string

  /**
   * Inline styles.
   */
  style?: CSSProperties

  /**
   * Test ID for automated testing.
   */
  testId?: string

  /**
   * Automation ID for AI agents and E2E tests. Maps to the `data-mol-id`
   * HTML attribute. Use `molId()` from `./automation.js` to generate
   * semantic IDs. (Tooling only — screen readers do not expose `data-*`
   * attributes; accessible names come from labels/`aria-*`.)
   */
  automationId?: string

  /**
   * Whether the component is disabled.
   */
  disabled?: boolean
}
```

#### `HTMLElementProps`

Base props for HTML elements.
Framework bindings should extend this with framework-specific attributes.

```typescript
interface HTMLElementProps extends BaseProps {
  id?: string
  title?: string
  tabIndex?: number
  role?: string
  'aria-label'?: string
  'aria-labelledby'?: string
  'aria-describedby'?: string
  'aria-hidden'?: boolean
  'aria-disabled'?: boolean
  'aria-expanded'?: boolean
  'aria-selected'?: boolean
  'aria-checked'?: boolean | 'mixed'
  'aria-pressed'?: boolean | 'mixed'
  'aria-invalid'?: boolean
  'aria-required'?: boolean
  'aria-readonly'?: boolean
  'aria-busy'?: boolean
  'aria-live'?: 'off' | 'polite' | 'assertive'
  onClick?: MouseEventHandler
  onDoubleClick?: MouseEventHandler
  onMouseEnter?: MouseEventHandler
  onMouseLeave?: MouseEventHandler
  onFocus?: FocusEventHandler
  onBlur?: FocusEventHandler
  onKeyDown?: KeyboardEventHandler
  onKeyUp?: KeyboardEventHandler
  onKeyPress?: KeyboardEventHandler
}
```

#### `InputElementProps`

Base props for input elements.

```typescript
interface InputElementProps extends HTMLElementProps {
  name?: string
  value?: string | number | readonly string[]
  defaultValue?: string | number | readonly string[]
  placeholder?: string
  required?: boolean
  readOnly?: boolean
  autoFocus?: boolean
  autoComplete?: string
  maxLength?: number
  minLength?: number
  pattern?: string
  onChange?: ChangeEventHandler
  onInput?: FormEventHandler
}
```

#### `ButtonElementProps`

Base props for button elements.

```typescript
interface ButtonElementProps extends HTMLElementProps {
  /**
   * Defaults to `'button'` in every framework binding, so a button never submits a
   * form by accident. A form's primary action must set `type="submit"`: Enter then
   * submits the form, and tests that select `button[type="submit"]` find it.
   */
  type?: 'button' | 'submit' | 'reset'
  name?: string
  value?: string
  form?: string
}
```

#### `RadioGroupProps`

Props for the RadioGroup component.

```typescript
interface RadioGroupProps<T = string> extends BaseProps {
  /**
   * Radio options.
   */
  options: RadioOption<T>[]

  /**
   * Current value.
   */
  value?: T

  /**
   * Change handler.
   */
  onChange?: (value: T) => void

  /**
   * Radio size.
   */
  size?: Size

  /**
   * Group label.
   */
  label?: string

  /**
   * Shared `name` attribute for the group's radio inputs (used for native
   * form submission). When omitted, a unique per-instance name is generated
   * so separate groups never merge — the visible `label` is deliberately
   * NOT used as the name, because two groups with the same label (e.g. two
   * "Size" pickers) would otherwise form ONE native radio group and
   * deselect each other.
   */
  name?: string

  /**
   * Layout direction.
   */
  direction?: 'horizontal' | 'vertical'

  /**
   * Error message.
   */
  error?: string
}
```

```typescript
   * Page change handler.
   */
  onChange: (page: number) => void

  /**
   * Number of sibling pages to show.
   */
  siblings?: number

  /**
   * Number of boundary pages to show.
   */
  boundaries?: number

  /**
   * Pagination size.
   */
  size?: Size

  /**
   * Whether to show first/last buttons.
   */
  showFirstLast?: boolean

  /**
   * Whether to show previous/next buttons.
   */
  showPrevNext?: boolean

  /**
   * Accessible labels for pagination controls.
   */
  labels?: {
    nav?: string
    first?: string
    previous?: string
    next?: string
    last?: string
    goToPage?: (page: number) => string
  }
}
```

#### `SkeletonProps`

Props for the Skeleton loading placeholder component.

```typescript
interface SkeletonProps extends BaseProps {
  /**
   * Skeleton width.
   */
  width?: string | number

  /**
   * Skeleton height.
   */
  height?: string | number

  /**
   * Whether the skeleton is circular.
   */
  circle?: boolean

  /**
   * Border radius.
   */
  borderRadius?: string | number

  /**
   * Animation type.
   */
  animation?: 'pulse' | 'wave' | 'none'
}
```

#### `AccordionItemData`

One accordion item (React's `AccordionItem`).

```typescript
interface AccordionItemData {
  /** The item's value (identity for expansion state). */
  value: string
  /** The header (text, or a TemplateRef from a node value). */
  header?: unknown
  /** The body (text, or a TemplateRef from a node value). */
  content?: unknown
  /** Whether the item's trigger is disabled. */
  disabled?: boolean
}
```

#### `RadioOption`

One radio option.

```typescript
interface RadioOption {
  /** The option's value. */
  value: string
  /** The option's label (text). */
  label?: unknown
  /** Whether this option is disabled. */
  disabled?: boolean
}
```

#### `ShellContextValue`

The shell context value (React's `ShellContextValue`).

```typescript
interface ShellContextValue {
  /** Whether the desktop shell (sidebar) is mounted. */
  readonly isDesktop: boolean
  /** The resolved sidebar/drawer width in pixels. */
  readonly sidebarWidthPx: number
  /** Whether the mobile drawer is open. */
  readonly drawerOpen: boolean
  /** Opens the drawer. */
  openDrawer: () => void
  /** Closes the drawer. */
  closeDrawer: () => void
  /** The drawer panel's DOM id. */
  readonly drawerId: string
  /** Whether a `Drawer` sub-component is part of the composition. */
  readonly hasDrawer: boolean
  /**
   * Registers the drawer's presence.
   * @param present - Whether a drawer mounted.
   */
  registerDrawer: (present: boolean) => void
}
```

#### `SelectPropsOption`

One select option (React's `SelectOption`).

```typescript
interface SelectPropsOption {
  /** The option's value. */
  value: string
  /** The option's label. */
  label?: unknown
  /** Whether the option is disabled. */
  disabled?: boolean
  /** Group heading the option belongs to. */
  group?: string
}
```

#### `ToastContextValue`

The toast context value (React's `ToastContextValue`).

```typescript
interface ToastContextValue {
  /** The current toasts, each with its id. */
  toasts: (ToastProps & { id: string })[]
  /**
   * Adds a toast.
   * @param toast - The toast to add.
   * @returns Its generated id.
   */
  addToast: (toast: Omit<ToastProps, 'onDismiss'>) => string
  /**
   * Removes a toast by id.
   * @param id - The toast's id.
   */
  removeToast: (id: string) => void
}
```

#### `UserMenuPopoverContext`

The popover context value (React's `UserMenuPopoverContextValue`).

```typescript
interface UserMenuPopoverContext {
  /** Whether the popover is open. */
  readonly open: boolean
  /**
   * Sets the open state.
   * @param open - The new state.
   */
  setOpen: (open: boolean) => void
  /** The resolved display name. */
  readonly displayName: string
  /** The resolved account email. */
  readonly email: string | undefined
  /** The id linking the trigger's `aria-controls` to the panel. */

}
```

#### `FormFieldContext`

The values `FormField` injects into its single child field.

```typescript
interface FormFieldContext {
  /** The field id (`FormField`'s `name`, or a generated id). */
  id?: string
  /** The id of the error/hint paragraph the field should describe itself with. */
  describedBy?: string
  /** Whether the field is invalid (`FormField` render an error). */
  invalid?: boolean
}
```

#### `OptimisticValueOptions`

Options for {@link createOptimisticValue}.

```typescript
interface OptimisticValueOptions<T> {
  /** The loaded server value. */
  value: T
  /** Saves the desired value; resolve with the confirmed value. */
  commit: OptimisticCommit<T>
  /** Equality override (defaults to {@link shallowEqual}). */
  equals?: (a: T, b: T) => boolean
  /** Called with a failed save after rollback. */
  onError?: (error: unknown, value: T) => void
}
```

#### `OptimisticValueState`

The externally visible state.

```typescript
interface OptimisticValueState<T> {
  /** The value to render. */
  value: T
  /** Whether a save is in flight. */
  pending: boolean
  /** The newest failure, when the newest save failed. */
  error: unknown
}
```

#### `OptimisticValue`

The controller.

```typescript
interface OptimisticValue<T> {
  /**
   * Gets the current state.
   * @returns The state snapshot.
   */
  getState(): OptimisticValueState<T>
  /**
   * Sets the desired value (starts or queues a save).
   * @param next - The desired value.
   */
  set(next: T): void
  /**
   * Updates the desired value from the current desired one.
   * @param change - Maps current to next.
   */
  update(change: (current: T) => T): void
  /**
   * Syncs a newer server value.
   * @param value - The confirmed server value.
   */
  sync(value: T): void
  /**
   * Subscribes to state changes.
   * @param listener - Called on every publish.
   * @returns Unsubscribe.
   */
  subscribe(listener: (state: OptimisticValueState<T>) => void): () => void
  /**
   * Resolves when the current save settles.
   * @returns The settled state.
   */
  settled(): Promise<OptimisticValueState<T>>
}
```

#### `ResolvedIcon`

The icon data plus the resolved class name, precomputed so the template
only binds (recomputing `getIcon` per CD pass would be wasteful and can
throw mid-template).

```typescript
interface ResolvedIcon {
  /** The bonded icon data. */
  icon: IconData
  /** Class for the `<svg>` (already joined, possibly empty). */
  className: string
  /** Whether the icon carries stroke styling. */
  stroked: boolean
  /** `0 0 20 20` fallback applied when the icon data has no viewBox. */
  viewBox: string
}
```

### Types

#### `Children`

Framework-agnostic child content.
Use `unknown` to allow any framework's node type (ReactNode, VNode, etc.).

```typescript
type Children = unknown
```

#### `EventHandler`

Framework-agnostic event handler.

```typescript
type EventHandler<E = Event> = (event: E) => void
```

- `event` — The DOM event object.

#### `MouseEventHandler`

Framework-agnostic mouse event handler.

```typescript
type MouseEventHandler = EventHandler<MouseEvent>
```

#### `KeyboardEventHandler`

Framework-agnostic keyboard event handler.

```typescript
type KeyboardEventHandler = EventHandler<KeyboardEvent>
```

#### `FocusEventHandler`

Framework-agnostic focus event handler.

```typescript
type FocusEventHandler = EventHandler<FocusEvent>
```

#### `ChangeEventHandler`

Framework-agnostic change event handler.

```typescript
type ChangeEventHandler = EventHandler<Event>
```

#### `FormEventHandler`

Framework-agnostic form event handler.

```typescript
type FormEventHandler = EventHandler<Event>
```

#### `Size`

Standard size scale used across all molecule UI components (buttons, inputs, badges, etc.).

```typescript
type Size = 'xs' | 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg' | 'xl'
```

#### `ColorVariant`

Semantic color variants used across components for status indication.

```typescript
type ColorVariant = 'primary' | 'secondary' | 'success' | 'warning' | 'error' | 'info'
```

#### `ButtonVariant`

Button visual variant styles.

```typescript
type ButtonVariant = 'solid' | 'outline' | 'ghost' | 'link'
```

#### `InputType`

Allowed HTML input type attribute values for the Input component.

```typescript
type InputType =
  | 'text'
  | 'email'
  | 'password'
  | 'number'
  | 'tel'
  | 'url'
  | 'search'
  | 'date'
  | 'time'
  | 'datetime-local'
```

#### `ModalSize`

Modal size variants including full-screen.

```typescript
type ModalSize = 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg' | 'xl' | 'full'
```

#### `TooltipPlacement`

Position where a tooltip renders relative to its trigger element (top, bottom, left, right, and corner variants).

```typescript
type TooltipPlacement =
  'top' | 'bottom' | 'left' | 'right' | 'top-start' | 'top-end' | 'bottom-start' | 'bottom-end'
```

#### `OptimisticCommit`

What a commit reports back.

```typescript
type OptimisticCommit<T> = (
  next: T,
  previous: T,
  confirm: (change: (current: T) => T) => void,
) => Promise<T | void>
```

### Classes

#### `Accordion`

Accordion component.

The host is the `div.accordionRoot` React renders. Items carry their
header/content as text or TemplateRef node values; expansion state is
internal unless the `value` input controls it (React's controlled /
uncontrolled pair), and ids are generated per item value so replacing the
`items` array keeps them stable (React's `useId` per item component).

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-accordion]',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [MolIconSvg, NgTemplateOutlet],
  inputs: [
    'items',
    'value',
    'defaultValue',
    'multiple',
    'collapsible',
    'className',
    'style',
    'testId',
  ],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
  },
  template: `@for (item of items ?? []; track item.value) {
    <div [class]="cm().accordionItem" [attr.data-state]="isOpen(item.value) ? 'open' : 'closed'">
      <button
        type="button"
        [attr.id]="ids(item.value).trigger"
        [class]="cm().cn(cm().accordion({ variant: 'default' }), cm().accordionTriggerBase)"
        (click)="toggle(item.value)"
        [attr.aria-expanded]="isOpen(item.value) ? 'true' : 'false'"
        [attr.aria-controls]="ids(item.value).content"
        [attr.data-state]="isOpen(item.value) ? 'open' : 'closed'"
        [disabled]="item.disabled"
      >
        @if (asTemplate(item.header); as tpl) {
          <ng-container [ngTemplateOutlet]="tpl" />
        } @else {
          {{ nodeText(item.header) }}
        }
        <svg mol-icon [data]="chevron()" />
      </button>
      <div
        [attr.id]="ids(item.value).content"
        role="region"
        [attr.aria-labelledby]="ids(item.value).trigger"
        [attr.data-state]="isOpen(item.value) ? 'open' : 'closed'"
        [class]="cm().accordionContent"
        [style.display]="isOpen(item.value) ? 'block' : 'none'"
      >
        <div [class]="cm().accordionContentInner">
          @if (asTemplate(item.content); as tpl) {
            <ng-container [ngTemplateOutlet]="tpl" />
          } @else {
            {{ nodeText(item.content) }}
          }
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  }`,
})
export class Accordion {
  /** The items to render. */
  items?: AccordionItemData[]
  /** Controlled expanded value(s). */
  value?: string | string[]
  /** Uncontrolled initial expanded value(s). */
  defaultValue?: string | string[]
  /** Whether several items may stay expanded. */
  multiple?: boolean
  /** Whether the last expanded item may collapse. */
  collapsible?: boolean
  /** Extra classes joined onto the root classes. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** `data-testid` for the root. */
  testId?: string

  /** Template helpers (templates see only class members). */
  readonly asTemplate = asTemplate
  /** Renders a plain-string node value. */
  readonly nodeText = nodeText

  /** Fires the new expansion state (single value, or array in multiple mode). */
  @Output() readonly change = new EventEmitter<string | string[]>()

  private internalExpanded: string[] | null = null
  private readonly idMap = new Map<string, { trigger: string; content: string }>()
  private idCount = 0

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Computes the root class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().accordionRoot, this.className)
  }

  /**
   * Serializes the inline style.
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }

  /**
   * The chevron glyph every trigger renders.
   * @returns The resolved icon.
   */
  chevron(): ResolvedIcon {
    return resolveIcon('chevron-down', this.cm().accordionChevron)
  }

  /**
   * Normalizes a value prop to the expanded list (React's `normalizeValue`).
   *
   * @param v - The single value, array, or undefined.
   * @returns The expanded values.
   */
  private normalize(v: string | string[] | undefined): string[] {
    if (v === undefined) return []
    return Array.isArray(v) ? v : [v]
  }

  /**
   * The expanded items: the controlled `value` when given, else the internal
   * state (seeded from `defaultValue` at first use).
   * @returns The expanded values.
   */
  expandedItems(): string[] {
    if (this.value !== undefined) return this.normalize(this.value)
    if (this.internalExpanded === null) this.internalExpanded = this.normalize(this.defaultValue)
    return this.internalExpanded
  }

  /**
   * Whether one item is expanded.
   *
   * @param value - The item value.
   * @returns True when expanded.
   */
  isOpen(value: string): boolean {
    return this.expandedItems().includes(value)
  }

  /**
   * The stable per-item ids (React generates them per item component; keying
   * by item value keeps them stable across an `items` replacement).
   *
   * @param value - The item value.
   * @returns The trigger and content ids.
   */
  ids(value: string): { trigger: string; content: string } {
    let ids = this.idMap.get(value)
    if (!ids) {
      ids = { trigger: `t${this.idCount}`, content: `c${this.idCount}` }
      this.idCount++
      this.idMap.set(value, ids)
    }
    return ids
  }

  /**
   * React's `toggleItem`: collapse guards `collapsible`, expand replaces or
   * appends per `multiple`, internal state updates only when uncontrolled,
   * and `onChange` receives the new state (single value or array).
   *
   * @param itemValue - The toggled item's value.
   */
  toggle(itemValue: string): void {
    const current = this.expandedItems()
    let next: string[]
    if (current.includes(itemValue)) {
      if (this.collapsible === false && current.length === 1) return
      next = current.filter((v) => v !== itemValue)
    } else {
      next = this.multiple ? [...current, itemValue] : [itemValue]
    }
    if (this.value === undefined) this.internalExpanded = next
    if (this.multiple) this.change.emit(next)
    else this.change.emit(next[0] ?? '')
  }
}
```

#### `Alert`

Alert component.

`role` follows `live` (assertive `alert` by default, polite `status` for a
banner that is part of the page's normal content).

React renders `{(icon || defaultIcon) && <span className={alertIconWrapper}>…}`:
the icon wrapper span exists only when a custom icon is given or the status
has a default glyph, and its content is the custom icon XOR the default
glyph. Here the span and the default glyph both always exist in the
template (projection capture points) and are detached from the DOM when
React would not render them ({@link SlotWrapper}); the decision is made at
view init, when view queries resolve.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-alert]',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [MolIconSvg],
  inputs: [
    'title',
    'status',
    'variant',
    'live',
    'dismissible',
    'dismissLabel',
    'className',
    'style',
    'testId',
  ],
  outputs: ['dismiss'],
  host: {
    '[attr.role]': 'live ? "alert" : "status"',
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
  },
  template: `<span [class]="cm().alertIconWrapper" #iconSlot>
      <svg mol-icon [data]="resolvedDefaultIcon()" #defaultGlyph />
      <ng-content select="[slot=icon]"></ng-content>
    </span>
    <div [class]="cm().alertContent">
      @if (title) {
        <h5 [class]="cm().alertTitle">{{ title }}</h5>
      }
      <div [class]="cm().alertDescription"><ng-content></ng-content></div>
    </div>
    @if (dismissible) {
      <button
        type="button"
        [class]="cm().alertDismiss"
        [attr.aria-label]="dismissText()"
        (click)="dismiss.emit($event)"
      >
        <svg mol-icon [data]="closeIcon()" />
      </button>
    }`,
})
export class Alert implements AfterViewInit, DoCheck {
  /** Alert title. */
  title?: string
  /** Status color. */
  status: ColorVariant = 'info'
  /** Visual variant. */
  variant?: 'solid' | 'subtle' | 'outline' | 'left-accent'
  /** Whether the alert announces assertively (default true). */
  live = true
  /** Whether a dismiss button renders. */
  dismissible?: boolean
  /** Accessible label for the dismiss button. */
  dismissLabel?: string
  /** Extra classes. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** `data-testid`. */
  testId?: string

  /** Fires the raw click event when the dismiss button is pressed. */
  @Output() readonly dismiss = new EventEmitter<MouseEvent>()

  /** The icon wrapper span (projection capture point). */
  @ViewChild('iconSlot') iconSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLSpanElement>
  /** The default status glyph (detached when a custom icon is projected). */
  @ViewChild('defaultGlyph', { read: ElementRef }) defaultGlyph?: ElementRef<SVGSVGElement>

  private hasCustomIcon = false

  /**
   * Reactive translation, read in the injection context. Its `t` reads the
   * locale, so the view re-renders the text on a locale switch (the static `t`
   * does not).
   */
  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  private readonly sync = new SlotWrapper(inject(ElementRef), inject(Renderer2))

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * The class-map variant for the status.
   * @returns The mapped variant.
   */
  get alertVariant(): 'default' | 'info' | 'success' | 'warning' | 'error' {
    return STATUS_VARIANT[this.status] || 'default'
  }

  /**
   * The default status glyph (none for the `default` variant, matching
   * React's `statusIconMap` gap).
   * @returns The resolved icon, or null when the variant has no glyph.
   */
  defaultIcon(): ResolvedIcon | null {
    const name = STATUS_ICON[this.alertVariant]
    return name ? resolveIcon(name, this.cm().iconMd) : null
  }

  /**
   * A non-null glyph to bind (an empty resolved icon when the variant has
   * none; that svg is detached from the DOM anyway).
   * @returns The resolved icon data.
   */
  resolvedDefaultIcon(): ResolvedIcon {
    return (
      this.defaultIcon() ?? {
        icon: { paths: [] },
        className: '',
        stroked: false,
        viewBox: '0 0 20 20',
      }
    )
  }

  /**
   * The dismiss button's X glyph.
   * @returns The resolved icon.
   */
  closeIcon(): ResolvedIcon {
    return resolveIcon('x-mark', this.cm().iconSm)
  }

  /**
   * The dismiss button's accessible label.
   * @returns The label text.
   */
  dismissText(): string {
    return (
      this.dismissLabel ?? this.i18n.t('ui.alert.dismiss', undefined, { defaultValue: 'Dismiss' })
    )
  }

  /**
   * Computes the alert class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().alert({ variant: this.alertVariant }),
      this.variant === 'left-accent' && this.cm().alertLeftAccent({ variant: this.alertVariant }),
      this.className,
    )
  }

  /**
   * Serializes the inline style.
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }

  /**
   * Decides icon presence at view init (view queries resolve only then) and
   * applies React's conditions.
   */
  ngAfterViewInit(): void {
    const span = this.iconSlot?.nativeElement
    const glyph = this.defaultGlyph?.nativeElement
    // Projected nodes are the span's children other than the default glyph.
    this.hasCustomIcon = !!span && hasNodesExcept(span, glyph)
    this.syncIcon()
  }

  /**
   * Re-reconciles on later passes.
   */
  ngDoCheck(): void {
    this.syncIcon()
  }

  /**
   * Applies React's `{(icon || defaultIcon) && <span>{icon || defaultIcon}</span>}`.
   */
  private syncIcon(): void {
    const span = this.iconSlot?.nativeElement
    this.sync.sync(
      this.defaultGlyph?.nativeElement,
      !this.hasCustomIcon && this.defaultIcon() !== null,
      span,
    )
    this.sync.sync(span, this.hasCustomIcon || this.defaultIcon() !== null)
  }
}
```

#### `AuthGuard`

Route-element guard for authenticated sections of a routing tree.

While the auth status is `'loading'` it renders the `loadingFallback` slot
(or the default `data-mol-id="auth-guard-loading"` tag); while
`'unauthenticated'` off the login path it renders nothing and redirects to
`loginPath` (state `from` carries the attempted location); otherwise it
renders the projected children. The host is unwrapped by the DOM normalizer
(`data-parity-unwrap`) and `display: contents` keeps it fragment-like —
React's root is a fragment. On the login path itself the guard renders its
children, so a guard wrapping the whole route tree does not redirect in a
loop.

The projected children need one stable capture point, so they live in an
always-mounted wrapper the guard moves into (or out of) the host itself:
React renders them bare, never inside a wrapper element, and renders
nothing at all while redirecting.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'mol-auth-guard',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: ['loadingKey', 'loadingDefault', 'loginPath', 'onAuthenticated'],
  host: {
    'data-parity-unwrap': '',
    style: 'display: contents',
  },
  template: `<div data-mol-id="auth-guard-loading" #defaultLoading>{{ loadingText() }}</div>
    <span #fallbackWrap><ng-content select="[slot=loadingFallback]"></ng-content></span>
    <span #childrenWrap><ng-content></ng-content></span>`,
})
export class AuthGuard implements AfterViewInit {
  /**
   * i18n key for the default loading-tag text (default `common.loading`).
   * Ignored when a `loadingFallback` slot is projected.
   */
  loadingKey?: string
  /** Fallback text if the i18n key is missing (default `Loading...`). */
  loadingDefault?: string
  /** Path to redirect to when unauthenticated (default `/login`). */
  loginPath?: string
  /** Fired when `isAuthenticated` becomes true (also when true at mount). */
  onAuthenticated?: () => void

  /** The default loading tag. */
  @ViewChild('defaultLoading') defaultLoading?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>
  /** The `loadingFallback` slot capture (always mounted). */
  @ViewChild('fallbackWrap') fallbackWrap?: ElementRef<HTMLSpanElement>
  /** The children capture; its nodes are moved bare into the host to render. */
  @ViewChild('childrenWrap') childrenWrap?: ElementRef<HTMLSpanElement>

  /** Auth state, read in the component's injection context. */
  readonly auth = useAuth()
  /** Router state and actions, read in the component's injection context. */
  readonly router = useRouter()

  /**
   * Reactive translation, read in the injection context. Its `t` reads the
   * locale, so the view re-renders the text on a locale switch (the static `t`
   * does not).
   */
  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  private readonly sync = new SlotWrapper(inject(ElementRef), inject(Renderer2))
  private readonly host = inject(ElementRef)
  private hasFallback = false
  private lastAuthenticated = false
  /** The login path the guard navigated to for the current redirect state. */
  private redirectedTo: string | null = null
  /** The children nodes currently adopted into the host. */
  private adopted: Node[] = []

  /**
   * Runs the first reconciliation after the view init (slot detection) and
   * again whenever the auth status or the location changes — the effect
   * tracks both signals directly, so the guard re-evaluates even though no
   * template binding reads them.
   */
  private readonly guardEffect = effect(() => {
    this.auth.status()
    this.router.location()
    this.reconcile()
  })

  /**
   * Detects the `loadingFallback` slot presence (view queries resolve only by
   * view init).
   */
  ngAfterViewInit(): void {
    this.hasFallback = hasNodes(this.fallbackWrap?.nativeElement)
    this.reconcile()
  }

  /**
   * Applies React's branch for the current auth status and location, fires
   * `onAuthenticated` on its rising edge, and redirects once per entry into
   * the redirect state.
   */
  private reconcile(): void {
    const status = this.auth.status()
    const authenticated = this.auth.isAuthenticated()
    if (this.onAuthenticated && authenticated && !this.lastAuthenticated) this.onAuthenticated()
    this.lastAuthenticated = authenticated

    const pathname = this.router.location().pathname
    const target = this.loginPath ?? '/login'
    const redirecting = status === 'unauthenticated' && pathname !== loginPathname(target)
    if (redirecting) {
      if (this.redirectedTo !== target) {
        this.redirectedTo = target
        this.router.navigate(target, {
          replace: true,
          state: { from: { ...this.router.location() } },
        })
      }
    } else {
      this.redirectedTo = null
    }

    const branch = status === 'loading' ? 'loading' : redirecting ? 'redirect' : 'children'
    this.renderBranch(branch)
  }

  /**
   * Shows exactly one branch's content, the way React's early returns do.
   *
   * @param branch - The branch to render.
   */
  private renderBranch(branch: 'loading' | 'redirect' | 'children'): void {
    const host = this.host.nativeElement as HTMLElement
    const wrap = this.childrenWrap?.nativeElement
    if (wrap) {
      if (branch === 'children') {
        while (wrap.firstChild) {
          const node = wrap.firstChild
          host.appendChild(node)
          this.adopted.push(node)
        }
      } else {
        for (const node of this.adopted) {
          if (node.parentElement === host) wrap.appendChild(node)
        }
        this.adopted = []
      }
      // The capture span itself never renders (React's children are bare).
      this.sync.sync(this.childrenWrap?.nativeElement, false)
    }
    this.sync.sync(this.defaultLoading?.nativeElement, branch === 'loading' && !this.hasFallback)
    this.sync.sync(this.fallbackWrap?.nativeElement, branch === 'loading' && this.hasFallback)
  }

  /**
   * The localized default loading text.
   * @returns The translated text.
   */
  loadingText(): string {
    return this.i18n.t(
      this.loadingKey ?? 'common.loading',
      {},
      {
        defaultValue: this.loadingDefault ?? 'Loading...',
      },
    )
  }
}
```

#### `Avatar`

Avatar component.

Renders the image while it loads; an image error (or no `src`) swaps to the
fallback: projected `[slot=fallback]` content, the name's initials, or a
user glyph. The image/fallback swap is a signal-driven `@if` (an error
re-renders); the three fallback variants are always-mounted capture points
detached from the DOM per React's ternary, since projected-content presence
is only knowable at view init ({@link SlotWrapper}).

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-avatar]',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [MolIconSvg],
  inputs: ['src', 'alt', 'name', 'size', 'rounded', 'className', 'style', 'testId'],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
  },
  template: `@if (!showFallback()) {
      <img
        [src]="src"
        [attr.alt]="altText()"
        [class]="cm().avatarImage"
        (error)="imageError.set(true)"
      />
    } @else {
      <div [class]="cm().avatarFallback" #fallbackEl>
        <ng-content select="[slot=fallback]"></ng-content>
        <span [class]="cm().avatarInitials" #initialsEl>{{ initials() }}</span>
        <svg mol-icon [data]="userIcon()" #userGlyph />
      </div>
    }`,
})
export class Avatar implements AfterViewChecked {
  /** Image source. */
  src?: string
  /** Image alt text. */
  alt?: string
  /** Display name (drives the initials fallback and default alt). */
  name?: string
  /** Avatar size preset or an exact pixel size. */
  size: 'xs' | 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg' | 'xl' | number = 'md'
  /** Whether the avatar is fully rounded (default true). */
  rounded = true
  /** Extra classes. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** `data-testid`. */
  testId?: string

  /** Set when the image fails to load. */
  readonly imageError = signal(false)

  /** The fallback container (React renders it only on the fallback branch). */
  @ViewChild('fallbackEl') fallbackEl?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>
  /** The initials span (detached unless the fallback branch picks it). */
  @ViewChild('initialsEl') initialsEl?: ElementRef<HTMLSpanElement>
  /** The user glyph (detached unless the fallback branch picks it). */
  @ViewChild('userGlyph', { read: ElementRef }) userGlyph?: ElementRef<SVGSVGElement>

  /**
   * Reactive translation, read in the injection context. Its `t` reads the
   * locale, so the view re-renders the text on a locale switch (the static `t`
   * does not).
   */
  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  private readonly sync = new SlotWrapper(inject(ElementRef), inject(Renderer2))

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Whether the fallback branch renders.
   * @returns True without a src or after an image error.
   */
  showFallback(): boolean {
    return !this.src || this.imageError()
  }

  /**
   * The image alt text (name or the localized default).
   * @returns The alt text.
   */
  altText(): string {
    return (
      this.alt || this.name || this.i18n.t('ui.avatar.alt', undefined, { defaultValue: 'Avatar' })
    )
  }

  /**
   * The initials for the given name.
   * @returns Up to two uppercase characters.
   */
  initials(): string {
    return getInitials(this.name)
  }

  /**
   * The fallback user glyph.
   * @returns The resolved icon.
   */
  userIcon(): ResolvedIcon {
    return resolveIcon('user', this.cm().avatarFallbackIcon)
  }

  /**
   * Computes the avatar class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    const avatarSize = typeof this.size === 'number' ? undefined : this.size
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().avatar({ size: avatarSize }),
      !this.rounded && this.cm().avatarSquare,
      this.className,
    )
  }

  /**
   * Serializes the merged inline style (an exact pixel size inline).
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    const customSize =
      typeof this.size === 'number' ? { width: this.size, height: this.size } : undefined
    return styleText({ ...this.style, ...customSize })
  }

  /**
   * Applies React's fallback ternary (`fallback ? fallback : name ? initials
   * : glyph`) after view queries have resolved for this pass. Projected
   * fallback nodes are exactly the container's children that are not the two
   * always-mounted template children.
   */
  ngAfterViewChecked(): void {
    const fallbackHost = this.fallbackEl?.nativeElement
    if (!fallbackHost) return
    const projected = Array.from(fallbackHost.childNodes).some(
      (node) =>
        node !== this.initialsEl?.nativeElement &&
        node !== this.userGlyph?.nativeElement &&
        (node.nodeType === 1 || (node.nodeType === 3 && (node.textContent ?? '').trim())),
    )
    this.sync.sync(this.initialsEl?.nativeElement, !projected && !!this.name, fallbackHost)
    this.sync.sync(this.userGlyph?.nativeElement, !projected && !this.name, fallbackHost)
  }
}
```

#### `Badge`

Badge component.

The host element is the `<span>` React renders (`span[mol-badge]`); the
color variant maps to `badge({ variant })` exactly as React's `Badge`.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'span[mol-badge]',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: ['color', 'variant', 'size', 'rounded', 'className', 'style', 'testId'],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
  },
  template: `<ng-content></ng-content>`,
})
export class Badge {
  /** Named color variant (`primary`, `success`, …). */
  color: ColorVariant = 'primary'
  /** Visual style (kept for React prop parity; the class map keys off `color`). */
  variant: 'solid' | 'outline' | 'subtle' = 'solid'
  /** Badge size. */
  size?: Size
  /** Whether the badge is fully rounded (default) or square. */
  rounded = true
  /** Extra classes joined onto the badge classes. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** `data-testid` for the badge. */
  testId?: string

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Computes the badge class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().badge({ variant: this.color, size: this.size }),
      !this.rounded && this.cm().badgeSquare,
      this.className,
    )
  }

  /**
   * Serializes the inline style.
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }
}
```

#### `SlotWrapper`

Attaches or detaches an always-mounted projection wrapper element from its
parent, remembering the re-insertion position with a comment anchor the DOM
normalizer strips.

React renders `{cond && <span>{slot}</span>}` — nothing when the condition
is false. Angular needs the `<ng-content>` capture point to stay mounted,
so the wrapper span always exists in the template and this helper removes
it from the DOM whenever React would not render it.

```typescript
class SlotWrapper {
  private readonly anchors = new Map<Node, Comment>()

  /**
   * Creates the wrapper helper.
   *
   * @param host - The component host element (default detach parent).
   * @param renderer - The DOM renderer.
   */
  constructor(
    private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLElement>,
    private readonly renderer: Renderer2,
  ) {}

  /**
   * Attaches or detaches the element (default parent: the component host).
   *
   * @param el - The wrapper element.
   * @param shouldAttach - Whether it should be in the DOM.
   * @param parent - Where the element lives when attached (default: host).
   */
  sync(el: Element | undefined | null, shouldAttach: boolean, parent?: Node): void {
    if (!el) return
    const host: Node = parent ?? this.host.nativeElement
    if (shouldAttach && !el.isConnected) {
      const anchor = this.anchors.get(el)
      if (anchor?.isConnected) {
        host.insertBefore(el, anchor)
        this.renderer.removeChild(host, anchor)
        this.anchors.delete(el)
      } else {
        host.appendChild(el)
      }
    } else if (!shouldAttach && el.isConnected) {
      // Placeholder comment keeps the re-insertion position stable; the
      // normalizer strips comment nodes, so it never shows in snapshots.
      const placeholder = this.renderer.createComment('slot')
      host.insertBefore(placeholder, el)
      this.anchors.set(el, placeholder)
      this.renderer.removeChild(host, el)
    }
  }
}
```

#### `Button`

Button component.

While `loading` is true, the native button is disabled and marked busy.
The host element is the `<button>` itself (`button[mol-button]`), matching
React's root exactly. Icon slots are projected as `[slot=leftIcon]` /
`[slot=rightIcon]`; each wrapping `<span>` renders only while the slot
carries nodes AND the button is not loading — React's
`{!loading && !!leftIcon && <span>…}` — implemented by detaching the
wrapper from the DOM (the projected nodes must have an always-mounted
capture point, so the wrapper lives in the template unconditionally).

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'button[mol-button]',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [Spinner],
  inputs: [
    'variant',
    'color',
    'size',
    'loading',
    'loadingText',
    'fullWidth',
    'className',
    'style',
    'testId',
    'disabled',
    'type',
  ],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
    '[attr.type]': 'type',
    '[disabled]': 'disabled || loading',
    '[attr.aria-busy]': 'loading ? "true" : "false"',
  },
  template: `@if (loading) {
      <div mol-spinner [size]="'sm'" [className]="cm().buttonIconLeft"></div>
    }
    <span [class]="cm().buttonIconLeft" #leftSlot
      ><ng-content select="[slot=leftIcon]"></ng-content
    ></span>
    @if (loading && loadingText) {
      <ng-container>{{ loadingText }}</ng-container>
    } @else {
      <ng-content></ng-content>
    }
    <span [class]="cm().buttonIconRight" #rightSlot
      ><ng-content select="[slot=rightIcon]"></ng-content
    ></span>`,
})
export class Button implements AfterViewInit, DoCheck {
  /** Button variant. */
  variant: ButtonVariant = 'solid'
  /** Button color. */
  color: ColorVariant = 'primary'
  /** Button size. */
  size: Size = 'md'
  /** Whether the button is in its loading (busy) state. */
  loading = false
  /** Text shown instead of children while loading. */
  loadingText?: string
  /** Whether the button stretches to fill its container. */
  fullWidth = false
  /** Extra classes joined onto the button classes. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** `data-testid` for the button. */
  testId?: string
  /** Disables the button. */
  disabled = false
  /** The button's `type` attribute. */
  type: 'button' | 'submit' | 'reset' = 'button'

  /** The left icon wrapper (always in the template; detached when unused). */
  @ViewChild('leftSlot') leftSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLSpanElement>
  /** The right icon wrapper. */
  @ViewChild('rightSlot') rightSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLSpanElement>

  private readonly sync = new SlotWrapper(inject(ElementRef), inject(Renderer2))

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Computes the button class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().button({
        variant: this.variant,
        color: this.color,
        size: this.size,
        fullWidth: this.fullWidth,
      }),
      this.className,
    )
  }

  /**
   * Serializes the inline style.
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }

  private hasLeft = false
  private hasRight = false

  /**
   * Detects slot content and detaches the wrappers React would not render.
   * View queries resolve only by view init (the first pass), so presence is
   * read here rather than at content init.
   */
  ngAfterViewInit(): void {
    const left = this.leftSlot?.nativeElement
    const right = this.rightSlot?.nativeElement
    this.hasLeft = left ? hasNodes(left) : false
    this.hasRight = right ? hasNodes(right) : false
    this.syncWrappers()
  }

  /**
   * Re-reconciles the wrappers when `loading` changes.
   */
  ngDoCheck(): void {
    this.syncWrappers()
  }

  /**
   * Applies React's `{!loading && !!icon}` wrapper conditions.
   */
  private syncWrappers(): void {
    this.sync.sync(this.leftSlot?.nativeElement, this.hasLeft && !this.loading)
    this.sync.sync(this.rightSlot?.nativeElement, this.hasRight && !this.loading)
  }
}
```

#### `Card`

Card component.

An interactive card renders `role="button"` and joins the keyboard tab
order; rest props (`aria-*`, `data-*`, `id`) land on the host.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-card]',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: ['variant', 'interactive', 'padding', 'className', 'style', 'testId'],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
    '[attr.role]': 'interactive ? "button" : null',
    '[attr.tabindex]': 'interactive ? 0 : null',
  },
  template: `<ng-content></ng-content>`,
})
export class Card {
  /** Card variant. */
  variant: 'default' | 'elevated' | 'outlined' | 'filled' | 'ghost' = 'elevated'
  /** Whether the card is keyboard-interactive. */
  interactive?: boolean
  /** Padding preset (`none` for no padding class). */
  padding: 'none' | 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg' = 'md'
  /** Extra classes. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** `data-testid`. */
  testId?: string

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Computes the card class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    const paddingClass = this.padding === 'none' ? '' : this.cm().cardPadding(this.padding)
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().card({ variant: VARIANT_MAP[this.variant] || 'default' }),
      paddingClass,
      this.interactive && this.cm().cardInteractive,
      this.className,
    )
  }

  /**
   * Serializes the inline style.
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }
}
```

#### `Checkbox`

Checkbox component.

The host is the `div.formFieldWrapper` React renders. The error message is
programmatically associated with the input through `aria-describedby`;
the input is React's `...rest` spread target, so rest attributes move
there at view init.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-checkbox]',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: [
    'label',
    'checked',
    'indeterminate',
    'size',
    'error',
    'className',
    'style',
    'testId',
    'disabled',
    'name',
    'id',
    'defaultChecked',
  ],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'cm().formFieldWrapper',
  },
  template: `<label [class]="cm().controlLabel">
      <input
        type="checkbox"
        [attr.id]="checkboxId()"
        [attr.name]="name"
        [attr.checked]="isChecked() ? '' : null"
        [disabled]="disabled"
        [attr.data-state]="checked ? 'checked' : 'unchecked'"
        [attr.aria-invalid]="error || fieldContext()?.invalid ? 'true' : 'false'"
        [attr.aria-describedby]="mergedDescribedBy()"
        [class]="classes()"
        [attr.style]="hostStyle()"
        [attr.data-testid]="testId"
        (click)="handleClick($event)"
        (change)="handleChange($event)"
        #input
      />
      <span [class]="cm().cn(cm().controlText, disabled && cm().controlDisabled)" #labelSlot
        >{{ label }}<ng-content select="[slot=label]"></ng-content
      ></span>
    </label>
    @if (error) {
      <p [attr.id]="errorId()" [class]="cm().cn(cm().formError, cm().sp('mt', 1))">{{ error }}</p>
    }`,
})
export class Checkbox implements AfterViewChecked {
  /** Checkbox label (text, or projected `[slot=label]` nodes). */
  label?: unknown
  /** Whether the checkbox is checked. */
  checked?: boolean
  /** Whether the checkbox renders the indeterminate state. */
  indeterminate?: boolean
  /** Checkbox size (kept for prop parity). */
  size?: unknown
  /** Error message (renders and associates via aria-describedby). */
  error?: string
  /** Extra classes joined onto the checkbox classes. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** `data-testid` for the input. */
  testId?: string
  /** Disables the checkbox. */
  disabled?: boolean
  /** Form field name. */
  name?: string
  /** Explicit id (defaults to the name). */
  id?: string
  /** Uncontrolled initial checked state (renders as the checked attribute). */
  defaultChecked?: boolean

  /**
   * The controlled-or-default checked state (React: `checked ?? defaultChecked`).
   * @returns True when the checked attribute renders.
   */
  isChecked(): boolean {
    return (this.checked ?? this.defaultChecked) === true
  }

  /** The genuine native `change` event. */
  @Output() readonly change = new EventEmitter<Event>()

  /** The native input (React's `...rest` spread target). */
  @ViewChild('input') input?: ElementRef<HTMLInputElement>
  /** The label text wrapper. */
  @ViewChild('labelSlot') labelSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLSpanElement>

  private readonly host = inject(ElementRef)
  /** The `aria-describedby` the caller spread onto the host (React's rest). */
  private restDescribedBy: string | null = null
  /** Whether the rest-describedby value was captured. */
  private restDescribedByCaptured = false
  private readonly sync = new SlotWrapper(inject(ElementRef), inject(Renderer2))
  private hasLabel = false
  private syncIndeterminate = false

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * The id of the label-side element (`id` or `name`).
   * @returns The id value or undefined.
   */
  checkboxId(): string | undefined {
    return this.id || this.fieldContext()?.id || this.name
  }

  /**
   * The error message id (a stable generated id, renumbered consistently by
   * the DOM normalizer like React's `useId`).
   * @returns The error element id.
   */
  errorId(): string {
    return `${this.checkboxId() ?? 'r0'}-error`
  }

  /**
   * The nearest enclosing `FormField` context (React's cloned props).
   * @returns The context, or null.
   */
  fieldContext(): FormFieldContext | null {
    return fieldContext(this.host.nativeElement as HTMLElement)
  }

  /**
   * The final `aria-describedby`: the enclosing FormField's error/hint
   * target, then this checkbox's own error (React's cloneElement join).
   * @returns The attribute value, or null.
   */
  mergedDescribedBy(): string | null {
    return (
      this.fieldContext()?.describedBy ??
      this.restDescribedBy ??
      (this.error ? this.errorId() : null)
    )
  }

  /**
   * Computes the checkbox class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().checkbox({ error: !!this.error }), this.className)
  }

  /**
   * Serializes the inline style for the input.
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }

  /**
   * React's checkbox `onChange` fires on the checkbox's CLICK event (React's
   * change-event plugin maps checkbox/radio changes to the click), so the
   * click event object is what callers receive.
   *
   * @param event - The native click event.
   */
  handleClick(event: Event): void {
    this.justClicked = true
    this.change.emit(event)
    queueMicrotask(() => (this.justClicked = false))
  }

  /**
   * A native change that did not come from this tick's click (a synthetic
   * `dispatchEvent`) is forwarded like React would; the event never reaches
   * the host, where the `change` output binding would record it twice. A
   * CONTROLLED checkbox (`checked` prop given) is restored to the prop value
   * afterwards, exactly like React's controlled-input state restore.
   *
   * @param event - The native change event.
   */
  handleChange(event: Event): void {
    event.stopPropagation()
    if (!this.justClicked) this.change.emit(event)
    if (this.checked !== undefined && this.input) this.input.nativeElement.checked = !!this.checked
  }

  private justClicked = false

  /**
   * Applies the indeterminate DOM flag (a property, not an attribute) and
   * React's `{!!label && <span>}` label condition.
   */
  ngAfterViewChecked(): void {
    const input = this.input?.nativeElement
    if (input) {
      if (!this.restDescribedByCaptured) {
        this.restDescribedBy = this.host.nativeElement.getAttribute('aria-describedby')
        this.restDescribedByCaptured = true
      }
      moveRestAttributes(this.host.nativeElement, input)
      if (this.syncIndeterminate !== !!this.indeterminate) {
        input.indeterminate = !!this.indeterminate
        this.syncIndeterminate = !!this.indeterminate
      }
    }
    this.hasLabel = !!this.label || hasNodes(this.labelSlot?.nativeElement)
    this.sync.sync(
      this.labelSlot?.nativeElement,
      this.hasLabel,
      this.labelSlot?.nativeElement.parentElement ?? undefined,
    )
  }
}
```

#### `ConfirmButton`

ConfirmButton component.

The host is the `<button>` itself (`button[mol-confirm-button]`). The three
labels are projected (`[slot=label]`, `[slot=confirmLabel]`,
`[slot=pendingLabel]`); each capture span stays detached from the DOM and
the visible phase's projected children are moved onto the button — React
renders exactly one label and no wrapper element. When the slot for the
armed/pending phase is empty, the localized default text renders instead
(React's `confirmLabel ?? t(…)`).

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'button[mol-confirm-button]',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: [
    'variant',
    'color',
    'size',
    'onConfirm',
    'disarmSeconds',
    'disabled',
    'testId',
    'className',
    'style',
  ],
  host: {
    '[attr.type]': '"button"',
    '(click)': 'handleClick()',
    '(document:keydown.escape)': 'handleEscape()',
    '[disabled]': 'disabled || phase() === "pending"',
    '[attr.aria-live]': '"polite"',
    '[attr.data-state]': 'phase()',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
  },
  template: `@if (phase() === 'armed' && !hasConfirmLabel) {
      Confirm?
    }
    @if (phase() === 'pending' && !hasPendingLabel) {
      Working…
    }
    <span #labelSlot><ng-content select="[slot=label]"></ng-content></span
    ><span #confirmSlot><ng-content select="[slot=confirmLabel]"></ng-content></span
    ><span #pendingSlot><ng-content select="[slot=pendingLabel]"></ng-content></span>`,
})
export class ConfirmButton implements AfterViewInit, DoCheck, OnDestroy {
  /** Button variant (passed through to the button classes). */
  variant?: ButtonVariant
  /** Button color; the armed state always shows the error color. */
  color?: ColorVariant
  /** Button size. */
  size?: Size
  /**
   * The confirm callback (React's `onConfirm` prop). Fired ONLY on the
   * confirming (second) click; while the returned promise is pending the
   * button stays in its confirming state and ignores further clicks.
   */
  onConfirm?: () => void | Promise<void>
  /** Seconds before the armed state auto-disarms (`0` disables auto-disarm). */
  disarmSeconds = 4
  /** Disables the control entirely (idle and confirming states). */
  disabled = false
  /** `data-testid` for the button. */
  testId?: string
  /** Extra classes joined onto the button classes. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>

  /** The label slot capture span (kept out of the DOM). */
  @ViewChild('labelSlot') labelSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLSpanElement>
  /** The armed-state label capture span. */
  @ViewChild('confirmSlot') confirmSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLSpanElement>
  /** The pending-state label capture span. */
  @ViewChild('pendingSlot') pendingSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLSpanElement>

  /** Whether the armed label slot carries content (read at view init). */
  hasConfirmLabel = false
  /** Whether the pending label slot carries content (read at view init). */
  hasPendingLabel = false

  /** The current phase: idle → armed → pending → idle. */
  readonly phase = signal<'idle' | 'armed' | 'pending'>('idle')

  private readonly el = inject(ElementRef)
  private readonly renderer = inject(Renderer2)
  private readonly sync = new SlotWrapper(this.el, this.renderer)
  private disarmTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null
  private anchor: Comment | null = null

  /**
   * Arms the auto-disarm timer for the armed phase (React's `useEffect` on
   * `[phase, disarmSeconds]`).
   */
  constructor() {
    effect(() => {
      if (this.disarmTimer) {
        clearTimeout(this.disarmTimer)
        this.disarmTimer = null
      }
      if (this.phase() === 'armed' && this.disarmSeconds > 0) {
        this.disarmTimer = setTimeout(() => {
          this.disarmTimer = null
          this.phase.set('idle')
        }, this.disarmSeconds * 1000)
      }
    })
  }

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Computes the button class list (the armed state shows the error color).
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().button({
        color: this.phase() === 'armed' ? 'error' : (this.color ?? 'primary'),
        size: this.size,
        variant: this.variant,
      }),
      this.className,
    )
  }

  /**
   * Serializes the inline style.
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }

  /**
   * React's click handler: idle arms, armed commits (awaiting the callback),
   * pending ignores.
   */
  private async handleClick(): Promise<void> {
    const current = this.phase()
    if (current === 'idle') {
      this.phase.set('armed')
      return
    }
    if (current === 'pending') return
    this.phase.set('pending')
    try {
      await this.onConfirm?.()
    } catch (_error) {
      // A rejected confirm means the action failed: the caller owns that
      // error and shows its own message, and the button returns to idle so
      // the user can retry. Ignoring it here only stops it becoming an
      // unhandled rejection.
    } finally {
      this.phase.set('idle')
    }
  }

  /**
   * Disarms an armed button (React's window Escape listener, bound while
   * armed; disarming an idle button is a no-op).
   */
  private handleEscape(): void {
    if (this.phase() === 'armed') this.disarm()
  }

  /**
   * React's `disarm`: cancels the timer and returns to idle.
   */
  private disarm(): void {
    if (this.disarmTimer) {
      clearTimeout(this.disarmTimer)
      this.disarmTimer = null
    }
    this.phase.set('idle')
  }

  /**
   * Reads slot presence at view init (view queries resolve only then) and
   * takes the capture spans out of the DOM.
   */
  ngAfterViewInit(): void {
    this.hasConfirmLabel = hasNodes(this.confirmSlot?.nativeElement)
    this.hasPendingLabel = hasNodes(this.pendingSlot?.nativeElement)
    for (const span of [this.labelSlot, this.confirmSlot, this.pendingSlot])
      this.sync.sync(span?.nativeElement, false)
    this.syncLabels()
  }

  /**
   * Re-reconciles the visible label on every pass (phase changes and later
   * `props` steps both go through a refresh).
   */
  ngDoCheck(): void {
    this.syncLabels()
  }

  /** The nodes currently shown on the button, and the slot they came from. */
  private visibleNodes: Node[] = []
  private visibleFrom: HTMLSpanElement | null = null

  /**
   * Shows exactly the visible phase's projected children on the button —
   * React renders exactly one label and no wrapper element.
   */
  private syncLabels(): void {
    const phase = this.phase()
    const target =
      phase === 'idle'
        ? (this.labelSlot?.nativeElement ?? null)
        : phase === 'armed' && this.hasConfirmLabel
          ? (this.confirmSlot?.nativeElement ?? null)
          : phase === 'pending' && this.hasPendingLabel
            ? (this.pendingSlot?.nativeElement ?? null)
            : null
    this.showSlot(target)
  }

  /**
   * Shows one slot's projected children on the button, parking whatever
   * another slot was showing back inside its capture span.
   *
   * @param span - The capture span to show (null parks everything).
   */
  private showSlot(span: HTMLSpanElement | null): void {
    const host = this.el.nativeElement as HTMLElement
    if (span === this.visibleFrom) return
    for (const node of this.visibleNodes) {
      if (node.parentElement === host) this.visibleFrom?.appendChild(node)
    }
    this.visibleNodes = []
    this.visibleFrom = null
    if (!span) return
    const nodes = Array.from(span.childNodes)
    for (const node of nodes) host.insertBefore(node, this.labelAnchor())
    this.visibleNodes = nodes
    this.visibleFrom = span
  }

  /**
   * The insertion point for the visible projected label (a comment the DOM
   * normalizer strips), kept before the fallback texts.
   * @returns The anchor comment.
   */
  private labelAnchor(): Comment {
    if (this.anchor) return this.anchor
    const anchor = this.renderer.createComment('label') as Comment
    const host = this.el.nativeElement as HTMLElement
    host.insertBefore(anchor, host.firstChild)
    this.anchor = anchor
    return anchor
  }

  /** Clears the auto-disarm timer (React's unmount cleanup). */
  ngOnDestroy(): void {
    if (this.disarmTimer) clearTimeout(this.disarmTimer)
  }
}
```

#### `ConfirmDialog`

ConfirmDialog component.

Composed from `Modal` and `Button` exactly as React composes them; the
dialog's own title/description/children are projected content (`[slot=title]`,
`[slot=description]`, default). The host is unwrapped (`data-parity-unwrap`,
React's `Modal` root is a fragment/portal).

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'mol-confirm-dialog',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [Button, Modal],
  inputs: ['open', 'title', 'description', 'confirmLabel', 'cancelLabel', 'destructive', 'loading'],
  outputs: ['close', 'confirm'],
  host: {
    'data-parity-unwrap': '',
    style: 'display: contents',
  },
  template: `<mol-modal [open]="open" (close)="close.emit($event)"
    ><div [class]="cm().stack(3)">
      <h2 [class]="cm().cn(cm().textSize('lg'), cm().fontWeight('bold'))">
        <ng-content select="[slot=title]"></ng-content>
      </h2>
      <p [class]="cm().textSize('sm')"><ng-content select="[slot=description]"></ng-content></p>
      <ng-content></ng-content>
      <div [class]="cm().flex({ justify: 'end', gap: 'sm' })">
        <button mol-button [variant]="'ghost'" [disabled]="!!loading" (click)="close.emit($event)">
          {{ cancelText() }}</button
        ><button
          mol-button
          [variant]="'solid'"
          [color]="destructive === false ? 'primary' : 'error'"
          [disabled]="!!loading"
          (click)="confirm.emit()"
        >
          {{ confirmText() }}
        </button>
      </div>
    </div></mol-modal
  >`,
})
export class ConfirmDialog {
  /** Whether the dialog is open. */
  open = false
  /** Title (node-valued prop). */
  title?: unknown
  /** Body / warning text (node-valued prop). */
  description?: unknown
  /** Confirm-button label. */
  confirmLabel?: string
  /** Cancel-button label. */
  cancelLabel?: string
  /** Whether the confirm action is destructive (default true). */
  destructive = true
  /** Disables both buttons (the caller owns the pending state). */
  loading?: boolean

  /** Fires when the dialog should close (escape, backdrop, cancel). */
  @Output() readonly close = new EventEmitter<unknown>()
  /** Fires when the user confirms. */
  @Output() readonly confirm = new EventEmitter<void>()

  /**
   * Reactive translation, read in the injection context. Its `t` reads the
   * locale, so the view re-renders the text on a locale switch (the static `t`
   * does not).
   */
  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * The cancel button's label.
   * @returns The label text.
   */
  cancelText(): string {
    return this.cancelLabel ?? this.i18n.t('confirm.cancel', {}, { defaultValue: 'Cancel' })
  }

  /**
   * The confirm button's label (React shows an ellipsis while loading).
   * @returns The label text.
   */
  confirmText(): string {
    return this.loading
      ? '…'
      : (this.confirmLabel ?? this.i18n.t('confirm.confirm', {}, { defaultValue: 'Confirm' }))
  }
}
```

#### `Dropdown`

Dropdown component.

Implements the WAI-ARIA APG menu-button pattern exactly as React does: a
single-element `trigger` is adopted as the real control (no wrapper) and
cloned with `aria-haspopup`, `aria-expanded`, `aria-controls`, `tabindex`
and the open/keyboard handlers; any other trigger (text, several nodes) is
wrapped in a `role="button"` fallback container. The menu is rendered into
`document.body` (React's portal) and carries the position, width and
roving-tabindex behavior.

The React root is a FRAGMENT (trigger + portal), so the host element is
unwrapped (`data-parity-unwrap`) and `display: contents` keeps it
fragment-like.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'mol-dropdown',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [MolTemplateOutlet],
  inputs: [
    'trigger',
    'items',
    'placement',
    'open',
    'align',
    'width',
    'className',
    'style',
    'testId',
  ],
  outputs: ['select', 'openChange'],
  host: {
    'data-parity-unwrap': '',
    style: 'display: contents',
  },
  template: `<span #triggerCapture><ng-content select="[slot=trigger]"></ng-content></span
    ><ng-container #menuAnchor></ng-container
    ><ng-template #menuTpl
      ><div
        [attr.id]="menuId"
        role="menu"
        data-state="open"
        [class]="menuClasses()"
        [style]="menuStyleText()"
        [attr.data-testid]="menuTestId()"
        (keydown)="handleMenuKeyDown($event)"
      >
        @for (item of items; track keyForItem(item, $index)) {
          @if (item.separator) {
            <div [class]="cm().dropdownSeparator" role="separator"></div>
          } @else {
            <div
              role="menuitem"
              [attr.tabindex]="itemTabIndex($index)"
              [class]="itemClasses(item)"
              (click)="handleItemClick(item)"
              (keydown)="handleItemKeyDown($event, item)"
              [attr.aria-disabled]="item.disabled ? 'true' : null"
              [attr.data-disabled]="item.disabled ? 'true' : null"
            >
              @if (item.icon) {
                <span [class]="cm().dropdownItemIcon">
                  @if (asTemplate(item.icon); as iconTpl) {
                    <ng-container [molTemplateOutlet]="iconTpl"></ng-container>
                  } @else {
                    {{ iconText(item.icon) }}
                  }
                </span>
              }
              <span [class]="cm().dropdownItemLabel">{{ item.label }}</span>
              @if (item.shortcut) {
                <span [class]="cm().dropdownItemShortcut">{{ item.shortcut }}</span>
              }
            </div>
          }
        }
      </div></ng-template
    >`,
})
export class Dropdown implements AfterViewInit, AfterViewChecked, DoCheck, OnDestroy {
  trigger?: unknown
  items: DropdownItem<string>[] = []
  placement: DropdownPlacement = 'bottom-start'
  open?: boolean
  align: DropdownAlign = 'start'
  width?: 'trigger' | 'auto' | number | string
  className?: string
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  testId?: string

  @Output() readonly select = new EventEmitter<string>()
  @Output() readonly openChange = new EventEmitter<boolean>()

  @ViewChild('triggerCapture') triggerCapture?: ElementRef<HTMLSpanElement>
  @ViewChild('menuAnchor', { read: ViewContainerRef }) menuAnchor?: ViewContainerRef
  @ViewChild('menuTpl') menuTpl?: TemplateRef<unknown>

  readonly menuId = nextInstanceId()
  private readonly internalOpen = signal(false)
  private readonly activeIndex = signal(-1)
  private readonly position = signal<Position>({ top: 0, left: 0 })

  readonly router = useRouter()

  private readonly el = inject(ElementRef)
  private readonly renderer = inject(Renderer2)
  private readonly cdr = inject(ChangeDetectorRef)
  private readonly host: HTMLElement = this.el.nativeElement
  private triggerEl: HTMLElement | null = null
  private menuView?: EmbeddedViewRef<unknown>
  private menuEl: HTMLDivElement | null = null
  private lastOpen = false
  private lastPlacement: DropdownPlacement = 'bottom-start'
  private lastAlign: DropdownAlign = 'start'
  private menuWidth: number | string | undefined
  private removedListeners: (() => void)[] = []
  private lastRoute = ''
  private pendingOpenFocus: 'first' | 'last' = 'first'
  private focusedItemEl: HTMLElement | null = null
  private checkedItems: DropdownItem<string>[] | null = null
  private itemHadFocus = false
  private readonly routeEffect = effect(() => {
    const location = this.router.location()
    const key = `${location.pathname}${location.search}`
    if (key === this.lastRoute) return
    this.lastRoute = key
    this.setOpen(false)
  })

  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  asTemplate(value: unknown): TemplateRef<unknown> | null {
    return value instanceof TemplateRef ? value : null
  }

  keyForItem(item: DropdownItem<string>, index: number): string {
    return item.separator ? `separator-${index}` : item.value
  }

  iconText(value: unknown): string {
    return typeof value === 'string' ? value : ''
  }

  isOpen(): boolean {
    return this.open !== undefined ? this.open : this.internalOpen()
  }

  setOpen(open: boolean): void {
    if (this.open === undefined) this.internalOpen.set(open)
    this.openChange.emit(open)
    this.cdr.markForCheck()
  }

  menuClasses(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().dropdownContent, this.className)
  }

  menuTestId(): string | null {
    return this.testId ? `${this.testId}-menu` : null
  }

  menuStyleText(): string | null {
    return styleText({
      ...this.style,
      position: 'absolute',
      top: this.position().top,
      left: this.position().left,
      width: this.menuWidth,
      zIndex: 50,
    })
  }

  itemClasses(item: DropdownItem<string>): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().dropdownItem, item.disabled && this.cm().dropdownItemDisabled)
  }

  itemTabIndex(index: number): number {
    const item = this.items[index]
    if (item?.disabled) return -1
    return index === this.activeIndex() ? 0 : -1
  }

  handleItemClick(item: DropdownItem<string>): void {
    if (item.disabled) return
    this.select.emit(item.value)
    this.closeAndRefocusTrigger()
  }

  handleItemKeyDown(event: KeyboardEvent, item: DropdownItem<string>): void {
    if (event.key !== 'Enter' && event.key !== ' ') return
    event.preventDefault()
    this.handleItemClick(item)
  }

  handleMenuKeyDown(event: KeyboardEvent): void {
    const focusable = this.focusableIndexes()
    if (focusable.length === 0) return
    const currentPos = focusable.indexOf(this.activeIndex())
    switch (event.key) {
      case 'ArrowDown':
        event.preventDefault()
        this.activeIndex.set(focusable[(currentPos + 1 + focusable.length) % focusable.length])
        this.focusActive()
        break
      case 'ArrowUp':
        event.preventDefault()
        this.activeIndex.set(focusable[(currentPos - 1 + focusable.length) % focusable.length])
        this.focusActive()
        break
      case 'Home':
        event.preventDefault()
        this.activeIndex.set(focusable[0])
        this.focusActive()
        break
      case 'End':
        event.preventDefault()
        this.activeIndex.set(focusable[focusable.length - 1])
        this.focusActive()
        break
      case 'Tab':
        this.handleClose()
        break
      default:
        break
    }
  }

  handleTriggerClick(): void {
    if (!this.isOpen()) this.pendingOpenFocus = 'first'
    this.setOpen(!this.isOpen())
  }

  handleTriggerKeyDown(event: KeyboardEvent): void {
    switch (event.key) {
      case 'Enter':
      case ' ':
        event.preventDefault()
        this.pendingOpenFocus = 'first'
        this.setOpen(true)
        break
      case 'ArrowDown':
        event.preventDefault()
        this.pendingOpenFocus = 'first'
        this.setOpen(true)
        break
      case 'ArrowUp':
        event.preventDefault()
        this.pendingOpenFocus = 'last'
        this.setOpen(true)
        break
      default:
        break
    }
  }

  handleClose(): void {
    this.setOpen(false)
  }

  closeAndRefocusTrigger(): void {
    this.handleClose()
    this.triggerEl?.focus()
  }

  ngAfterViewInit(): void {
    this.adoptTrigger()
    this.syncMenu()
  }

  ngDoCheck(): void {
    this.itemHadFocus = this.focusedItemEl !== null && document.activeElement === this.focusedItemEl
    this.syncMenu()
  }

  ngOnDestroy(): void {
    this.removeListeners()
    this.destroyMenu()
  }
// … truncated — 16 more members: adoptTrigger, listen, focusableIndexes, itemElements, elementForIndex, focusActive, ngAfterViewChecked, syncMenu, openMenu, destroyMenu, updateMenuWidth, schedulePosition, updatePosition, addListeners, removeListeners, syncTrigger; the full declaration is in node_modules/@molecule/app-ui-angular/dist/components/dropdown.component.d.ts
```

```typescript
}
```
