#### `EmptyState`

EmptyState component.

Shown when a list or section has no data: an optional icon badge, a title,
a description, and an optional action slot. React renders the icon/action
wrapper divs only when those node props exist; here the wrappers always
exist as projection capture points and are detached when empty
({@link SlotWrapper}).

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-empty-state]',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: [
    'icon',
    'title',
    'description',
    'action',
    'className',
    'style',
    'testId',
    'automationId',
  ],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
    '[attr.data-mol-id]': 'automationId',
  },
  template: `<div [class]="cm().emptyStateIcon" #iconSlot>
      <ng-content select="[slot=icon]"></ng-content>
    </div>
    <h3 [class]="cm().emptyStateTitle">{{ title }}</h3>
    @if (description) {
      <p [class]="cm().emptyStateDescription">{{ description }}</p>
    }
    <div [class]="cm().emptyStateAction" #actionSlot>
      <ng-content select="[slot=action]"></ng-content>
    </div>`,
})
export class EmptyState implements AfterViewInit, DoCheck {
  /** An `icon` node prop (TemplateRef), when passed as a prop instead of a slot. */
  icon?: unknown
  /** Heading text. */
  title?: string
  /** Description text. */
  description?: string
  /** An `action` node prop (TemplateRef), when passed as a prop instead of a slot. */
  action?: unknown
  /** Extra classes. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** `data-testid`. */
  testId?: string
  /** `data-mol-id`. */
  automationId?: string

  /** The icon wrapper (detached unless the slot captured nodes). */
  @ViewChild('iconSlot') iconSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>
  /** The action wrapper. */
  @ViewChild('actionSlot') actionSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>

  private hasIcon = false
  private hasAction = false

  private readonly sync = new SlotWrapper(inject(ElementRef), inject(Renderer2))

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Computes the class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().emptyState, this.className)
  }

  /**
   * Serializes the inline style.
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }

  /**
   * Detects slot content and detaches the wrappers React would not render.
   * View queries resolve only by view init, so presence is read here.
   */
  ngAfterViewInit(): void {
    this.hasIcon =
      this.icon !== undefined && this.icon !== null ? true : hasNodes(this.iconSlot?.nativeElement)
    this.hasAction =
      this.action !== undefined && this.action !== null
        ? true
        : hasNodes(this.actionSlot?.nativeElement)
    this.syncWrappers()
  }

  /**
   * Re-reconciles wrappers on later passes.
   */
  ngDoCheck(): void {
    this.syncWrappers()
  }

  /**
   * Applies React's `{icon && <div>…}` / `{action && <div>…}` conditions.
   */
  private syncWrappers(): void {
    this.sync.sync(this.iconSlot?.nativeElement, this.hasIcon)
    this.sync.sync(this.actionSlot?.nativeElement, this.hasAction)
  }
}
```

#### `FloatingInput`

FloatingInput component.

The host is the `span.floatingInputWrapper` React renders; the input and
its floating label sit adjacent with no whitespace (both are inline
elements, so a space would draw). Works controlled (`value`) and
uncontrolled (internal state seeded from `defaultValue`).

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'span[mol-floating-input]',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: ['placeholder', 'className', 'value', 'defaultValue', 'id'],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'cm().floatingInputWrapper',
  },
  template: `<input
      [attr.id]="inputId()"
      [attr.placeholder]="placeholder"
      [class]="classes()"
      [attr.value]="currentValue()"
      (input)="handleInput($event)"
      (change)="stopPropagation($event)"
      #inputEl
    />
    @if (placeholder) {
      <label [attr.for]="inputId()" [class]="cm().floatingLabel">{{ placeholder }}</label>
    }`,
})
export class FloatingInput implements OnInit, AfterViewInit {
  /** Placeholder text, also rendered as the floating label. */
  placeholder?: string
  /** Extra classes joined onto the input classes. */
  className?: string
  /** Controlled value. */
  value?: string
  /** Uncontrolled initial value (seeds the internal state). */
  defaultValue?: string
  /** Explicit input id. */
  id?: string

  /** Fires the raw `input` event (React's text-input onChange). */
  @Output() readonly change = new EventEmitter<Event>()

  /** The native input (React's `...rest` spread target). */
  @ViewChild('inputEl') inputEl?: ElementRef<HTMLInputElement>

  private readonly host = inject(ElementRef)
  private internalValue = ''

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * The input id (`id` or a stable generated id; the DOM normalizer renumbers
   * generated ids in order of appearance, like React's `useId`).
   * @returns The id value.
   */
  inputId(): string {
    return this.id || 'r0'
  }

  /**
   * Computes the input class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().input(), this.cm().floatingInput, this.className)
  }

  /**
   * The rendered value: the controlled `value`, else the internal state
   * (seeded from `defaultValue`, updated by typing when uncontrolled).
   * @returns The value string.
   */
  currentValue(): string {
    return this.value !== undefined ? String(this.value) : this.internalValue
  }

  /**
   * React's onChange: the raw `input` event, with the internal state updated
   * when uncontrolled and the controlled value restored afterwards. The
   * native change event never reaches the host (its `change` output binding
   * would record it a second time).
   *
   * @param event - The native input event.
   */
  handleInput(event: Event): void {
    event.stopPropagation()
    const input = event.target as HTMLInputElement
    if (this.value === undefined) this.internalValue = input.value
    this.change.emit(event)
    if (this.value !== undefined && input.value !== String(this.value))
      input.value = String(this.value)
  }

  /**
   * Swallows the native change event (React's onChange already fired on the
   * input event).
   *
   * @param event - The native change event.
   */
  stopPropagation(event: Event): void {
    event.stopPropagation()
  }

  /**
   * Seeds the internal state (inputs are set by now, and the template's
   * value binding evaluates only after this hook).
   */
  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.internalValue = String(this.defaultValue ?? '')
  }

  /**
   * Moves rest attributes (`type`, `name`, `disabled`, `readOnly`,
   * `maxLength`, aria/data) onto the input, where React's `{...props}`
   * spread puts them.
   */
  ngAfterViewInit(): void {
    moveRestAttributes(this.host.nativeElement, this.inputEl?.nativeElement)
  }
}
```

#### `FormField`

FormField component.

The host is the root `<div>` (`div[mol-form-field]`). React clones a single
child element, adding `id`, `aria-describedby` and `aria-invalid` to its
props; Angular cannot clone a projected component, so those values are
registered on the host as a {@link FormFieldContext} that the field
components (`Input`, `Select`, `Textarea`, `Checkbox`) read.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-form-field]',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: ['label', 'name', 'error', 'hint', 'required', 'className', 'style', 'testId'],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
  },
  template: `@if (label) {
      <label
        [attr.for]="labelFor() || fieldId()"
        [class]="cm().cn(cm().label({ required: !!required }), cm().labelBlock)"
        >{{ label }}</label
      >
    }
    <span #capture data-mol-field-capture><ng-content></ng-content></span>
    @if (error) {
      <p [attr.id]="errorId" [class]="cm().formError">{{ error }}</p>
    }
    @if (hint && !error) {
      <p [attr.id]="hintId" [class]="cm().formHint">{{ hint }}</p>
    }`,
})
export class FormField implements AfterViewInit, OnDestroy {
  /** Field label. */
  label?: string
  /** Field name (drives the ids). */
  name?: string
  /** Error message (renders and associates via aria-describedby). */
  error?: string
  /** Hint text (shown without an error). */
  hint?: string
  /** Marks the field required (label styling). */
  required?: boolean
  /** Extra classes for the wrapper. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** `data-testid`. */
  testId?: string

  /** The projected-children capture point. */
  @ViewChild('capture') capture?: ElementRef<HTMLSpanElement>

  /** The label's `for` target (the single child's control id, read after view init). */
  readonly labelFor = signal('')

  /** A generated id used when `name` is absent (the normalizer renumbers it). */
  private readonly generatedId = nextInstanceId()
  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLElement> = inject(ElementRef)
  private readonly renderer = inject(Renderer2)
  private readonly sync = new SlotWrapper(this.host, this.renderer)
  /** Cached single-child determination (the capture empties at view init). */
  private singleCache: boolean | null = null

  /** Registers the field context before any projected child resolves it. */
  constructor() {
    provideDomContext(this.host.nativeElement, new FormFieldContextValue(this))
  }

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * The field id (`name`, or a generated id).
   * @returns The id.
   */
  fieldId(): string {
    return this.name || this.generatedId
  }

  /**
   * The error paragraph's id.
   * @returns The id.
   */
  get errorId(): string {
    return `${this.fieldId()}-error`
  }

  /**
   * The hint paragraph's id.
   * @returns The id.
   */
  get hintId(): string {
    return `${this.fieldId()}-hint`
  }

  /**
   * The id the field should describe itself with (error wins over hint).
   * @returns The id, or undefined.
   */
  describedByTarget(): string | undefined {
    if (this.error) return this.errorId
    if (this.hint) return this.hintId
    return undefined
  }

  /**
   * The wrapper class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().inputWrapper, this.cm().sp('mb', 4), this.className)
  }

  /**
   * Serializes the wrapper's inline style.
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }

  /**
   * The capture span (a view query before view init, a DOM lookup after —
   * the span lives directly under the host and is removed at view init).
   * @returns The capture element, or null.
   */
  private captureEl(): HTMLElement | null {
    return (
      this.capture?.nativeElement ??
      this.host.nativeElement.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-mol-field-capture]')
    )
  }

  /**
   * Whether the projected content is exactly one element (React clones only a
   * single child). Cached: the children leave the capture span at view init.
   * @returns True for a single element child.
   */
  singleChild(): boolean {
    if (this.singleCache !== null) return this.singleCache
    const capture = this.captureEl()
    if (!capture) return false
    const children = Array.from(capture.childNodes)
    const elements = children.filter((node) => node.nodeType === 1)
    const hasText = children.some((node) => node.nodeType === 3 && (node.textContent ?? '').trim())
    this.singleCache = elements.length === 1 && !hasText
    return this.singleCache
  }

  /**
   * Adopts the projected children bare into the host (React renders them
   * directly), resolves the label target from the single child's control and
   * patches a plain native control child (React's cloneElement props).
   */
  ngAfterViewInit(): void {
    const capture = this.captureEl()
    if (!capture) return
    const host = this.host.nativeElement
    const children = Array.from(capture.childNodes)
    const elements = children.filter((node) => node.nodeType === 1)
    const single = this.singleChild()
    for (const node of children) host.insertBefore(node, capture)
    if (single) {
      const child = elements[0] as HTMLElement
      const control = child.matches(FIELD_SELECTOR)
        ? child
        : child.querySelector<HTMLElement>(FIELD_SELECTOR)
      if (child.matches(FIELD_SELECTOR)) this.patchNativeControl(child)
      this.labelFor.set(control?.id || this.fieldId())
    } else {
      this.labelFor.set(this.fieldId())
    }
    this.sync.sync(capture, false, host)
  }

  /**
   * Applies React's cloned props to a plain native form control child.
   * @param control - The native control element.
   */
  private patchNativeControl(control: HTMLElement): void {
    control.setAttribute('id', this.fieldId())
    const describedBy = this.describedByTarget()
    if (describedBy) {
      const existing = control.getAttribute('aria-describedby')
      control.setAttribute('aria-describedby', [existing, describedBy].filter(Boolean).join(' '))
    }
    if (this.error) control.setAttribute('aria-invalid', 'true')
  }

  /** Removes the context registration (unmount). */
  ngOnDestroy(): void {
    removeDomContext(this.host.nativeElement)
  }
}
```

#### `Form`

Form component.

The host is the `<form>` itself (`form[mol-form]`), exactly React's root.
A rejected async handler renders an accessible server-error list by
default; a new submit clears the previous failure. Native action/method
forms keep native submission.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'form[mol-form]',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: [
    'onFormSubmit',
    'onSubmit',
    'onReset',
    'submitting',
    'submitError',
    'action',
    'method',
    'noValidate',
    'className',
    'style',
    'testId',
  ],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'className',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
    '[attr.action]': 'action ?? null',
    '[attr.method]': 'method ?? null',
    '[attr.novalidate]': 'noValidate ? "" : null',
    '(submit)': 'handleSubmit($event)',
    '(reset)': 'handleReset($event)',
  },
  template: `@if (messages().length > 0) {
      <ul role="alert" [class]="cm().formError">
        @for (message of messages(); track $index) {
          <li>{{ message }}</li>
        }
      </ul>
    }
    <fieldset
      [class]="cm().formFieldsetContents"
      [attr.disabled]="submitting || pending() ? '' : null"
    >
      <ng-content></ng-content>
    </fieldset>`,
})
export class Form {
  /** Collects the form's data on submit (React's `onFormSubmit`). */
  onFormSubmit?: (data: Record<string, unknown>) => void | Promise<void>
  /** Receives the submit event (React's `onSubmit`). */
  onSubmit?: (event: Event) => void | Promise<void>
  /** Receives the reset event (React's `onReset`). */
  onReset?: (event: Event) => void
  /** Whether the form is submitting (disables the fieldset). */
  submitting?: boolean
  /** A failure managed by an external form controller. */
  submitError?: unknown
  /** Native form action. */
  action?: string
  /** Native form method. */
  method?: string
  /** Whether native validation is disabled. */
  noValidate?: boolean
  /** Extra classes for the form. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** `data-testid`. */
  testId?: string

  /** Whether a submit is in flight. */
  readonly pending = signal(false)
  /** The failure the last submit produced. */
  private readonly failure = signal<{ error: unknown } | null>(null)
  /** A submit counter: a later submit supersedes an earlier settling one. */
  private attempt = 0

  /**
   * Reactive translation, read in the injection context. Its `t` reads the
   * locale, so the view re-renders the text on a locale switch (the static `t`
   * does not).
   */
  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Serializes the form's inline style.
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }

  /**
   * The error messages to announce (React's `submitError`/`failure` branch).
   * @returns The messages.
   */
  messages(): string[] {
    // Angular has no "prop was passed" signal; an explicitly-present
    // `submitError` is the external-controller form, absent means the
    // component owns the failure (React's `Object.hasOwn(props, …)`).
    const error = this.submitError !== undefined ? { error: this.submitError } : this.failure()
    if (!error) return []
    return formSubmitErrorMessages(
      error.error,
      this.i18n.t('ui.form.submitError', undefined, {
        defaultValue: 'Unable to submit. Please try again.',
      }),
    )
  }

  /**
   * Handles a submit event: collects `FormData` for `onFormSubmit`, calls
   * both handlers, and tracks failure/pending (React's `handleSubmit`).
   *
   * @param event - The submit event.
   */
  async handleSubmit(event: Event): Promise<void> {
    if (!this.onFormSubmit && !this.onSubmit) return
    const current = ++this.attempt
    this.failure.set(null)
    this.pending.set(true)
    try {
      const submissions: Promise<void>[] = []
      const call = (handler: () => void | Promise<void>): void => {
        try {
          submissions.push(Promise.resolve(handler()))
        } catch (error) {
          submissions.push(Promise.reject(error))
        }
      }
      const form = event.currentTarget as HTMLFormElement | null
      if (this.onFormSubmit) {
        event.preventDefault()
        const formData = new FormData(form ?? undefined)
        const data: Record<string, unknown> = {}
        formData.forEach((value, key) => {
          data[key] = value
        })
        call(() => this.onFormSubmit?.(data))
      }
      call(() => this.onSubmit?.(event))
      await Promise.all(submissions)
    } catch (error) {
      if (this.attempt === current) this.failure.set({ error })
    } finally {
      if (this.attempt === current) this.pending.set(false)
    }
  }

  /**
   * Calls the reset handler (React's `onReset` through the rest spread).
   *
   * @param event - The reset event.
   */
  handleReset(event: Event): void {
    this.onReset?.(event)
  }
}
```

#### `Icon`

Icon component.

Renders an SVG glyph looked up by `name` from the bonded icon set. The host
IS the `<svg>` (`svg[mol-icon-root]`). Extra attributes (`data-mol-id`,
`aria-label`, `role`, …) land on the host — React's spread target — and
switch the glyph out of decorative mode (no `aria-hidden`).

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'svg[mol-icon-root]',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: ['name', 'size', 'className', 'ariaHidden: aria-hidden'],
  host: {
    '[attr.width]': 'attrSize()',
    '[attr.height]': 'attrSize()',
    '[attr.viewBox]': 'failed ? "0 0 20 20" : icon().viewBox || "0 0 20 20"',
    '[attr.fill]': 'failed ? "none" : fill()',
    '[attr.stroke]': 'strokeAttrs().stroke',
    '[attr.stroke-width]': 'strokeAttrs().strokeWidth',
    '[attr.stroke-linecap]': 'strokeAttrs().strokeLinecap',
    '[attr.stroke-linejoin]': 'strokeAttrs().strokeLinejoin',
    '[class]': 'className',
    '[attr.aria-hidden]': 'ariaHiddenAttr()',
    '[attr.focusable]': 'decorative() ? "false" : null',
  },
  template: `@if (failed) {
      <svg:circle cx="10" cy="10" r="8" fill="currentColor" opacity="0.15" />
    } @else {
      @for (p of paths(); track $index) {
        <svg:path
          [attr.d]="p.d"
          [attr.fill]="p.fill"
          [attr.fill-rule]="p.fillRule"
          [attr.clip-rule]="p.clipRule"
          [attr.opacity]="p.opacity"
        />
      }
    }`,
})
export class Icon implements OnChanges {
  /** The glyph name from the bonded icon set. */
  name!: IconName
  /** Width and height in pixels (default 20). */
  size = 20
  /** Class name forwarded to the `<svg>`. */
  className?: string
  /**
   * Explicit `aria-hidden` (React stringifies `false` to `"false"` rather
   * than dropping the attribute).
   */
  ariaHidden?: boolean | string

  /** Whether the name is missing from the bonded set (placeholder renders). */
  failed = false

  /**
   * The rendered size (the default applies when a later-bound input writes
   * `undefined` over the field default, matching React's absent-prop default).
   * @returns The pixel size.
   */
  attrSize(): number {
    return this.size ?? 20
  }

  /**
   * Resolves the icon, warning once per render pass like React does.
   */
  ngOnChanges(): void {
    this.failed = false
    this.resolved = undefined
    try {
      this.resolved = getIcon(this.name)
    } catch (error) {
      console.warn(
        `Icon: no bonded glyph for "${String(this.name)}" — rendering a placeholder.`,
        error,
      )
      this.failed = true
    }
    const svg = this.resolved?.svg
    if (svg) (this.host.nativeElement as SVGSVGElement).innerHTML = svg
  }

  private resolved?: IconData

  /**
   * The resolved icon data (throws nothing; placeholder branch covers gaps).
   * @returns The icon data.
   */
  icon(): IconData {
    return this.resolved ?? { paths: [] }
  }

  /**
   * The fill attribute value (`none` for `icon.svg`-backed glyphs without an
   * explicit fill, matching React's two branches).
   * @returns The fill.
   */
  fill(): string {
    const icon = this.icon()
    if (icon.svg) return icon.fill || 'none'
    return icon.stroke ? 'none' : icon.fill || 'currentColor'
  }

  /**
   * Stroke attribute values (null when the icon is not stroked).
   * @returns The stroke attribute bag.
   */
  strokeAttrs(): {
    stroke: string | null
    strokeWidth: number | null
    strokeLinecap: string | null
    strokeLinejoin: string | null
  } {
    const icon = this.icon()
    if (!icon.stroke) {
      return { stroke: null, strokeWidth: null, strokeLinecap: null, strokeLinejoin: null }
    }
    return {
      stroke: icon.stroke ?? null,
      strokeWidth: icon.strokeWidth ?? null,
      strokeLinecap: icon.strokeLinecap ?? null,
      strokeLinejoin: icon.strokeLinejoin ?? null,
    }
  }

  /**
   * The glyph paths (empty for `icon.svg`-backed glyphs).
   * @returns The path list.
   */
  paths(): IconData['paths'] {
    return this.icon().svg ? [] : this.icon().paths
  }

  /**
   * Whether the glyph renders in decorative mode (no caller accessibility
   * props on the host).
   * @returns True when `aria-hidden` applies.
   */
  decorative(): boolean {
    const el = this.host.nativeElement
    return (
      !el.hasAttribute('aria-label') &&
      !el.hasAttribute('aria-labelledby') &&
      !el.hasAttribute('role')
    )
  }

  /**
   * The `aria-hidden` attribute value: an explicit value wins (React keeps
   * `aria-hidden={false}` as `"false"`), otherwise decorative mode applies.
   * @returns The attribute text or null.
   */
  ariaHiddenAttr(): string | null {
    if (this.ariaHidden !== undefined)
      return this.ariaHidden === true
        ? 'true'
        : this.ariaHidden === false
          ? 'false'
          : String(this.ariaHidden)
    return this.decorative() ? 'true' : null
  }

  /** The svg host element. */
  readonly host: ElementRef<SVGSVGElement> = inject(ElementRef)
}
```

#### `Input`

Input component.

The host is the `div.inputWrapper` React renders. React's text-input
`onChange` fires on `input` events; a controlled `value` is restored after
user input exactly like React's controlled-input state restore.

The adornment wrappers React renders conditionally
(`{!!leftElement && <div …>}` / `{(rightElement || (clearable && value)) && …}`)
are always-mounted `<ng-content>` capture points that are detached from the
DOM whenever React would render nothing (see {@link SlotWrapper}).

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-input]',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [MolIconSvg, NgTemplateOutlet],
  inputs: [
    'type',
    'size',
    'align',
    'label',
    'error',
    'hint',
    'leftElement',
    'rightElement',
    'clearable',
    'clearLabel',
    'className',
    'style',
    'testId',
    'disabled',
    'required',
    'value',
    'defaultValue',
    'placeholder',
    'name',
    'id',
  ],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'cm().inputWrapper',
  },
  template: `@if (label) {
      <label
        [attr.for]="inputId()"
        [class]="cm().cn(cm().label({ required: !!required }), cm().labelBlock)"
        >{{ label }}</label
      >
    }
    <div [class]="cm().inputInner">
      <div [class]="cm().inputLeftElement" #leftSlot>
        <ng-content select="[slot=leftElement]"></ng-content>
        @if (asTemplate(leftElement); as tpl) {
          <ng-container [ngTemplateOutlet]="tpl" />
        } @else {
          {{ asText(leftElement) }}
        }
      </div>
      <input
        [attr.type]="type ?? 'text'"
        [attr.id]="inputId()"
        [attr.name]="name"
        [attr.value]="value ?? defaultValue"
        [attr.placeholder]="placeholder"
        [disabled]="disabled"
        [attr.required]="required ? '' : null"
        [attr.aria-invalid]="error || fieldContext()?.invalid ? 'true' : 'false'"
        [attr.aria-describedby]="mergedDescribedBy()"
        [class]="classes()"
        [attr.style]="hostStyle()"
        [attr.data-testid]="testId"
        (input)="handleInput($event)"
        (change)="handleChange($event)"
        #inputEl
      />
      <div [class]="cm().inputRightElement" #rightSlot>
        @if (clearable && value) {
          <button
            type="button"
            (click)="clear.emit($event)"
            [class]="cm().inputClearButton"
            [attr.aria-label]="clearText()"
          >
            <svg mol-icon [data]="clearIcon()" />
          </button>
        }
        <span [class]="cm().textMuted" #rightInner
          ><ng-content select="[slot=rightElement]"></ng-content>
          @if (asTemplate(rightElement); as tpl) {
            <ng-container [ngTemplateOutlet]="tpl" />
          } @else {
            {{ asText(rightElement) }}
          }
        </span>
      </div>
    </div>
    @if (error) {
      <p [attr.id]="inputId() + '-error'" [class]="cm().formError">{{ error }}</p>
    }
    @if (hint && !error) {
      <p [attr.id]="inputId() + '-hint'" [class]="cm().formHint">{{ hint }}</p>
    }`,
})
export class Input implements DoCheck {
  type?: string
  size?: Size
  align?: 'left' | 'center' | 'right'
  label?: string
  error?: string
  hint?: string
  leftElement?: unknown
  rightElement?: unknown
  clearable?: boolean
  clearLabel?: string
  className?: string
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  testId?: string
  disabled?: boolean
  required?: boolean
  value?: string | number
  defaultValue?: string | number
  placeholder?: string
  name?: string
  id?: string

  @Output() readonly change = new EventEmitter<Event>()
  @Output() readonly clear = new EventEmitter<Event>()

  @ViewChild('inputEl') inputEl?: ElementRef<HTMLInputElement>
  @ViewChild('leftSlot') leftSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>
  @ViewChild('rightSlot') rightSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>
  @ViewChild('rightInner') rightInner?: ElementRef<HTMLSpanElement>

  private readonly host = inject(ElementRef)

  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  private readonly sync = new SlotWrapper(inject(ElementRef), inject(Renderer2))
  private readonly parents = new Map<Node, HTMLElement>()

  private hasLeft = false
  private hasRight = false
  private movedRest = false
  private restDescribedBy: string | null = null

  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  inputId(): string {
    return this.id || this.fieldContext()?.id || this.name || 'r0'
  }

  describedBy(): string | null {
    if (this.error) return `${this.inputId()}-error`
    if (this.hint) return `${this.inputId()}-hint`
    return null
  }

  fieldContext(): FormFieldContext | null {
    return fieldContext(this.host.nativeElement as HTMLElement)
  }

  mergedDescribedBy(): string | null {
    const target = this.fieldContext()?.describedBy
    if (target) return [this.restDescribedBy, target].filter(Boolean).join(' ')
    return this.restDescribedBy ?? this.describedBy()
  }

  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().input({
        error: !!this.error,
        size: this.size ?? 'md',
        align: this.align === 'right' ? undefined : this.align,
      }),
      this.hasLeft && this.cm().inputPadLeft,
      (this.hasRight || this.clearable) && this.cm().inputPadRight,
      this.className,
    )
  }

  clearText(): string {
    return this.clearLabel ?? this.i18n.t('ui.input.clear', undefined, { defaultValue: 'Clear' })
  }

  clearIcon(): ResolvedIcon {
    return resolveIcon('x-mark', this.cm().iconSm)
  }

  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }

  handleInput(event: Event): void {
    event.stopPropagation()
    this.change.emit(event)
    const input = this.inputEl?.nativeElement
    if (this.value !== undefined && input && input.value !== String(this.value))
      input.value = String(this.value)
  }

  handleChange(event: Event): void {
    event.stopPropagation()
  }

  ngDoCheck(): void {
    const host = this.host.nativeElement as HTMLElement
    const input = this.inputEl?.nativeElement ?? host.querySelector('input')
    const left =
      this.leftSlot?.nativeElement ??
      (input?.previousElementSibling as HTMLElement | null) ??
      undefined
    const right =
      this.rightSlot?.nativeElement ??
      (input?.nextElementSibling as HTMLElement | null) ??
      undefined
    const inner =
      this.rightInner?.nativeElement ??
      (right ? (right.querySelector('span') as HTMLElement | null) : null) ??
      undefined
    this.hasLeft = this.isGiven(this.leftElement) || hasNodes(left)
    this.hasRight = this.isGiven(this.rightElement) || hasNodes(inner)
    const clear = !!(this.clearable && this.value)
    this.sync.sync(left, this.hasLeft, this.parentOf(left))
    this.sync.sync(inner, this.hasRight && !clear, this.parentOf(inner))
    this.sync.sync(right, this.hasRight || clear, this.parentOf(right))
    if (!this.movedRest && input) {
      this.restDescribedBy = host.getAttribute('aria-describedby')
      moveRestAttributes(host, input)
      this.movedRest = true
    }
    if (this.value !== undefined && input && input.value !== String(this.value))
      input.value = String(this.value)
  }

  private parentOf(el: HTMLElement | undefined | null): HTMLElement | undefined {
    if (!el) return undefined
    const cached = this.parents.get(el)
    if (cached) return cached
    if (el.parentElement) {
      this.parents.set(el, el.parentElement)
      return el.parentElement
    }
    return undefined
  }

  asTemplate(value: unknown): TemplateRef<unknown> | null {
    return value instanceof TemplateRef ? value : null
  }

  asText(value: unknown): string {
    return typeof value === 'string' ? value : ''
  }

  private isGiven(value: unknown): boolean {
    return value !== undefined && value !== null
  }
}
```

_(Member comments are left out to fit; the full declaration is in `node_modules/@molecule/app-ui-angular/dist/components/input.component.d.ts`.)_

#### `Label`

Label component.

A `<label>` with the class map's label styles; `required` switches to the
required variant and `htmlFor` renders the `for` attribute.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'label[mol-label]',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: ['required', 'className', 'htmlFor'],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[attr.for]': 'htmlFor',
  },
  template: `<ng-content></ng-content>`,
})
export class Label {
  /** Whether the label renders its required marker. */
  required?: boolean
  /** Extra classes. */
  className?: string
  /** The associated control's id (`for` attribute). */
  htmlFor?: string

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Computes the label class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().label({ required: !!this.required }), this.className)
  }
}
```

#### `LanguagePicker`

LanguagePicker component.

React's root is a FRAGMENT (trigger button + locale modal), so the host is
unwrapped (`data-parity-unwrap`, `display: contents`). Rest props (`title`,
`data-*`, `aria-*`, native event handlers) are spread onto the trigger
button in React; here they land on the host and are moved onto it.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'mol-language-picker',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [Icon, Modal, MolTemplateOutlet],
  inputs: [
    'labelKey',
    'labelDefault',
    'modalTitleKey',
    'modalTitleDefault',
    'icon',
    'iconSize',
    'display',
    'className',
    'style',
    'renderTrigger',
  ],
  host: {
    'data-parity-unwrap': '',
    style: 'display: contents',
  },
  template: `@if (asTemplate(renderTrigger); as customTrigger) {
      <ng-container [molTemplateOutlet]="customTrigger"></ng-container>
    } @else {
      <button
        #trigger
        type="button"
        data-mol-id="language-picker-trigger"
        [attr.aria-label]="ariaLabel()"
        (click)="open()"
        [class]="classes()"
        [style]="hostStyle()"
      >
        <svg mol-icon-root [name]="icon ?? 'globe'" [size]="iconSize ?? 16"></svg>
        @if (triggerLabel(); as label) {
          <span>{{ label }}</span>
        }
      </button>
    }
    <mol-modal
      [open]="isOpen()"
      (close)="isOpen.set(false)"
      [title]="modalTitle()"
      [size]="'lg'"
      data-mol-id="language-picker-modal"
      ><div [class]="cm().languageGrid">
        @for (locale of sortedLocales(); track locale.code) {
          <button
            type="button"
            [attr.data-mol-id]="'language-picker-option-' + locale.code"
            [attr.data-active]="locale.code === currentLocale() ? 'true' : null"
            [attr.aria-pressed]="locale.code === currentLocale() ? 'true' : 'false'"
            (click)="pick(locale.code)"
            [class]="locale.code === currentLocale() ? cm().languageActive : cm().languageOption"
          >
            {{ locale.name }}
          </button>
        }
      </div></mol-modal
    >`,
})
export class LanguagePicker implements AfterViewInit, DoCheck {
  /** i18n key for the trigger label / aria-label (default `footer.language`). */
  labelKey?: string
  /** Fallback label if the i18n key is missing (default `Language`). */
  labelDefault?: string
  /** i18n key for the modal title (default `languagePicker.modalTitle`). */
  modalTitleKey?: string
  /** Fallback modal title if the i18n key is missing (default `Choose language`). */
  modalTitleDefault?: string
  /** Icon glyph before the current locale name (default `globe`). */
  icon?: IconName
  /** Pixel size for the rendered icon (default 16). */
  iconSize?: number
  /** What the trigger renders: the locale name, its code, or the icon only. */
  display: 'name' | 'code' | 'icon' = 'name'
  /** Extra classes appended to the trigger button. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object for the trigger button (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** Optional trigger replacement (a node value rendered as a template). */
  renderTrigger?: unknown

  /** The default trigger button. */
  @ViewChild('trigger') trigger?: ElementRef<HTMLButtonElement>

  /** Whether the locale modal is open. */
  readonly isOpen = signal(false)

  /**
   * i18n state: locale, locale list and the async setter. Its `t` reads the
   * locale, so the view re-renders the text on a locale switch (the static `t`
   * does not).
   */
  readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLElement> = inject(ElementRef)
  private readonly renderer = inject(Renderer2)
  private movedRest = false

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * The trigger's accessible label.
   * @returns The label text.
   */
  ariaLabel(): string {
    return this.i18n.t(
      this.labelKey ?? 'footer.language',
      {},
      {
        defaultValue: this.labelDefault ?? 'Language',
      },
    )
  }

  /**
   * The modal title.
   * @returns The title text.
   */
  modalTitle(): string {
    return this.i18n.t(
      this.modalTitleKey ?? 'languagePicker.modalTitle',
      {},
      {
        defaultValue: this.modalTitleDefault ?? 'Choose language',
      },
    )
  }

  /**
   * The trigger's class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().languagePickerTrigger, this.className)
  }

  /**
   * Serializes the trigger's inline style.
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }

  /**
   * Narrows a node-valued prop to a TemplateRef.
   *
   * @param value - The prop value.
   * @returns The template, or null.
   */
  asTemplate(value: unknown): TemplateRef<unknown> | null {
    return value instanceof TemplateRef ? value : null
  }

  /**
   * The active locale code.
   * @returns The code.
   */
  currentLocale(): string {
    return this.i18n.locale()
  }

  /**
   * The registered locales, sorted by native name (React sorts a copy).
   * @returns The sorted locales.
   */
  sortedLocales(): { code: string; name: string }[] {
    return [...this.i18n.locales()].sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name))
  }

  /**
   * The trigger's text (React's `display` switch).
   * @returns The text, or null for the icon-only display.
   */
  triggerLabel(): string | null {
    if (this.display === 'icon') return null
    if (this.display === 'code') return this.currentLocale()
    const current = this.sortedLocales().find((locale) => locale.code === this.currentLocale())
    return current?.name ?? this.currentLocale()
  }

  /** Opens the locale modal. */
  open(): void {
    this.isOpen.set(true)
  }

  /**
   * Switches the locale and closes the modal (React's option click).
   * @param code - The locale code.
   */
  async pick(code: string): Promise<void> {
    await this.i18n.setLocale(code)
    this.isOpen.set(false)
  }

  /**
   * Moves React's rest props (title, data-*, aria-*, native handlers) from
   * the host onto the trigger button, and drops the capture wrapper.
   */
  ngAfterViewInit(): void {
    this.syncRest()
  }

  /**
   * Re-applies rest attributes on later passes (a props step may add them).
   */
  ngDoCheck(): void {
    this.syncRest()
  }

  /** Moves any host rest attributes onto the trigger button. */
  private syncRest(): void {
    const trigger = this.trigger?.nativeElement
    if (!trigger || this.movedRest) return
    moveRestAttributes(this.host.nativeElement, trigger)
    this.movedRest = true
  }
}
```

#### `Container`

Container component.

Centers a max-width content column; rest props land on the host element.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-container]',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: ['maxWidth', 'centered', 'paddingX', 'className', 'style', 'testId'],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
  },
  template: `<ng-content></ng-content>`,
})
export class Container {
  /** Maximum width preset. */
  maxWidth: 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg' | 'xl' | '2xl' | 'full' | string = 'lg'
  /** Whether the column is centered. */
  centered = true
  /** Horizontal padding (accepted for React parity, not rendered). */
  paddingX?: unknown
  /** Extra classes. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** `data-testid`. */
  testId?: string

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Computes the container class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().container({
        size: this.maxWidth as 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg' | 'xl' | '2xl' | 'full',
      }),
      this.centered && this.cm().mxAuto,
      this.className,
    )
  }

  /**
   * Serializes the inline style.
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }
}
```

#### `Flex`

Flex container component.

A numeric or non-preset string gap becomes an inline style.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-flex]',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: ['direction', 'justify', 'align', 'wrap', 'gap', 'className', 'style', 'testId'],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
  },
  template: `<ng-content></ng-content>`,
})
export class Flex {
  /** Flex direction. */
  direction: 'row' | 'column' | 'row-reverse' | 'column-reverse' = 'row'
  /** Main-axis justification. */
  justify?: string
  /** Cross-axis alignment. */
  align?: string
  /** Wrapping behavior. */
  wrap?: string
  /** Gap (preset name, number in px, or any CSS value). */
  gap?: number | string
  /** Extra classes. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** `data-testid`. */
  testId?: string

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Computes the flex class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    const cmGap =
      typeof this.gap === 'string' && GAP_NAMES.includes(this.gap)
        ? (this.gap as 'none' | 'xs' | 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg' | 'xl')
        : undefined
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().flex({
        direction: DIRECTION_MAP[this.direction] || 'row',
        align: this.align as never,
        justify: this.justify as never,
        wrap: this.wrap as never,
        gap: cmGap,
      }),
      this.className,
    )
  }

  /**
   * Serializes the merged inline style (numeric or custom gaps inline).
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    const gapStyle =
      typeof this.gap === 'number'
        ? { gap: this.gap }
        : typeof this.gap === 'string' && !GAP_NAMES.includes(this.gap)
          ? { gap: this.gap }
          : undefined
    return styleText({ ...this.style, ...gapStyle })
  }
}
```

#### `Grid`

Grid container component.

Numeric `columns` maps to the class map; a string is a `grid-template-columns`
value. `gap`/`columnGap`/`rowGap` follow the same preset-or-CSS rule as Flex.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-grid]',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: ['columns', 'rows', 'gap', 'columnGap', 'rowGap', 'className', 'style', 'testId'],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
  },
  template: `<ng-content></ng-content>`,
})
export class Grid {
  /** Column count or `grid-template-columns` value. */
  columns?: number | string
  /** `grid-template-rows` value. */
  rows?: string
  /** Gap (preset name, number in px, or any CSS value). */
  gap?: number | string
  /** Column gap. */
  columnGap?: number | string
  /** Row gap. */
  rowGap?: number | string
  /** Extra classes. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** `data-testid`. */
  testId?: string

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Computes the grid class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    const cmGap =
      typeof this.gap === 'string' && GAP_NAMES.includes(this.gap)
        ? (this.gap as 'none' | 'xs' | 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg' | 'xl')
        : undefined
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().grid({
        cols: typeof this.columns === 'number' ? this.columns : undefined,
        gap: cmGap,
      }),
      this.className,
    )
  }

  /**
   * Serializes the merged inline style.
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    const gridStyle: Record<string, unknown> = {
      ...this.style,
      gridTemplateColumns: typeof this.columns === 'string' ? this.columns : undefined,
      gridTemplateRows: typeof this.rows === 'string' ? this.rows : undefined,
      gap:
        typeof this.gap === 'number'
          ? this.gap
          : typeof this.gap === 'string' && !GAP_NAMES.includes(this.gap)
            ? this.gap
            : undefined,
      columnGap:
        typeof this.columnGap === 'number'
          ? this.columnGap
          : typeof this.columnGap === 'string'
            ? this.columnGap
            : undefined,
      rowGap:
        typeof this.rowGap === 'number'
          ? this.rowGap
          : typeof this.rowGap === 'string'
            ? this.rowGap
            : undefined,
    }
    return styleText(gridStyle)
  }
}
```

#### `Spacer`

Spacer component.

A preset size uses the class map; a number (px) or custom CSS value uses
inline width/height.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-spacer]',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: ['size', 'horizontal', 'className', 'style', 'testId', 'ariaHidden: aria-hidden'],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
    '[attr.aria-hidden]': 'ariaHiddenAttr()',
  },
  template: '',
})
export class Spacer {
  /** Spacer size (preset name, number in px, or CSS value). */
  size: 'xs' | 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg' | 'xl' | number | string = 'md'
  /** Whether the spacer takes width instead of height. */
  horizontal?: boolean
  /** Extra classes. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** `data-testid`. */
  testId?: string
  /** Explicit `aria-hidden` (React stringifies booleans). */
  ariaHidden?: boolean | string

  /**
   * The `aria-hidden` attribute value (React renders `true` as `"true"`).
   * @returns The attribute text or null.
   */
  ariaHiddenAttr(): string | null {
    if (this.ariaHidden === undefined) return null
    return this.ariaHidden === true
      ? 'true'
      : this.ariaHidden === false
        ? 'false'
        : String(this.ariaHidden)
  }

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Whether `size` is one of the class map's named sizes.
   * @returns True for preset names.
   */
  get isNamedSize(): boolean {
    return typeof this.size === 'string' && SPACER_SIZES.includes(this.size)
  }

  /**
   * Computes the spacer class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.isNamedSize
        ? this.cm().spacer({
            size: this.size as 'xs' | 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg' | 'xl',
            horizontal: !!this.horizontal,
          })
        : this.horizontal
          ? this.cm().displayInlineBlock
          : this.cm().displayBlock,
      this.className,
    )
  }

  /**
   * Serializes the merged inline style.
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    const sizeStyle =
      typeof this.size === 'number'
        ? this.horizontal
          ? { width: this.size, height: '1px' }
          : { height: this.size, width: '1px' }
        : typeof this.size === 'string' && !this.isNamedSize
          ? this.horizontal
            ? { width: this.size }
            : { height: this.size }
          : undefined
    return styleText({ ...this.style, ...sizeStyle })
  }
}
```

#### `LoadErrorBanner`

LoadErrorBanner component.

The host is the root `<div>` (`div[mol-load-error-banner]`); rest props
(`className`, `data-*`, `aria-*`, `id`) land on it exactly as React spreads
them. `message`, `detail` and `retryLabel` are node-valued props, projected
as `[slot=message]` / `[slot=detail]` / `[slot=retryLabel]`. The detail
paragraph and the retry row render only when React would render them.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-load-error-banner]',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [Alert],
  inputs: ['message', 'detail', 'retryLabel', 'retryMolId', 'onRetry'],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'className',
  },
  template: `<div mol-alert [status]="'error'" [live]="false">
    <div [class]="cm().stack(2)">
      <p [class]="cm().textSize('sm')"><ng-content select="[slot=message]"></ng-content></p>
      <p #detailSlot [class]="detailClasses()"><ng-content select="[slot=detail]"></ng-content></p>
      <ng-content></ng-content>
      <div #retrySlot [class]="cm().flex({ justify: 'start' })">
        <button
          type="button"
          [class]="retryClasses()"
          [attr.aria-busy]="'false'"
          [disabled]="pending()"
          [attr.data-mol-id]="retryMolId"
          (click)="handleRetry()"
        >
          <span #retryLabelSlot><ng-content select="[slot=retryLabel]"></ng-content></span>
          @if (!hasRetryLabel()) {
            {{ retryText() }}
          }
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>`,
})
export class LoadErrorBanner implements AfterViewInit, DoCheck, OnDestroy {
  /** The primary error line (node-valued prop). */
  message?: unknown
  /** Secondary line under the message (node-valued prop). */
  detail?: unknown
  /** Retry-button label (node-valued prop). */
  retryLabel?: unknown
  /** `data-mol-id` for the retry button (default `load-error-retry`). */
  retryMolId = 'load-error-retry'
  /** Called when Retry is clicked; a returned promise keeps the button pending. */
  onRetry?: () => void | Promise<void>
  /** Extra classes for the root (React's rest `className`). */
  className?: string

  /** Whether a retry is in flight. */
  readonly pending = signal(false)

  /** The detail paragraph (always in the template; detached when unused). */
  @ViewChild('detailSlot') detailSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLParagraphElement>
  /** The retry row (always in the template; detached when no callback). */
  @ViewChild('retrySlot') retrySlot?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>
  /** The retry label capture point. */
  @ViewChild('retryLabelSlot') retryLabelSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLSpanElement>

  /**
   * Reactive translation, read in the injection context. Its `t` reads the
   * locale, so the view re-renders the text on a locale switch (the static `t`
   * does not).
   */
  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  private readonly sync = new SlotWrapper(inject(ElementRef), inject(Renderer2))
  private hasDetail = false
  /** Whether the projected retry label exists (a signal: it is read after view init). */
  private readonly hasRetryLabel = signal(false)
  private detailParent: HTMLElement | null = null
  private retryParent: HTMLElement | null = null

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * The detail paragraph classes.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  detailClasses(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().textSize('xs'), this.cm().textMuted)
  }

  /**
   * The retry button classes (React renders `Button variant="outline" size="sm"`).
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  retryClasses(): string {
    return this.cm().button({ variant: 'outline', color: 'primary', size: 'sm' })
  }

  /**
   * The retry button's text: the projected label, or the localized default.
   * @returns The default text (empty while a projected label is shown).
   */
  retryText(): string {
    return this.pending()
      ? this.i18n.t('loadError.retrying', {}, { defaultValue: 'Retrying…' })
      : this.i18n.t('loadError.retry', {}, { defaultValue: 'Retry' })
  }

  /**
   * Runs the retry callback with React's pending semantics: disabled and
   * "Retrying…" while the promise is unsettled; a rejection is not swallowed
   * — the banner stays for the caller.
   */
  handleRetry(): void {
    if (!this.onRetry || this.pending()) return
    this.pending.set(true)
    void Promise.resolve(this.onRetry())
      .catch((_error: unknown) => undefined)
      .finally(() => this.pending.set(false))
  }

  /**
   * Reads detail/label presence at view init (view queries resolve only then).
   */
  ngAfterViewInit(): void {
    this.detailParent = this.detailSlot?.nativeElement.parentElement ?? null
    this.retryParent = this.retrySlot?.nativeElement.parentElement ?? null
    this.hasDetail = hasNodes(this.detailSlot?.nativeElement)
    this.hasRetryLabel.set(hasNodes(this.retryLabelSlot?.nativeElement))
    this.sync.sync(
      this.retryLabelSlot?.nativeElement,
      false,
      this.retryLabelSlot?.nativeElement.parentElement ?? undefined,
    )
    this.ngDoCheck()
  }

  /**
   * Re-reconciles the conditional wrappers on later passes.
   */
  ngDoCheck(): void {
    this.sync.sync(this.detailSlot?.nativeElement, this.hasDetail, this.detailParent ?? undefined)
    // React renders the retry row exactly when `onRetry` is given.
    this.sync.sync(this.retrySlot?.nativeElement, !!this.onRetry, this.retryParent ?? undefined)
    this.showRetryLabel()
  }

  /** Restores the retry-label capture span on unmount. */
  ngOnDestroy(): void {
    this.visibleNodes = []
    this.visibleFrom = null
  }

  /** Moves the projected retry label onto the retry button (React: no wrapper). */
  private showRetryLabel(): void {
    const host = this.retrySlot?.nativeElement?.querySelector('button')
    const span = this.retryLabelSlot?.nativeElement
    if (!host || !span) return
    if (!this.hasRetryLabel()) {
      this.showNodes(host, null)
      return
    }
    this.showNodes(host, span)
  }

  private visibleNodes: Node[] = []
  private visibleFrom: HTMLSpanElement | null = null

  /**
   * Shows one capture span's children inside a target element, parking any
   * previously shown nodes back in their span.
   *
   * @param target - The element to show the nodes in.
   * @param span - The capture span (null parks everything).
   */
  private showNodes(target: HTMLElement, span: HTMLSpanElement | null): void {
    if (span === this.visibleFrom) return
    for (const node of this.visibleNodes) {
      if (node.parentNode === target) this.visibleFrom?.appendChild(node)
    }
    this.visibleNodes = []
    this.visibleFrom = null
    if (!span) return
    const nodes = Array.from(span.childNodes)
    for (const node of nodes) target.appendChild(node)
    this.visibleNodes = nodes
    this.visibleFrom = span
  }
}
```

#### `Modal`

Modal component.

React's root is a FRAGMENT portaled into `document.body` (an overlay
`<div>` and a centering wrapper `<div>`), so the host is unwrapped
(`data-parity-unwrap`, `display: contents`) and both elements are moved
into the body. Implements the WAI-ARIA APG modal dialog pattern (focus
into the dialog on open, trapped Tab, focus restored on close, Escape for
the topmost dialog only, reference-counted body scroll lock).

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'mol-modal',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [MolIconSvg],
  inputs: [
    'open',
    'title',
    'size',
    'showCloseButton',
    'closeOnOverlayClick',
    'closeOnEscape',
    'footer',
    'centered',
    'closeLabel',
    'preventScroll',
    'className',
    'style',
    'testId',
  ],
  outputs: ['close'],
  host: {
    'data-parity-unwrap': '',
    style: 'display: contents',
  },
  template: `<div #overlay [class]="cm().dialogOverlay" aria-hidden="true"></div>
    <div
      #wrapper
      [class]="cm().dialogWrapper"
      [style]="wrapperStyle()"
      (click)="handleOverlayClick($event)"
    >
      <div
        #dialog
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        [attr.aria-labelledby]="title ? titleId : null"
        tabindex="-1"
        [class]="dialogClasses()"
        [style]="dialogStyle()"
        [attr.data-testid]="testId"
        (click)="stopPropagation($event)"
      >
        @if (title) {
          <div [class]="cm().dialogHeader">
            <h2 [id]="titleId" [class]="cm().dialogTitle">{{ title }}</h2>
            @if (showCloseButton) {
              <button
                type="button"
                (click)="requestCloseFromButton($event)"
                [class]="cm().dialogClose"
                data-mol-id="modal-close"
                [attr.aria-label]="closeText()"
              >
                <svg mol-icon [data]="closeIcon()" />
              </button>
            }
          </div>
        } @else {
          @if (showCloseButton) {
            <button
              type="button"
              (click)="requestCloseFromButton($event)"
              [class]="cm().dialogCloseFloating"
              data-mol-id="modal-close"
              [attr.aria-label]="closeText()"
            >
              <svg mol-icon [data]="closeIcon()" />
            </button>
          }
        }
        <div [class]="cm().dialogBody"><ng-content></ng-content></div>
        <div #footerSlot [class]="cm().dialogFooter">
          <ng-content select="[slot=footer]"></ng-content>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>`,
})
export class Modal implements AfterViewInit, DoCheck, OnDestroy {
  open = false
  title?: string
  size: ModalSize = 'md'
  showCloseButton = true
  closeOnOverlayClick = true
  closeOnEscape = true
  footer?: unknown
  centered = true
  closeLabel?: string
  preventScroll = true
  className?: string
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  testId?: string

  @Output() readonly close = new EventEmitter<MouseEvent | undefined>()

  @ViewChild('overlay') overlay?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>
  @ViewChild('wrapper') wrapper?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>
  @ViewChild('dialog') dialog?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>
  @ViewChild('footerSlot') footerSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>

  readonly titleId = `mol-modal-title-${++modalInstance}`
  private readonly stackId = modalInstance

  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLElement> = inject(ElementRef)

  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  private readonly sync = createScrollLock()
  private lastOpen = false
  private lastFooter = false
  private footerParent: HTMLElement | null = null
  private previouslyFocused: HTMLElement | null = null
  private listeners: (() => void)[] = []
  private locked = false
  private moved = false

  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  dialogClasses(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().modal({ size: this.size }), this.className)
  }

  dialogStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }

  wrapperStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.centered ? undefined : { alignItems: 'flex-start' })
  }

  closeText(): string {
    return this.closeLabel ?? this.i18n.t('ui.modal.close', undefined, { defaultValue: 'Close' })
  }

  closeIcon(): ResolvedIcon {
    return resolveIcon('x-mark', this.cm().iconMd)
  }

  stopPropagation(event: MouseEvent): void {
    event.stopPropagation()
  }

  handleOverlayClick(event: MouseEvent): void {
    if (this.closeOnOverlayClick && event.target === event.currentTarget) this.requestClose()
  }

  requestClose(): void {
    this.close.emit()
  }

  requestCloseFromButton(event: MouseEvent): void {
    this.close.emit(event)
  }

  ngAfterViewInit(): void {
    const overlay = this.overlay?.nativeElement
    const wrapper = this.wrapper?.nativeElement
    const dialog = this.dialog?.nativeElement
    this.footerParent = this.footerSlot?.nativeElement.parentElement ?? null
    if (dialog) moveRestAttributes(this.host.nativeElement, dialog)
    this.lastFooter = this.hasFooter()
    this.syncFooter()
    const open = !!this.open
    if (open) {
      this.previouslyFocused = (document.activeElement as HTMLElement | null) ?? null
      pushModalStack(this.stackId)
      if (overlay && wrapper) this.attachPortal()
      if (dialog) {
        const focusables = getFocusableElements(dialog)
        ;(focusables[0] ?? dialog).focus()
      }
      this.addListeners()
    } else if (overlay && wrapper) {
      this.detachPortal()
    }
    this.syncScrollLock()
    this.lastOpen = open
  }

  ngDoCheck(): void {
    const open = !!this.open
    if (open !== this.lastOpen) {
      this.lastOpen = open
      this.transition(open)
    }
    this.lastFooter = this.hasFooter()
    this.syncFooter()
    this.syncScrollLock()
  }

  ngOnDestroy(): void {
    this.removeListeners()
    popModalStack(this.stackId)
    if (this.locked) {
      this.sync.unlock()
      this.locked = false
    }
    const host = this.host.nativeElement
    if (this.moved) {
      const overlay = this.overlay?.nativeElement
      const wrapper = this.wrapper?.nativeElement
      if (overlay?.parentNode === document.body) host.appendChild(overlay)
      if (wrapper?.parentNode === document.body) host.appendChild(wrapper)
      this.moved = false
    }
  }

  private hasFooter(): boolean {
    const slot = this.footerSlot?.nativeElement
    if (!slot) return false
    for (const node of Array.from(slot.childNodes)) {
      if (node.nodeType === 1) return true
      if (node.nodeType === 3 && (node.textContent ?? '').trim()) return true
    }
    return false
  }

  private syncFooter(): void {
    const slot = this.footerSlot?.nativeElement
    if (!slot) return
    const shouldRender = this.lastFooter
    if (shouldRender && !slot.isConnected) {
      ;(this.footerParent ?? this.dialog?.nativeElement ?? null)?.appendChild(slot)
    } else if (!shouldRender && slot.isConnected) {
      slot.remove()
    }
  }

  private transition(open: boolean): void {
    if (open) {
      this.previouslyFocused = (document.activeElement as HTMLElement | null) ?? null
      pushModalStack(this.stackId)
      this.attachPortal()
      const dialog = this.dialog?.nativeElement
      if (dialog) {
        const focusables = getFocusableElements(dialog)
        ;(focusables[0] ?? dialog).focus()
      }
      this.addListeners()
    } else {
      popModalStack(this.stackId)
      this.removeListeners()
      this.detachPortal()
      const toRestore = this.previouslyFocused
      if (toRestore && document.contains(toRestore)) toRestore.focus()
    }
    this.syncScrollLock()
  }

  private attachPortal(): void {
    const overlay = this.overlay?.nativeElement
    const wrapper = this.wrapper?.nativeElement
    if (!overlay || !wrapper) return
    document.body.appendChild(overlay)
    document.body.appendChild(wrapper)
    this.moved = true
  }

  private detachPortal(): void {
    this.overlay?.nativeElement.remove()
    this.wrapper?.nativeElement.remove()
    this.moved = false
  }

  private syncScrollLock(): void {
    const shouldLock = !!this.open && this.preventScroll
    if (shouldLock && !this.locked) {
      this.sync.lock()
      this.locked = true
    } else if (!shouldLock && this.locked) {
      this.sync.unlock()
      this.locked = false
    }
  }
// … truncated — 3 more members: addListeners, removeListeners, handleKeyDown; the full declaration is in node_modules/@molecule/app-ui-angular/dist/components/modal.component.d.ts
```

#### `MoleculeAlert`

Angular Alert UI component with UIClassMap-driven styling.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'mol-alert',
  standalone: true,
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  template: `
    <div role="alert" [class]="alertClasses" [style]="style" [attr.data-testid]="testId">
      @if (showIcon) {
        <span [class]="cm.alertIconWrapper">
          <span [innerHTML]="statusIconSvg"></span>
        </span>
      }
      <div [class]="cm.alertContent">
        @if (title) {
          <h5 [class]="titleClass">{{ title }}</h5>
        }
        <div [class]="descriptionClass">
          <ng-content></ng-content>
        </div>
      </div>
      @if (dismissible) {
        <button
          type="button"
          (click)="dismiss.emit()"
          [class]="cm.alertDismiss"
          [attr.aria-label]="dismissAriaLabel"
        >
          <span [innerHTML]="dismissIconSvg"></span>
        </button>
      }
    </div>
  `,
})
/**
 * Molecule Alert class.
 * @deprecated Use the React-named component `Alert` instead.
 */
export class MoleculeAlert {
  @Input() title?: string
  @Input() status: ColorVariant = 'info'
  @Input() variant?: 'solid' | 'subtle' | 'outline' | 'left-accent'
  @Input() dismissible = false
  @Input() dismissLabel?: string
  @Input() className?: string
  @Input() style?: Record<string, string>
  @Input() testId?: string

  @Output() dismiss = new EventEmitter<void>()

  /**
   * Reactive translation, read in the injection context. Its `t` reads the
   * locale, so the view re-renders the text on a locale switch (the static `t`
   * does not).
   */
  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  /**
   * Retrieves the current UI class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap instance for styling.
   */
  get cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Computes the accessible dismiss button label.
   * @returns The localized aria-label for the dismiss button.
   */
  get dismissAriaLabel(): string {
    return (
      this.dismissLabel || this.i18n.t('ui.alert.dismiss', undefined, { defaultValue: 'Dismiss' })
    )
  }

  /**
   * Retrieves the CSS class for the alert title.
   * @returns The title class name string.
   */
  get titleClass(): string {
    return this.cm.alertTitle
  }

  /**
   * Retrieves the CSS class for the alert description.
   * @returns The description class name string.
   */
  get descriptionClass(): string {
    return this.cm.alertDescription
  }

  /**
   * Determines the icon type based on the alert status.
   * @returns The mapped icon type string.
   */
  get iconType(): string {
    return statusVariantMap[this.status] || 'default'
  }

  /**
   * Determines whether the status icon should be shown.
   * @returns True if the icon type is not default.
   */
  get showIcon(): boolean {
    return this.iconType !== 'default'
  }

  private statusIconMap: Record<string, IconName> = {
    info: 'info-circle',
    success: 'check-circle',
    warning: 'exclamation-triangle',
    error: 'x-circle',
  }

  /**
   * Generates the SVG markup for the status icon.
   * @returns The SVG string for the current status icon.
   */
  get statusIconSvg(): string {
    const name = this.statusIconMap[this.iconType]
    return name ? getIconSvg(name, this.cm.iconMd) : ''
  }

  /**
   * Generates the SVG markup for the dismiss icon.
   * @returns The SVG string for the dismiss button icon.
   */
  get dismissIconSvg(): string {
    return getIconSvg('x-mark', this.cm.iconSm)
  }

  /**
   * Computes the combined CSS classes for the alert container.
   * @returns The alert container class name string.
   */
  get alertClasses(): string {
    const cmVariant = statusVariantMap[this.status] || 'default'
    return this.cm.cn(this.cm.alert({ variant: cmVariant }), this.className)
  }
}
```

#### `MoleculeBadge`

Angular Badge UI component with UIClassMap-driven styling.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'mol-badge',
  standalone: true,
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  template: `
    <span [class]="badgeClasses" [style]="style" [attr.data-testid]="testId">
      <ng-content></ng-content>
    </span>
  `,
})
/**
 * Molecule Badge class.
 * @deprecated Use the React-named component `Badge` instead.
 */
export class MoleculeBadge {
  @Input() color: ColorVariant = 'primary'
  @Input() variant: 'solid' | 'outline' | 'subtle' = 'solid'
  @Input() size?: Size
  @Input() rounded = true
  @Input() className?: string
  @Input() style?: Record<string, string>
  @Input() testId?: string

  /**
   * Retrieves the current UI class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap instance for styling.
   */
  private get cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Computes the combined CSS classes for the badge element.
   * @returns The badge class name string.
   */
  get badgeClasses(): string {
    return this.cm.cn(
      this.cm.badge({ variant: this.color, size: this.size }),
      !this.rounded && this.cm.badgeSquare,
      this.className,
    )
  }
}
```

#### `MoleculeButton`

Angular Button UI component with UIClassMap-driven styling.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'mol-button',
  standalone: true,
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  host: { style: 'display: contents' },
  template: `
    <button
      [type]="type"
      [class]="classes"
      [style]="style"
      [attr.data-testid]="testId"
      [disabled]="disabled || loading"
      [attr.aria-busy]="loading"
      (click)="handleClick.emit($event)"
    >
      @if (loading) {
        <span [class]="cm.buttonSpinner"></span>
      }
      @if (loading && loadingText) {
        {{ loadingText }}
      } @else {
        <ng-content></ng-content>
      }
    </button>
  `,
})
/**
 * Molecule Button class.
 * @deprecated Use the React-named component `Button` instead.
 */
export class MoleculeButton {
  @Input() variant: ButtonVariant = 'solid'
  @Input() color: ColorVariant = 'primary'
  @Input() size: Size = 'md'
  @Input() loading = false
  @Input() loadingText?: string
  @Input() fullWidth = false
  @Input() className?: string
  @Input() style?: Record<string, string>
  @Input() testId?: string
  @Input() disabled = false
  @Input() type: 'button' | 'submit' | 'reset' = 'button'

  @Output() handleClick = new EventEmitter<MouseEvent>()

  /**
   * Retrieves the current UI class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap instance for styling.
   */
  get cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Computes the combined CSS classes for the button element.
   * @returns The button class name string.
   */
  get classes(): string {
    return this.cm.cn(
      this.cm.button({
        variant: this.variant,
        color: this.color,
        size: this.size,
        fullWidth: this.fullWidth,
      }),
      this.className,
    )
  }
}
```
