#### `MoleculeSwitch`

Angular Switch UI component with UIClassMap-driven styling.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'mol-switch',
  standalone: true,
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  template: `
    <label [class]="wrapperClasses">
      <button
        type="button"
        role="switch"
        [attr.aria-checked]="checked"
        [disabled]="disabled"
        [attr.data-state]="state"
        [class]="switchClasses"
        [style]="style"
        [attr.data-testid]="testId"
        (click)="onToggle()"
      >
        <span [attr.data-state]="state" [class]="thumbClasses"></span>
      </button>
      @if (label) {
        <span [class]="cm.controlText">{{ label }}</span>
      }
    </label>
  `,
})
/**
 * Molecule Switch class.
 * @deprecated Use the React-named component `Switch` instead.
 */
export class MoleculeSwitch {
  @Input() label?: string
  @Input() checked = false
  @Input() size: Size = 'md'
  @Input() color?: ColorVariant
  @Input() className?: string
  @Input() style?: Record<string, string>
  @Input() testId?: string
  @Input() disabled = false

  @Output() checkedChange = new EventEmitter<boolean>()

  /**
   * Retrieves the current UI class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap instance for styling.
   */
  get cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Computes the current checked/unchecked state string.
   * @returns The state string, either "checked" or "unchecked".
   */
  get state(): string {
    return this.checked ? 'checked' : 'unchecked'
  }

  /**
   * Computes the CSS classes for the switch wrapper label.
   * @returns The wrapper class name string.
   */
  get wrapperClasses(): string {
    return this.cm.cn(this.cm.controlLabel, this.disabled && this.cm.controlDisabled)
  }

  /**
   * Computes the combined CSS classes for the switch button.
   * @returns The switch class name string.
   */
  get switchClasses(): string {
    return this.cm.cn(this.cm.switchBase({ size: this.size }), this.className)
  }

  /**
   * Computes the CSS classes for the switch thumb indicator.
   * @returns The thumb class name string.
   */
  get thumbClasses(): string {
    return this.cm.switchThumb({ size: this.size })
  }

  /**
   * Handles the switch toggle action.
   */
  onToggle(): void {
    if (!this.disabled) {
      this.checkedChange.emit(!this.checked)
    }
  }
}
```

#### `MoleculeToast`

Angular Toast UI component with UIClassMap-driven styling.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'mol-toast',
  standalone: true,
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  template: `
    @if (isVisible) {
      <div
        role="alert"
        data-state="open"
        [class]="toastClasses"
        [style]="style"
        [attr.data-testid]="testId"
      >
        @if (showIcon) {
          <span [class]="cm.toastIconWrapper">
            <span [innerHTML]="statusIconSvg"></span>
          </span>
        }
        <div [class]="cm.toastContentWrapper">
          @if (title) {
            <div [class]="titleClass">{{ title }}</div>
          }
          @if (description) {
            <div [class]="descriptionClass">{{ description }}</div>
          } @else {
            <div [class]="descriptionClass">
              <ng-content></ng-content>
            </div>
          }
        </div>
        @if (dismissible) {
          <button
            type="button"
            (click)="onDismiss()"
            [class]="closeButtonClass"
            [attr.aria-label]="closeAriaLabel"
          >
            <span [innerHTML]="dismissIconSvg"></span>
          </button>
        }
      </div>
    }
  `,
})
/**
 * Molecule Toast class.
 * @deprecated Use the React-named component `Toast` instead.
 */
export class MoleculeToast implements OnInit, OnDestroy {
  @Input() title?: string
  @Input() description?: string
  @Input() status: ColorVariant = 'info'
  @Input() duration = 5000
  @Input() dismissible = true
  @Input() closeLabel?: string
  @Input() position: 'top' | 'top-right' | 'top-left' | 'bottom' | 'bottom-right' | 'bottom-left' =
    'bottom-right'
  @Input() className?: string
  @Input() style?: Record<string, string>
  @Input() testId?: string

  @Output() dismiss = new EventEmitter<void>()

  isVisible = true
  private timer?: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>

  /**
   * Reactive translation, read in the injection context. Its `t` reads the
   * locale, so the view re-renders the text on a locale switch (the static `t`
   * does not).
   */
  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  /**
   * Retrieves the current UI class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap instance for styling.
   */
  get cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Computes the accessible close button label.
   * @returns The localized aria-label for the close button.
   */
  get closeAriaLabel(): string {
    return this.closeLabel || this.i18n.t('ui.toast.close', undefined, { defaultValue: 'Close' })
  }

  /**
   * Retrieves the CSS class for the toast title.
   * @returns The title class name string.
   */
  get titleClass(): string {
    return this.cm.toastTitle
  }

  /**
   * Retrieves the CSS class for the toast description.
   * @returns The description class name string.
   */
  get descriptionClass(): string {
    return this.cm.toastDescription
  }

  /**
   * Retrieves the CSS class for the close button.
   * @returns The close button class name string.
   */
  get closeButtonClass(): string {
    return this.cm.toastClose
  }

  /**
   * Determines the icon type based on the toast status.
   * @returns The mapped icon type string.
   */
  get iconType(): string {
    return statusVariantMap[this.status] || 'default'
  }

  /**
   * Determines whether the status icon should be shown.
   * @returns True if the icon type is not default.
   */
  get showIcon(): boolean {
    return this.iconType !== 'default'
  }

  private statusIconMap: Record<string, IconName> = {
    info: 'info-circle',
    success: 'check-circle',
    warning: 'exclamation-triangle',
    error: 'x-circle',
  }

  /**
   * Generates the SVG markup for the status icon.
   * @returns The SVG string for the current status icon.
   */
  get statusIconSvg(): string {
    const name = this.statusIconMap[this.iconType]
    return name ? getIconSvg(name, this.cm.iconMd) : ''
  }

  /**
   * Generates the SVG markup for the dismiss icon.
   * @returns The SVG string for the dismiss button icon.
   */
  get dismissIconSvg(): string {
    return getIconSvg('x-mark', this.cm.iconSm)
  }

  /**
   * Computes the combined CSS classes for the toast container.
   * @returns The toast class name string.
   */
  get toastClasses(): string {
    const variant = statusVariantMap[this.status] || 'default'
    return this.cm.cn(this.cm.toast({ variant }), this.className)
  }

  /**
   * Starts the auto-dismiss timer on component creation.
   */
  ngOnInit(): void {
    if (this.duration > 0) {
      this.timer = setTimeout(() => {
        this.isVisible = false
        this.dismiss.emit()
      }, this.duration)
    }
  }

  /**
   * Clears the auto-dismiss timer on component destruction.
   */
  ngOnDestroy(): void {
    if (this.timer) {
      clearTimeout(this.timer)
    }
  }

  /**
   * Handles the dismiss action by hiding the toast.
   */
  onDismiss(): void {
    this.isVisible = false
    this.dismiss.emit()
  }
}
```

#### `MoleculeTooltip`

Angular Tooltip UI component with UIClassMap-driven styling.

Wraps child content and shows a tooltip on hover/focus. `hasArrow` renders
a small themed pointer at the resolved `placement` edge.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'mol-tooltip',
  standalone: true,
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  template: `
    <div
      #trigger
      [class]="cm.tooltipTrigger"
      (mouseenter)="show()"
      (mouseleave)="hide()"
      (focus)="show()"
      (blur)="hide()"
    >
      <ng-content></ng-content>
    </div>
    @if (isVisible) {
      <!--
        Outer positioned wrapper carries the top/left/z-index placement; the
        arrow renders as its SIBLING (not nested inside the visible box),
        because tooltipContent sets overflow-hidden — an arrow meant to poke
        past the box's own edge would be clipped invisible if it were a
        descendant of that element instead.
      -->
      <div [style]="positionStyle">
        <div
          #tooltipEl
          role="tooltip"
          [class]="tooltipClasses"
          [style]="style"
          [attr.data-testid]="testId"
        >
          {{ content }}
        </div>
        @if (hasArrow) {
          <span aria-hidden="true" [style]="arrowStyle"></span>
        }
      </div>
    }
  `,
})
/**
 * Molecule Tooltip class.
 * @deprecated Use the React-named component `Tooltip` instead.
 */
export class MoleculeTooltip implements OnDestroy {
  @Input() content = ''
  @Input() placement: TooltipPlacement = 'top'
  @Input() delay = 0
  @Input() hasArrow = false
  @Input() className?: string
  @Input() style?: Record<string, string>
  @Input() testId?: string

  @ViewChild('trigger') triggerRef?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>
  @ViewChild('tooltipEl') tooltipRef?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>

  isVisible = false
  positionStyle: Record<string, string> = {}
  private timeoutId?: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>

  /**
   * Retrieves the current UI class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap instance for styling.
   */
  get cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Computes the combined CSS classes for the tooltip content.
   * @returns The tooltip class name string.
   */
  get tooltipClasses(): string {
    return this.cm.cn(this.cm.tooltipContent, this.className)
  }

  /**
   * Retrieves the inline style for the tooltip's arrow element.
   * @returns Inline style positioning the rotated-square arrow.
   */
  get arrowStyle(): Record<string, string> {
    return getTooltipArrowStyle(this.placement)
  }

  /**
   * Shows the tooltip with optional delay.
   */
  show(): void {
    if (this.delay > 0) {
      this.timeoutId = setTimeout(() => {
        this.isVisible = true
        this.updatePosition()
      }, this.delay)
    } else {
      this.isVisible = true
      requestAnimationFrame(() => this.updatePosition())
    }
  }

  /**
   * Hides the tooltip and cancels any pending delay.
   */
  hide(): void {
    if (this.timeoutId) {
      clearTimeout(this.timeoutId)
    }
    this.isVisible = false
  }

  /**
   * Clears pending timeouts on component destruction.
   */
  ngOnDestroy(): void {
    if (this.timeoutId) {
      clearTimeout(this.timeoutId)
    }
  }

  /**
   * Calculates and applies the tooltip position relative to the trigger.
   */
  private updatePosition(): void {
    if (!this.triggerRef?.nativeElement || !this.tooltipRef?.nativeElement) return

    const triggerRect = this.triggerRef.nativeElement.getBoundingClientRect()
    const tooltipRect = this.tooltipRef.nativeElement.getBoundingClientRect()
    const offset = 8

    const { top, left, width, height } = triggerRect
    const tooltipWidth = tooltipRect.width
    const tooltipHeight = tooltipRect.height

    const positions: Record<TooltipPlacement, { top: number; left: number }> = {
      top: {
        top: top + window.scrollY - tooltipHeight - offset,
        left: left + window.scrollX + width / 2 - tooltipWidth / 2,
      },
      bottom: {
        top: top + window.scrollY + height + offset,
        left: left + window.scrollX + width / 2 - tooltipWidth / 2,
      },
      left: {
        top: top + window.scrollY + height / 2 - tooltipHeight / 2,
        left: left + window.scrollX - tooltipWidth - offset,
      },
      right: {
        top: top + window.scrollY + height / 2 - tooltipHeight / 2,
        left: left + window.scrollX + width + offset,
      },
      'top-start': {
        top: top + window.scrollY - tooltipHeight - offset,
        left: left + window.scrollX,
      },
      'top-end': {
        top: top + window.scrollY - tooltipHeight - offset,
        left: left + window.scrollX + width - tooltipWidth,
      },
      'bottom-start': {
        top: top + window.scrollY + height + offset,
        left: left + window.scrollX,
      },
      'bottom-end': {
        top: top + window.scrollY + height + offset,
        left: left + window.scrollX + width - tooltipWidth,
      },
    }

    const pos = positions[this.placement]
    this.positionStyle = {
      position: 'absolute',
      top: `${pos.top}px`,
      left: `${pos.left}px`,
      zIndex: '9999',
    }
  }
}
```

#### `OptimisticToggleButton`

OptimisticToggleButton component.

The host is the `<button>` itself (`button[mol-optimistic-toggle-button]`),
exactly React's root (it renders `Button`). The two labels are node-valued
props, projected as `[slot=activeLabel]` / `[slot=inactiveLabel]`; exactly
one is shown, moved onto the button with no wrapper, matching React's
`{toggle.value ? activeLabel : inactiveLabel}`.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'button[mol-optimistic-toggle-button]',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: [
    'value',
    'commit',
    'activeLabel',
    'inactiveLabel',
    'onError',
    'variant',
    'color',
    'size',
    'fullWidth',
    'leftIcon',
    'rightIcon',
    'className',
    'style',
    'testId',
    'disabled',
  ],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.type]': '"button"',
    '[attr.data-mol-id]': '"optimistic-toggle"',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
    '[disabled]': 'disabled',
    '[attr.aria-pressed]': 'state().value ? "true" : "false"',
    '[attr.aria-busy]': 'state().pending ? "true" : "false"',
    '(click)': 'handleClick()',
  },
  template: `<span #leftSlot [class]="cm().buttonIconLeft"
      ><ng-content select="[slot=leftIcon]"></ng-content></span
    ><span #activeSlot><ng-content select="[slot=activeLabel]"></ng-content></span
    ><span #inactiveSlot><ng-content select="[slot=inactiveLabel]"></ng-content></span
    ><span #rightSlot [class]="cm().buttonIconRight"
      ><ng-content select="[slot=rightIcon]"></ng-content
    ></span>`,
})
export class OptimisticToggleButton implements AfterViewInit, DoCheck, OnDestroy {
  /** The loaded server value. */
  value = false
  /** Saves the desired boolean and returns the server-confirmed boolean. */
  commit?: (desired: boolean) => Promise<boolean | void> | void
  /** Label when the displayed value is true (node-valued prop). */
  activeLabel?: unknown
  /** Label when the displayed value is false (node-valued prop). */
  inactiveLabel?: unknown
  /** Receives a failed save after the controller has restored the last confirmed value. */
  onError?: (error: unknown) => void
  /** Button variant. */
  variant?: ButtonVariant
  /** Button color. */
  color?: ColorVariant
  /** Button size. */
  size?: Size
  /** Whether the button stretches to fill its container. */
  fullWidth = false
  /** Left icon (node-valued prop). */
  leftIcon?: unknown
  /** Right icon (node-valued prop). */
  rightIcon?: unknown
  /** Extra classes. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** `data-testid`. */
  testId?: string
  /** Disables the button (permissions, never a pending save). */
  disabled = false

  /** The optimistic controller. */
  private readonly controller: OptimisticValue<boolean>
  /** The controller state (a signal so the zoneless view re-checks on every publish). */
  readonly state = signal<OptimisticValueState<boolean>>({
    value: false,
    pending: false,
    error: null,
  })

  /** The left icon wrapper. */
  @ViewChild('leftSlot') leftSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLSpanElement>
  /** The active-label capture span. */
  @ViewChild('activeSlot') activeSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLSpanElement>
  /** The inactive-label capture span. */
  @ViewChild('inactiveSlot') inactiveSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLSpanElement>
  /** The right icon wrapper. */
  @ViewChild('rightSlot') rightSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLSpanElement>

  private readonly el = inject(ElementRef)
  private readonly renderer = inject(Renderer2)
  private readonly sync = new SlotWrapper(this.el, this.renderer)
  private unsubscribe?: () => void
  private lastReceived = false
  private hasLeft = false
  private hasRight = false
  private visibleNodes: Node[] = []
  private visibleFrom: HTMLSpanElement | null = null
  private anchor: Comment | null = null

  /** Creates the optimistic controller and mirrors its state into the signal. */
  constructor() {
    this.controller = createOptimisticValue<boolean>({
      value: this.value,
      commit: (next) => this.commit?.(next) ?? Promise.resolve(next),
      onError: (error) => this.onError?.(error),
    })
    this.unsubscribe = this.controller.subscribe((next) => this.state.set({ ...next }))
    this.state.set(this.controller.getState())
  }

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Computes the button class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().button({
        variant: this.variant ?? 'solid',
        color: this.color ?? 'primary',
        size: this.size ?? 'md',
        fullWidth: this.fullWidth,
      }),
      this.className,
    )
  }

  /**
   * Serializes the inline style.
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }

  /** Flips the displayed value and saves the newest desired value. */
  handleClick(): void {
    this.controller.update((current) => !current)
  }

  /**
   * Reads slot presence at view init (view queries resolve only then) and
   * takes the label capture spans out of the DOM.
   */
  ngAfterViewInit(): void {
    this.hasLeft = hasNodes(this.leftSlot?.nativeElement)
    this.hasRight = hasNodes(this.rightSlot?.nativeElement)
    this.sync.sync(this.activeSlot?.nativeElement, false)
    this.sync.sync(this.inactiveSlot?.nativeElement, false)
    this.sync.sync(this.leftSlot?.nativeElement, this.hasLeft)
    this.sync.sync(this.rightSlot?.nativeElement, this.hasRight)
    this.syncLabels()
  }

  /**
   * Takes a newer server value the way React's effect does (only when it
   * differs from the last one received) and re-reconciles the label.
   */
  ngDoCheck(): void {
    if (this.value !== this.lastReceived) {
      this.lastReceived = this.value
      this.controller.sync(this.value)
    }
    this.syncLabels()
  }

  /** Stops mirroring controller state into the signal (unmount). */
  ngOnDestroy(): void {
    this.unsubscribe?.()
    this.visibleNodes = []
    this.visibleFrom = null
  }

  /**
   * Shows exactly the displayed value's projected children on the button —
   * React renders exactly one label and no wrapper element.
   */
  private syncLabels(): void {
    const state = this.controller.getState()
    this.showSlot(
      state.value
        ? (this.activeSlot?.nativeElement ?? null)
        : (this.inactiveSlot?.nativeElement ?? null),
    )
  }

  /**
   * Shows one slot's projected children on the button, parking whatever
   * another slot was showing back inside its capture span.
   *
   * @param span - The capture span to show (null parks everything).
   */
  private showSlot(span: HTMLSpanElement | null): void {
    const host = this.el.nativeElement as HTMLElement
    if (span === this.visibleFrom) return
    for (const node of this.visibleNodes) {
      if (node.parentNode === host) this.visibleFrom?.appendChild(node)
    }
    this.visibleNodes = []
    this.visibleFrom = null
    if (!span) return
    const nodes = Array.from(span.childNodes)
    for (const node of nodes) host.insertBefore(node, this.labelAnchor())
    this.visibleNodes = nodes
    this.visibleFrom = span
  }

  /**
   * The insertion point for the visible projected label (a comment the DOM
   * normalizer strips), kept after the left icon wrapper.
   * @returns The anchor comment.
   */
  private labelAnchor(): Comment {
    if (this.anchor?.isConnected) return this.anchor
    const anchor = this.renderer.createComment('label') as Comment
    const host = this.el.nativeElement as HTMLElement
    const left = this.leftSlot?.nativeElement
    host.insertBefore(anchor, left ? left.nextSibling : host.firstChild)
    this.anchor = anchor
    return anchor
  }
}
```

#### `PageHeader`

PageHeader component.

The host is the root `<div>` (`div[mol-page-header]`), exactly React's
root. `actions` is a node-valued prop, projected as `[slot=actions]`; its
wrapper `<div>` renders only when the slot carries nodes (React's
`{actions ? <div>…</div> : null}`), implemented by detaching the wrapper
from the DOM — the projected nodes need an always-mounted capture point.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-page-header]',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: ['title', 'description', 'breadcrumbs', 'className', 'style', 'testId', 'automationId'],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
    '[attr.data-mol-id]': 'automationId',
  },
  template: `@if (breadcrumbs && breadcrumbs.length > 0) {
      <nav [class]="cm().pageHeaderBreadcrumbs" aria-label="Breadcrumb">
        @for (crumb of breadcrumbs; track $index) {
          @if ($index > 0) {
            <span [class]="cm().pageHeaderBreadcrumbSeparator" aria-hidden="true">/</span>
          }
          @if (crumb.href) {
            <a [href]="crumb.href" [class]="cm().pageHeaderBreadcrumbItem">{{ crumb.label }}</a>
          } @else {
            <span [class]="cm().pageHeaderBreadcrumbItem">{{ crumb.label }}</span>
          }
        }
      </nav>
    }
    <div [class]="cm().flex({ justify: 'between', align: 'center' })">
      <div>
        <h1 [class]="cm().pageHeaderTitle">{{ title }}</h1>
        @if (description) {
          <p [class]="cm().pageHeaderDescription">{{ description }}</p>
        }
      </div>
      <div #actionsSlot [class]="cm().pageHeaderActions">
        <ng-content select="[slot=actions]"></ng-content>
      </div>
    </div>`,
})
export class PageHeader implements AfterViewInit, DoCheck {
  /** Page title. */
  title = ''
  /** Page description. */
  description?: string
  /** Breadcrumb trail (empty or absent renders no nav). */
  breadcrumbs?: Breadcrumb[]
  /** Extra classes. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** `data-testid`. */
  testId?: string
  /** `data-mol-id` automation id. */
  automationId?: string

  /** The actions wrapper (always in the template; detached when unused). */
  @ViewChild('actionsSlot') actionsSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>

  private readonly sync = new SlotWrapper(inject(ElementRef), inject(Renderer2))
  private hasActions = false
  private actionsParent: HTMLElement | null = null

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Computes the root class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().pageHeader, this.className)
  }

  /**
   * Serializes the inline style.
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }

  /**
   * Reads actions presence at view init (view queries resolve only then).
   */
  ngAfterViewInit(): void {
    this.actionsParent = this.actionsSlot?.nativeElement.parentElement ?? null
    this.hasActions = hasNodes(this.actionsSlot?.nativeElement)
    this.syncActions()
  }

  /**
   * Re-reconciles the actions wrapper on later passes.
   */
  ngDoCheck(): void {
    this.syncActions()
  }

  /**
   * Applies React's `{actions ? <div>…</div> : null}`.
   */
  private syncActions(): void {
    this.sync.sync(
      this.actionsSlot?.nativeElement,
      this.hasActions,
      this.actionsParent ?? undefined,
    )
  }
}
```

#### `PageShell`

PageShell component.

The host is the root `<div>` (`div[mol-page-shell]`). `sidebar` and
`topbar` are node-valued props projected as `[slot=sidebar]` /
`[slot=topbar]`; each wrapper element (`<aside>`, `<header>`) renders only
when React would render it (`{sidebar ? … : null}`), implemented by
detaching the always-mounted capture element from the DOM.

The sidebar toggle button renders only when the caller passed
`onSidebarToggle` (React's `{onSidebarToggle ? … : null}`); with an
`@Output`, that presence is the output's subscriber count.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-page-shell]',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: ['sidebar', 'topbar', 'sidebarCollapsed', 'className', 'style', 'testId', 'automationId'],
  outputs: ['sidebarToggle'],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
    '[attr.data-mol-id]': 'automationId',
  },
  template: `<aside #sidebarSlot [class]="sidebarClasses()">
      @if (toggleVisible()) {
        <button
          type="button"
          [class]="cm().pageShellSidebarToggle"
          (click)="sidebarToggle.emit($event)"
          [attr.aria-label]="toggleLabel()"
        >
          {{ sidebarCollapsed ? '▶' : '◀' }}
        </button>
      }
      <ng-content select="[slot=sidebar]"></ng-content>
    </aside>
    <div [class]="cm().flex1">
      <header #topbarSlot [class]="cm().pageShellTopbar">
        <ng-content select="[slot=topbar]"></ng-content>
      </header>
      <main [class]="cm().pageShellContent"><ng-content></ng-content></main>
    </div>`,
})
export class PageShell implements AfterViewInit, DoCheck {
  /** Sidebar content (node-valued prop). */
  sidebar?: unknown
  /** Top bar content (node-valued prop). */
  topbar?: unknown
  /** Whether the sidebar is collapsed. */
  sidebarCollapsed = false
  /** Extra classes. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** `data-testid`. */
  testId?: string
  /** `data-mol-id` automation id. */
  automationId?: string

  /** Fires when the sidebar toggle button is pressed (React's `onSidebarToggle`). */
  @Output() readonly sidebarToggle = new EventEmitter<MouseEvent>()

  /** The sidebar wrapper (always in the template; detached when unused). */
  @ViewChild('sidebarSlot') sidebarSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLElement>
  /** The top bar wrapper (always in the template; detached when unused). */
  @ViewChild('topbarSlot') topbarSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLElement>

  /**
   * Reactive translation, read in the injection context. Its `t` reads the
   * locale, so the view re-renders the text on a locale switch (the static `t`
   * does not).
   */
  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  private readonly sync = new SlotWrapper(inject(ElementRef), inject(Renderer2))
  private hasSidebar = false
  private hasTopbar = false
  private sidebarParent: HTMLElement | null = null
  private topbarParent: HTMLElement | null = null

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Computes the root class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().pageShell, this.className)
  }

  /**
   * Computes the sidebar wrapper class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  sidebarClasses(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().pageShellSidebar,
      this.sidebarCollapsed && this.cm().pageShellSidebarCollapsed,
    )
  }

  /**
   * Whether the toggle button renders (React renders it only when
   * `onSidebarToggle` is given; a bound output means the caller listens).
   * @returns True when a listener is attached.
   */
  toggleVisible(): boolean {
    return this.sidebarToggle.observed
  }

  /**
   * The toggle button's accessible label.
   * @returns The label text.
   */
  toggleLabel(): string {
    return this.sidebarCollapsed
      ? this.i18n.t('ui.pageShell.expandSidebar', undefined, { defaultValue: 'Expand sidebar' })
      : this.i18n.t('ui.pageShell.collapseSidebar', undefined, { defaultValue: 'Collapse sidebar' })
  }

  /**
   * Serializes the inline style.
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }

  /**
   * Reads slot presence at view init (view queries resolve only then).
   */
  ngAfterViewInit(): void {
    this.sidebarParent = this.sidebarSlot?.nativeElement.parentElement ?? null
    this.topbarParent = this.topbarSlot?.nativeElement.parentElement ?? null
    this.hasSidebar = hasNodes(this.sidebarSlot?.nativeElement)
    this.hasTopbar = hasNodes(this.topbarSlot?.nativeElement)
    this.syncWrappers()
  }

  /**
   * Re-reconciles the wrappers on later passes.
   */
  ngDoCheck(): void {
    this.syncWrappers()
  }

  /**
   * Applies React's `{sidebar ? … : null}` / `{topbar ? … : null}`.
   */
  private syncWrappers(): void {
    this.sync.sync(
      this.sidebarSlot?.nativeElement,
      this.hasSidebar,
      this.sidebarParent ?? undefined,
    )
    this.sync.sync(this.topbarSlot?.nativeElement, this.hasTopbar, this.topbarParent ?? undefined)
  }
}
```

#### `Pagination`

Pagination component.

The host is the `<nav>` (`nav[mol-pagination]`), exactly React's root.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'nav[mol-pagination]',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [MolIconSvg],
  inputs: [
    'page',
    'totalPages',
    'siblings',
    'boundaries',
    'size',
    'showFirstLast',
    'showPrevNext',
    'labels',
    'className',
    'style',
    'testId',
    'disabled',
  ],
  outputs: ['change'],
  host: {
    role: 'navigation',
    '[attr.aria-label]': 'navLabel()',
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
  },
  template: `<ul [class]="cm().paginationContent">
    @if (showFirstLast) {
      <li>
        <button
          type="button"
          [class]="buttonClasses(false)"
          (click)="handlePageChange(1)"
          [disabled]="page === 1 || !!disabled"
          [attr.aria-label]="firstLabel()"
        >
          <svg mol-icon [data]="icon('chevrons-left')" />
        </button>
      </li>
    }
    @if (showPrevNext) {
      <li>
        <button
          type="button"
          [class]="buttonClasses(false)"
          (click)="handlePageChange(page - 1)"
          [disabled]="page === 1 || !!disabled"
          [attr.aria-label]="previousLabel()"
        >
          <svg mol-icon [data]="icon('chevron-left')" />
        </button>
      </li>
    }
    @for (item of range(); track $index) {
      @if (item === 'ellipsis') {
        <li>
          <span [class]="cm().paginationEllipsis" aria-hidden="true"
            ><svg mol-icon [data]="icon('ellipsis-horizontal')"
          /></span>
        </li>
      } @else {
        <li>
          <button
            type="button"
            [class]="buttonClasses(item === page)"
            (click)="handlePageChange(item)"
            [disabled]="!!disabled"
            [attr.aria-label]="pageLabel(item)"
            [attr.aria-current]="item === page ? 'page' : null"
          >
            {{ item }}
          </button>
        </li>
      }
    }
    @if (showPrevNext) {
      <li>
        <button
          type="button"
          [class]="buttonClasses(false)"
          (click)="handlePageChange(page + 1)"
          [disabled]="page === totalPages || !!disabled"
          [attr.aria-label]="nextLabel()"
        >
          <svg mol-icon [data]="icon('chevron-right')" />
        </button>
      </li>
    }
    @if (showFirstLast) {
      <li>
        <button
          type="button"
          [class]="buttonClasses(false)"
          (click)="handlePageChange(totalPages)"
          [disabled]="page === totalPages || !!disabled"
          [attr.aria-label]="lastLabel()"
        >
          <svg mol-icon [data]="icon('chevrons-right')" />
        </button>
      </li>
    }
  </ul>`,
})
export class Pagination {
  /** The active page. */
  page = 1
  /** The total number of pages. */
  totalPages = 1
  /** Sibling pages around the active page (default 1). */
  siblings = 1
  /** Boundary pages at the start and end (default 1). */
  boundaries = 1
  /** Button size. */
  size: 'xs' | 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg' | 'xl' = 'md'
  /** Whether the first/last buttons render. */
  showFirstLast = false
  /** Whether the previous/next buttons render (default true). */
  showPrevNext = true
  /** Accessible labels. */
  labels?: PaginationLabels
  /** Extra classes for the nav. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** `data-testid`. */
  testId?: string
  /** Disables every button. */
  disabled?: boolean

  /** Fires with the requested page (React's `onChange`). */
  @Output() readonly change = new EventEmitter<number>()

  /**
   * Reactive translation, read in the injection context. Its `t` reads the
   * locale, so the view re-renders the text on a locale switch (the static `t`
   * does not).
   */
  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * The root class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().paginationRoot, this.className)
  }

  /**
   * Serializes the root's inline style.
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }

  /**
   * The generated page range.
   * @returns The range.
   */
  range(): (number | 'ellipsis')[] {
    return generatePaginationRange(this.page, this.totalPages, this.siblings, this.boundaries)
  }

  /**
   * A pagination button's class list.
   * @param isActive - Whether it is the active page.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  buttonClasses(isActive: boolean): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().pagination({ active: isActive, size: this.size }),
      this.cm().paginationInteractive,
    )
  }

  /**
   * A button glyph.
   * @param name - The icon name.
   * @returns The resolved icon.
   */
  icon(
    name:
      'chevrons-left' | 'chevron-left' | 'chevron-right' | 'chevrons-right' | 'ellipsis-horizontal',
  ): ResolvedIcon {
    return resolveIcon(name, this.cm().iconSm)
  }

  /**
   * The nav's accessible label.
   * @returns The label.
   */
  navLabel(): string {
    return (
      this.labels?.nav ??
      this.i18n.t('ui.pagination.nav', undefined, { defaultValue: 'Pagination' })
    )
  }

  /**
   * The first-page button's accessible label.
   * @returns The label.
   */
  firstLabel(): string {
    return (
      this.labels?.first ??
      this.i18n.t('ui.pagination.first', undefined, { defaultValue: 'Go to first page' })
    )
  }

  /**
   * The previous-page button's accessible label.
   * @returns The label.
   */
  previousLabel(): string {
    return (
      this.labels?.previous ??
      this.i18n.t('ui.pagination.previous', undefined, { defaultValue: 'Go to previous page' })
    )
  }

  /**
   * The next-page button's accessible label.
   * @returns The label.
   */
  nextLabel(): string {
    return (
      this.labels?.next ??
      this.i18n.t('ui.pagination.next', undefined, { defaultValue: 'Go to next page' })
    )
  }

  /**
   * The last-page button's accessible label.
   * @returns The label.
   */
  lastLabel(): string {
    return (
      this.labels?.last ??
      this.i18n.t('ui.pagination.last', undefined, { defaultValue: 'Go to last page' })
    )
  }

  /**
   * A numbered page button's accessible label (a custom `labels.goToPage`
   * wins, then the localized default).
   *
   * @param item - The page number.
   * @returns The label.
   */
  pageLabel(item: number): string {
    const custom = this.labels?.goToPage?.(item)
    if (custom !== undefined && custom !== null) return custom
    return this.i18n.t(
      'ui.pagination.goToPage',
      { page: item },
      { defaultValue: 'Go to page {{page}}' },
    )
  }

  /**
   * Requests a page change (React's `handlePageChange`).
   * @param newPage - The requested page.
   */
  handlePageChange(newPage: number): void {
    if (newPage >= 1 && newPage <= this.totalPages && newPage !== this.page && !this.disabled) {
      this.change.emit(newPage)
    }
  }
}
```

#### `PanelCloseProvider`

Provides the drawer-close callback to descendants.

Rendered internally by `UserMenu` and `SidebarUserCard` around their panel
content; apps do not normally render it directly. The host is unwrapped
(`data-parity-unwrap`, React's context provider renders no DOM) and the
projected children render directly inside it.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'mol-panel-close-provider',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: ['close'],
  host: {
    'data-parity-unwrap': '',
    style: 'display: contents',
  },
  template: `<ng-content></ng-content>`,
})
export class PanelCloseProvider implements OnDestroy {
  /** Callback that dismisses the enclosing drawer/modal. */
  close?: () => void

  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLElement> = inject(ElementRef)
  /** Registers the context value on the host (before any projected child resolves it). */
  constructor() {
    provideDomContext(this.host.nativeElement, { close: () => this.close?.() })
  }

  /** Removes the context registration (unmount). */
  ngOnDestroy(): void {
    removeDomContext(this.host.nativeElement)
  }
}
```

#### `Progress`

Progress component.

The host is the wrapper div React renders; `aria-valuenow` is clamped into
`[0, max]` to match the clamped visual bar.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-progress]',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: [
    'value',
    'max',
    'size',
    'color',
    'showValue',
    'label',
    'indeterminate',
    'className',
    'style',
    'testId',
  ],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
  },
  template: `@if (label || showValue) {
      <div [class]="cm().progressLabelContainer">
        @if (label) {
          <span [class]="cm().progressLabelText">{{ label }}</span>
        }
        @if (showValue) {
          <span [class]="cm().progressLabelText"
            ><ng-container>{{ rounded }}</ng-container
            ><ng-container>{{ '%' }}</ng-container></span
          >
        }
      </div>
    }
    <div
      [class]="cm().cn(cm().progress(), cm().progressHeight(size ?? 'md'))"
      role="progressbar"
      [attr.aria-valuenow]="indeterminate ? null : clamped"
      aria-valuemin="0"
      [attr.aria-valuemax]="max"
      [attr.aria-label]="label ?? progressLabel()"
    >
      <div [class]="barClasses()" [attr.style]="barStyle()"></div>
    </div>`,
})
export class Progress {
  /** Progress value (0–max). */
  value = 0
  /** Maximum value. */
  max = 100
  /** Progress size. */
  size?: Size
  /** Progress color. */
  color?: ColorVariant
  /** Whether the numeric value label renders. */
  showValue?: boolean
  /** Accessible label. */
  label?: string
  /** Whether the progress is indeterminate. */
  indeterminate?: boolean
  /** Extra classes joined onto the wrapper classes. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** `data-testid`. */
  testId?: string

  /**
   * Reactive translation, read in the injection context. Its `t` reads the
   * locale, so the view re-renders the text on a locale switch (the static `t`
   * does not).
   */
  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * The percentage, clamped to [0, 100].
   * @returns The percentage.
   */
  get percent(): number {
    const max = this.max ?? 100
    return Math.min(Math.max((this.value / max) * 100, 0), 100)
  }

  /**
   * The rounded percentage shown in the value label.
   * @returns The whole percentage.
   */
  get rounded(): number {
    return Math.round(this.percent)
  }

  /**
   * The clamped aria-valuenow.
   * @returns The clamped value.
   */
  get clamped(): number {
    const max = this.max ?? 100
    return Math.min(Math.max(this.value, 0), max)
  }

  /**
   * The accessible label with the localized default.
   * @returns The label text.
   */
  progressLabel(): string {
    return this.i18n.t('ui.progress.label', undefined, { defaultValue: 'Progress' })
  }

  /**
   * Computes the wrapper class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().progressWrapper, this.className)
  }

  /**
   * Computes the bar class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  barClasses(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().progressBar(),
      this.cm().progressColor(this.color ?? 'primary'),
      this.indeterminate && this.cm().progressIndeterminate,
    )
  }

  /**
   * Serializes the bar's inline style (the translateX fill, unless indeterminate).
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  barStyle(): string | null {
    if (this.indeterminate) return null
    return styleText({ transform: `translateX(-${100 - this.percent}%)` })
  }

  /**
   * Serializes the inline style.
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }
}
```

#### `PromptDialog`

PromptDialog component.

Composed from `Modal`, `Input` and `Button` exactly as React composes
them. Resolving `onSubmit` closes the dialog and resets it; a rejection
keeps it open with the value intact. The host is unwrapped
(`data-parity-unwrap`, React's `Modal` root is a fragment/portal).

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'mol-prompt-dialog',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [Button, Input, Modal],
  inputs: [
    'open',
    'title',
    'description',
    'placeholder',
    'initialValue',
    'onSubmit',
    'confirmLabel',
    'cancelLabel',
    'destructive',
    'inputMolId',
    'confirmMolId',
    'cancelMolId',
  ],
  outputs: ['close'],
  host: {
    'data-parity-unwrap': '',
    style: 'display: contents',
  },
  template: `<mol-modal [open]="open" (close)="onModalClose()"
    ><form (submit)="handleSubmit($event)">
      <div [class]="cm().stack(3)">
        <h2 #titleSlot [class]="cm().cn(cm().textSize('lg'), cm().fontWeight('bold'))">
          <ng-content select="[slot=title]"></ng-content>
        </h2>
        @if (description) {
          <p [class]="cm().textSize('sm')">{{ description }}</p>
        }
        <div
          mol-input
          [value]="value()"
          [placeholder]="placeholder"
          [disabled]="pending()"
          [attr.data-mol-id]="inputMolId"
          (change)="onInput($event)"
        ></div>
        <ng-content></ng-content>
        <div [class]="cm().flex({ justify: 'end', gap: 'sm' })">
          <button
            mol-button
            [type]="'button'"
            [variant]="'ghost'"
            [disabled]="pending()"
            (click)="close.emit($event)"
            [attr.data-mol-id]="cancelMolId"
          >
            {{ cancelText() }}</button
          ><button
            mol-button
            [type]="'submit'"
            [variant]="'solid'"
            [color]="destructive ? 'error' : 'primary'"
            [disabled]="pending()"
            [attr.data-mol-id]="confirmMolId"
          >
            {{ confirmText() }}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </form></mol-modal
  >`,
})
export class PromptDialog implements AfterViewInit, DoCheck {
  /** Whether the dialog is open. */
  open = false
  /** Dialog heading (node-valued prop). */
  title?: unknown
  /** Explanatory line under the title. */
  description?: string
  /** Input placeholder. */
  placeholder?: string
  /** Value the input starts from every time the dialog opens. */
  initialValue = ''
  /**
   * Called with the entered value on confirm. Resolve to close the dialog;
   * reject or throw to keep it open.
   */
  onSubmit?: (value: string) => void | Promise<void>
  /** Confirm-button label. */
  confirmLabel?: string
  /** Cancel-button label. */
  cancelLabel?: string
  /** Renders the confirm button in the error color. */
  destructive = false
  /** `data-mol-id` for the text input. */
  inputMolId?: string
  /** `data-mol-id` for the confirm button. */
  confirmMolId?: string
  /** `data-mol-id` for the cancel button. */
  cancelMolId?: string

  /** Fires when the dialog should close (escape, backdrop, cancel, a fulfilled submit). */
  @Output() readonly close = new EventEmitter<unknown>()

  /** The dialog heading element (its text seeds the input's aria-label). */
  @ViewChild('titleSlot') titleSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLHeadingElement>
  /** The composed input (its native element receives React's aria-label). */
  @ViewChild(Input) inputComponent?: Input

  /** The current input value. */
  readonly value = signal('')
  /** Whether a submit is in flight. */
  readonly pending = signal(false)

  /**
   * Reactive translation, read in the injection context. Its `t` reads the
   * locale, so the view re-renders the text on a locale switch (the static `t`
   * does not).
   */
  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  private lastOpen = false
  private lastInitial = ''

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * The cancel button's label.
   * @returns The label text.
   */
  cancelText(): string {
    return this.cancelLabel ?? this.i18n.t('prompt.cancel', {}, { defaultValue: 'Cancel' })
  }

  /**
   * The confirm button's label.
   * @returns The label text.
   */
  confirmText(): string {
    return this.confirmLabel ?? this.i18n.t('prompt.confirm', {}, { defaultValue: 'Confirm' })
  }

  /**
   * The input's accessible label: React uses the title when it is a plain
   * string, otherwise the placeholder.
   * @returns The label, or null when React would render none.
   */
  inputLabel(): string | null {
    const title = this.titleSlot?.nativeElement
    if (title) {
      const hasElement = Array.from(title.childNodes).some((node) => node.nodeType === 1)
      const text = (title.textContent ?? '').trim()
      if (!hasElement && text) return text
    }
    return this.placeholder ?? null
  }

  /**
   * Seeds the value when the dialog opens (React's `[open, initialValue]`
   * effect).
   */
  ngAfterViewInit(): void {
    this.applyInputLabel()
    this.lastOpen = !!this.open
    this.lastInitial = this.initialValue
    if (this.open) {
      this.value.set(this.initialValue)
      this.pending.set(false)
    }
  }

  /**
   * Re-seeds the value on a later open transition (React's effect).
   */
  ngDoCheck(): void {
    const open = !!this.open
    this.applyInputLabel()
    if (open !== this.lastOpen || (open && this.initialValue !== this.lastInitial)) {
      this.lastOpen = open
      this.lastInitial = this.initialValue
      if (open) {
        this.value.set(this.initialValue)
        this.pending.set(false)
      }
    }
  }

  /**
   * Applies React's `aria-label` to the inner input (it is a rest prop there,
   * and the view queries it needs resolve only after view init).
   */
  private applyInputLabel(): void {
    const input = this.inputComponent?.inputEl?.nativeElement
    if (!input) return
    const label = this.inputLabel()
    if (label === null) input.removeAttribute('aria-label')
    else input.setAttribute('aria-label', label)
  }

  /**
   * Tracks the typed value (React's controlled `onChange`).
   *
   * @param event - The native input event.
   */
  onInput(event: Event): void {
    this.value.set((event.target as HTMLInputElement).value)
  }

  /**
   * Modal's close request: ignored while a submit is pending (React passes a
   * no-op `onClose` while pending).
   */
  onModalClose(): void {
    if (this.pending()) return
    this.close.emit()
  }

  /**
   * Submits the entered value: resolve closes and resets; reject keeps the
   * dialog open (React's `handleSubmit`).
   *
   * @param event - The form submit event.
   */
  handleSubmit(event: Event): void {
    event.preventDefault()
    if (this.pending()) return
    this.pending.set(true)
    void Promise.resolve(this.onSubmit?.(this.value()))
      .then(() => {
        this.pending.set(false)
        this.close.emit()
      })
      .catch((_error: unknown) => {
        // Deliberate: a rejection keeps the dialog open and the caller owns
        // the error, rendering it through `children`.
        this.pending.set(false)
      })
  }
}
```

#### `RadioGroup`

RadioGroup component.

The host is the `role="radiogroup"` div React renders. Selecting an option
emits that option's VALUE (not the DOM event) through the `change` output,
exactly like React's per-option handler.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-radio-group]',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: [
    'options',
    'value',
    'size',
    'label',
    'name',
    'direction',
    'error',
    'className',
    'style',
    'testId',
    'disabled',
  ],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'className',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
    '[attr.role]': '"radiogroup"',
    '[attr.aria-label]': 'groupLabel()',
    '[attr.aria-invalid]': 'error ? "true" : null',
    '[attr.aria-describedby]': 'error ? errorId() : null',
  },
  template: `@if (label) {
      <div [class]="cm().cn(cm().label({ required: false }), cm().sp('mb', 2))">{{ label }}</div>
    }
    <div [class]="cm().radioGroupLayout(direction ?? 'vertical')">
      @for (option of options ?? []; track option.value) {
        <label
          [class]="
            cm().cn(cm().controlLabel, (disabled || option.disabled) && cm().controlDisabled)
          "
        >
          <input
            type="radio"
            [attr.name]="groupName()"
            [attr.value]="option.value"
            [attr.checked]="value === option.value ? '' : null"
            [disabled]="disabled || option.disabled"
            (change)="select($event)"
            [attr.data-state]="value === option.value ? 'checked' : 'unchecked'"
            [class]="cm().cn(cm().radio({ error: !!error }), cm().cursorPointer)"
          />
          <span [class]="cm().controlText">{{ option.label }}</span>
        </label>
      }
    </div>
    @if (error) {
      <p [attr.id]="errorId()" [class]="cm().cn(cm().formError, cm().sp('mt', 1))">{{ error }}</p>
    }`,
})
export class RadioGroup {
  /** The options to render. */
  options?: RadioOption[]
  /** The selected option value. */
  value?: string
  /** Group size (kept for prop parity). */
  size?: unknown
  /** Group label. */
  label?: string
  /** Native radio group name (defaults to a stable generated name). */
  name?: string
  /** Layout direction. */
  direction?: 'vertical' | 'horizontal'
  /** Error message. */
  error?: string
  /** Extra classes for the group root. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** `data-testid`. */
  testId?: string
  /** Disables every option. */
  disabled?: boolean

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * The native radio group name.
   * @returns The name value.
   */
  groupName(): string {
    return this.name || 'r0'
  }

  /**
   * The error paragraph id.
   * @returns The id value.
   */
  errorId(): string {
    return `${this.groupName()}-error`
  }

  /**
   * The accessible group label.
   * @returns The label text.
   */
  groupLabel(): string {
    return (
      this.label ?? this.i18n.t('ui.radioGroup.label', undefined, { defaultValue: 'Radio group' })
    )
  }

  /**
   * Serializes the inline style.
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }

  /**
   * Emits the selected option's value; the change event stops here so the
   * host-level `change` output binding does not record it a second time. Every
   * radio's checked state is then restored to the controlled `value` — checking
   * one radio natively UNCHECKS the previously selected one, so restoring only
   * the clicked input would leave the group with no selection at all.
   *
   * @param event - The native radio change event.
   */
  select(event: Event): void {
    event.stopPropagation()
    this.change.emit((event.target as HTMLInputElement).value)
    // React always controls radios (checked={value === option.value}); restore.
    const radios = (this.host.nativeElement as HTMLElement).querySelectorAll('input[type="radio"]')
    for (const radio of Array.from(radios) as HTMLInputElement[])
      radio.checked = this.value === radio.value
  }

  /** Fires the selected option's value. */
  @Output() readonly change = new EventEmitter<string>()

  /**
   * Reactive translation, read in the injection context. Its `t` reads the
   * locale, so the view re-renders the text on a locale switch (the static `t`
   * does not).
   */
  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  private readonly host = inject(ElementRef)
}
```

#### `ResponsiveAppShell`

Root of the responsive app shell: owns the breakpoint and drawer state,
closes the drawer on navigation and on desktop-crossing resizes, and
provides everything to the sub-components through the DOM-ancestor
context (React's context provider renders no DOM).

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-responsive-app-shell]',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: ['sidebarWidth', 'className', 'dataMolId'],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'rootStyle()',
    '[attr.data-mol-id]': 'dataMolId',
  },
  template: `<ng-content></ng-content>`,
})
export class ResponsiveAppShell implements DoCheck, OnDestroy {
  /** Sidebar (and drawer) width: `sm`/`md`/`lg` or an exact pixel number. */
  sidebarWidth?: ShellSidebarWidth
  /** Extra classes on the root flex container. */
  className?: string
  /** `data-mol-id` for the root (default `shell-root`). */
  dataMolId = 'shell-root'

  /** Whether the viewport is at the desktop breakpoint. */
  readonly isDesktop = useIsDesktop()
  /** Whether the mobile drawer is open. */
  readonly drawerOpen = signal(false)
  /** Whether a Drawer sub-component is composed. */
  readonly hasDrawer = signal(false)
  /** The drawer panel's DOM id. */
  readonly drawerId = nextInstanceId()
  /** Router state: navigation closes the drawer. */
  readonly router = useRouter()

  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLElement> = inject(ElementRef)
  private lastRoute: string | null = null
  private readonly routeEffect = effect(() => {
    const location = this.router.location()
    const key = `${location.pathname}${location.search}${(location as { hash?: string }).hash ?? ''}`
    if (this.lastRoute === key) return
    this.lastRoute = key
    // React's effect runs on mount too; closing a closed drawer is a no-op.
    this.drawerOpen.set(false)
  })
  private readonly desktopEffect = effect(() => {
    if (this.isDesktop()) this.drawerOpen.set(false)
  })

  /** Registers the shell context on the host before any projected child resolves it. */
  constructor() {
    provideDomContext(this.host.nativeElement, new ShellContextValueImpl(this))
  }

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * The root class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().flex({}),
      this.cm().page,
      this.cm().minH('screen'),
      this.className,
    )
  }

  /**
   * The root's inline style (row on desktop, column below the breakpoint).
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  rootStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText({ flexDirection: this.isDesktop() ? 'row' : 'column' })
  }

  /** Keeps a plain-field drawer width change in sync with the sub-components. */
  ngDoCheck(): void {
    // Nothing to reconcile: every value is read through the context getters.
  }

  /** Removes the context registration (unmount). */
  ngOnDestroy(): void {
    removeDomContext(this.host.nativeElement)
  }
}
```

#### `ResponsiveAppShellTopBar`

The shell's `<header>` landmark: the drawer menu trigger (mobile, when a
Drawer is composed), the brand, and trailing actions.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'header[mol-responsive-app-shell-topbar]',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [MolIconSvg],
  inputs: ['mobileOnly', 'className', 'dataMolId', 'testId'],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.data-mol-id]': 'dataMolId',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
  },
  template: `<div
      [class]="cm().cn(cm().flex({ align: 'center', gap: 'sm' }))"
      [style]="'min-width: 0px'"
    >
      @if (shell().hasDrawer && !shell().isDesktop) {
        <button
          type="button"
          (click)="shell().openDrawer()"
          [attr.aria-label]="triggerLabel()"
          [attr.aria-expanded]="shell().drawerOpen ? 'true' : 'false'"
          [attr.aria-controls]="shell().drawerId"
          data-mol-id="shell-drawer-open"
          [class]="triggerClasses()"
          [style]="triggerStyle()"
        >
          <svg mol-icon [data]="menuIcon()" />
        </button>
      }
      <div #brandSlot [style]="'min-width: 0px'">
        <ng-content select="[slot=brand]"></ng-content>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div #actionsSlot [class]="cm().cn(cm().flex({ align: 'center', gap: 'sm' }), cm().shrink0)">
      <ng-content select="[slot=actions]"></ng-content>
    </div>`,
})
export class ResponsiveAppShellTopBar implements AfterViewInit, DoCheck, OnDestroy {
  /** Render the top bar only below the breakpoint (no dead DOM on desktop). */
  mobileOnly = false
  /** Extra classes on the `<header>`. */
  className?: string
  /** `data-mol-id` (default `shell-topbar`). */
  dataMolId = 'shell-topbar'
  /** `data-testid`. */
  testId?: string

  /** The brand wrapper (detached when no brand is given). */
  @ViewChild('brandSlot') brandSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>
  /** The actions wrapper (detached when no actions are given). */
  @ViewChild('actionsSlot') actionsSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>

  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLElement> = inject(ElementRef)

  /**
   * Reactive translation, read in the injection context. Its `t` reads the
   * locale, so the view re-renders the text on a locale switch (the static `t`
   * does not).
   */
  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  private readonly renderer = inject(Renderer2)
  private readonly sync = new SlotWrapper(this.host, this.renderer)
  private brandParent: HTMLElement | null = null
  private actionsParent: HTMLElement | null = null
  private hasBrand = false
  private hasActions = false
  private detachedParent: Node | null = null
  private detachedAnchor: Node | null = null

  /**
   * The nearest shell context.
   * @returns The context (null outside the shell).
   */
  shell(): ShellContextValue {
    // Cached: a detached (mobileOnly) header loses its DOM ancestors, so the
    // lookup must not be repeated after the element leaves the tree.
    this.context ??= contextOrDefault(this.host.nativeElement)
    return this.context
  }

  /** The resolved shell context. */
  private context: ShellContextValue | null = null

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * The header class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().flex({ align: 'center', justify: 'between', gap: 'sm' }),
      this.cm().sp('px', 4),
      this.cm().h(14),
      this.cm().surface,
      this.cm().borderB,
      this.cm().position('sticky'),
      this.className,
    )
  }

  /**
   * The header's inline style (sticky offset and z-index below the drawer).
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText({ top: 0, zIndex: 20 })
  }

  /**
   * The drawer trigger's class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  triggerClasses(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().flex({ align: 'center', justify: 'center' }),
      this.cm().shrink0,
      this.cm().roundedFull,
      this.cm().cursorPointer,
    )
  }

  /** The drawer trigger's inline size (44px minimum tap target). */
  triggerStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText({ width: 44, height: 44 })
  }

  /**
   * The trigger's accessible label.
   * @returns The label text.
   */
  triggerLabel(): string {
    return this.i18n.t('appShell.openMenu', {}, { defaultValue: 'Open navigation menu' })
  }

  /**
   * The menu glyph.
   * @returns The resolved icon.
   */
  menuIcon(): ResolvedIcon {
    return resolveIcon('menu', this.cm().iconMd)
  }

  /** Reads slot presence and applies the `mobileOnly` mount condition. */
  ngAfterViewInit(): void {
    this.brandParent = this.brandSlot?.nativeElement.parentElement ?? null
    this.actionsParent = this.actionsSlot?.nativeElement.parentElement ?? null
    this.hasBrand = hasNodes(this.brandSlot?.nativeElement)
    this.hasActions = hasNodes(this.actionsSlot?.nativeElement)
    this.syncWrappers()
    this.syncVisibility()
  }

  /** Re-reconciles the wrappers and the mount condition. */
  ngDoCheck(): void {
    this.syncWrappers()
    this.syncVisibility()
  }

  /** Restores a detached header (unmount). */
  ngOnDestroy(): void {
    this.reattach()
  }

  /** Applies React's `{mobileOnly && isDesktop ? null : <header>}`. */
  private syncVisibility(): void {
    const hidden = this.mobileOnly && this.shell().isDesktop
    const host = this.host.nativeElement
    if (hidden && host.isConnected) {
      this.detachedParent = host.parentNode
      this.detachedAnchor = host.nextSibling
      host.parentNode?.removeChild(host)
    } else if (!hidden && !host.isConnected && this.detachedParent) {
      this.detachedParent.insertBefore(host, this.detachedAnchor)
      this.detachedParent = null
      this.detachedAnchor = null
    }
  }

  /** Restores the header into its parent (Angular's removal needs it there). */
  private reattach(): void {
    const host = this.host.nativeElement
    if (!host.isConnected && this.detachedParent) {
      this.detachedParent.insertBefore(host, this.detachedAnchor)
      this.detachedParent = null
      this.detachedAnchor = null
    }
  }

  /** Applies React's conditional brand/actions wrappers. */
  private syncWrappers(): void {
    // While the header is detached (mobileOnly on desktop) every node reads as
    // disconnected; attempting a re-attach would append it in the wrong place.
    if (!this.host.nativeElement.isConnected) return
    this.sync.sync(this.brandSlot?.nativeElement, this.hasBrand, this.brandParent ?? undefined)
    this.sync.sync(
      this.actionsSlot?.nativeElement,
      this.hasActions,
      this.actionsParent ?? undefined,
    )
  }
}
```

#### `ResponsiveAppShellSidebar`

The desktop sidebar (`<aside>` + `<nav>` landmarks); renders nothing below
the breakpoint (the Drawer carries the nav there).

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'aside[mol-responsive-app-shell-sidebar]',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: ['navLabel', 'className', 'dataMolId', 'testId'],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.data-mol-id]': 'dataMolId',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
  },
  template: `<nav [attr.aria-label]="navLabelText()" [class]="cm().cn(cm().flex1, cm().stack(1))">
      <ng-content></ng-content>
    </nav>
    <div
      #footerSlot
      [class]="
        cm().cn(
          cm().flex({ align: 'center', justify: 'between', gap: 'sm' }),
          cm().borderT,
          cm().sp('p', 3),
          cm().shrink0
        )
      "
    >
      <ng-content select="[slot=footer]"></ng-content>
    </div>`,
})
export class ResponsiveAppShellSidebar implements AfterViewInit, DoCheck, OnDestroy {
  /** Accessible name for the `<nav>` landmark. */
  navLabel?: string
  /** Extra classes on the `<aside>`. */
  className?: string
  /** `data-mol-id` (default `shell-sidebar`). */
  dataMolId = 'shell-sidebar'
  /** `data-testid`. */
  testId?: string

  /** The footer wrapper (detached when no footer is given). */
  @ViewChild('footerSlot') footerSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>

  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLElement> = inject(ElementRef)

  /**
   * Reactive translation, read in the injection context. Its `t` reads the
   * locale, so the view re-renders the text on a locale switch (the static `t`
   * does not).
   */
  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  private readonly renderer = inject(Renderer2)
  private readonly sync = new SlotWrapper(this.host, this.renderer)
  private footerParent: HTMLElement | null = null
  private hasFooter = false
  private detachedParent: Node | null = null
  private detachedAnchor: Node | null = null

  /**
   * The nearest shell context.
   * @returns The context (defaults outside the shell).
   */
  shell(): ShellContextValue {
    // Cached: a detached (mobile) sidebar loses its DOM ancestors.
    this.context ??= contextOrDefault(this.host.nativeElement)
    return this.context
  }

  /** The resolved shell context. */
  private context: ShellContextValue | null = null

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * The aside class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().flex({ direction: 'col' }),
      this.cm().shrink0,
      this.cm().surface,
      this.cm().borderR,
      this.cm().h('screen'),
      this.cm().position('sticky'),
      this.className,
    )
  }

  /**
   * The aside's inline style (exact width, sticky top, internal scroll).
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText({ top: 0, width: this.shell().sidebarWidthPx, overflowY: 'auto' })
  }

  /**
   * The nav's accessible label.
   * @returns The label text.
   */
  navLabelText(): string {
    return (
      this.navLabel ??
      this.i18n.t('appShell.primaryNav', {}, { defaultValue: 'Primary navigation' })
    )
  }

  /** Applies the initial mount condition and footer presence. */
  ngAfterViewInit(): void {
    this.footerParent = this.footerSlot?.nativeElement.parentElement ?? null
    this.hasFooter = hasNodes(this.footerSlot?.nativeElement)
    this.syncWrapper()
    this.syncVisibility()
  }

  /** Re-reconciles the mount condition and the footer wrapper. */
  ngDoCheck(): void {
    this.syncWrapper()
    this.syncVisibility()
  }

  /** Restores a detached aside (unmount). */
  ngOnDestroy(): void {
    this.reattach()
  }

  /** Applies React's `{!isDesktop ? null : <aside>}`. */
  private syncVisibility(): void {
    const hidden = !this.shell().isDesktop
    const host = this.host.nativeElement
    if (hidden && host.isConnected) {
      this.detachedParent = host.parentNode
      this.detachedAnchor = host.nextSibling
      host.parentNode?.removeChild(host)
    } else if (!hidden && !host.isConnected && this.detachedParent) {
      this.detachedParent.insertBefore(host, this.detachedAnchor)
      this.detachedParent = null
      this.detachedAnchor = null
    }
  }

  /** Restores the aside into its parent. */
  private reattach(): void {
    const host = this.host.nativeElement
    if (!host.isConnected && this.detachedParent) {
      this.detachedParent.insertBefore(host, this.detachedAnchor)
      this.detachedParent = null
      this.detachedAnchor = null
    }
  }

  /** Applies React's conditional footer wrapper. */
  private syncWrapper(): void {
    // While the aside is detached (mobile) every node reads as disconnected.
    if (!this.host.nativeElement.isConnected) return
    this.sync.sync(this.footerSlot?.nativeElement, this.hasFooter, this.footerParent ?? undefined)
  }
}
```

#### `ResponsiveAppShellDrawer`

The mobile nav drawer (<768px): a left-anchored dialog panel over a
backdrop, portaled into `document.body`.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'mol-responsive-app-shell-drawer',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [MolIconSvg],
  inputs: ['navLabel', 'showCloseButton', 'className', 'dataMolId', 'closeDataMolId', 'testId'],
  host: {
    'data-parity-unwrap': '',
    style: 'display: contents',
    '[attr.data-open]': 'shell().drawerOpen ? "true" : null',
  },
  template: `<div #overlay [class]="cm().overlay" aria-hidden="true"></div>
    <div
      #wrapper
      [class]="cm().cn(cm().position('fixed'), cm().inset0)"
      [style]="wrapperStyle()"
      (click)="handleBackdropClick($event)"
    >
      <div
        #panel
        [id]="shell().drawerId"
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        [attr.aria-label]="navLabelText()"
        tabindex="-1"
        [class]="panelClasses()"
        [style]="panelStyle()"
        [attr.data-mol-id]="dataMolId"
        [attr.data-testid]="testId"
        (click)="stopPropagation($event)"
      >
        <div
          #headerRow
          [class]="
            cm().cn(
              cm().flex({ align: 'center', justify: 'between', gap: 'sm' }),
              cm().sp('p', 3),
              cm().shrink0
            )
          "
        >
          <div #headerSlot [style]="'min-width: 0px'">
            <ng-content select="[slot=header]"></ng-content>
          </div>
          @if (!headerPresent()) {
            <span aria-hidden="true"></span>
          }
          @if (showCloseButton) {
            <button
              type="button"
              (click)="shell().closeDrawer()"
              [attr.aria-label]="closeLabel()"
              [attr.data-mol-id]="closeDataMolId"
              [class]="closeClasses()"
            >
              <svg mol-icon [data]="closeIcon()" />
            </button>
          }
        </div>
        <nav [attr.aria-label]="navLabelText()" [class]="cm().cn(cm().flex1, cm().stack(1))">
          <ng-content></ng-content>
        </nav>
        <div
          #footerSlot
          [class]="
            cm().cn(
              cm().flex({ direction: 'col', gap: 'sm' }),
              cm().borderT,
              cm().sp('p', 3),
              cm().shrink0
            )
          "
        >
          <ng-content select="[slot=footer]"></ng-content>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>`,
})
export class ResponsiveAppShellDrawer implements AfterViewInit, DoCheck, OnDestroy {
  navLabel?: string
  showCloseButton = true
  className?: string
  dataMolId = 'shell-drawer'
  closeDataMolId = 'shell-drawer-close'
  testId?: string

  @ViewChild('overlay') overlay?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>
  @ViewChild('wrapper') wrapper?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>
  @ViewChild('panel') panel?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>
  @ViewChild('headerRow') headerRow?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>
  @ViewChild('headerSlot') headerSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>
  @ViewChild('footerSlot') footerSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>

  readonly headerPresent = signal(false)

  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLElement> = inject(ElementRef)

  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  private readonly renderer = inject(Renderer2)
  private readonly sync = new SlotWrapper(this.host, this.renderer)
  private readonly scrollLock = createScrollLock()
  private hasFooter = false
  private footerParent: HTMLElement | null = null
  private headerParent: HTMLElement | null = null
  private headerRowParent: HTMLElement | null = null
  private attached = false
  private locked = false
  private lastOpen = false
  private previouslyFocused: HTMLElement | null = null
  private listeners: (() => void)[] = []

  shell(): ShellContextValue {
    this.context ??= contextOrDefault(this.host.nativeElement)
    return this.context
  }

  private context: ShellContextValue | null = null

  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  panelClasses(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().flex({ direction: 'col' }), this.cm().surface, this.className)
  }

  panelStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText({
      width: this.shell().sidebarWidthPx,
      maxWidth: '85vw',
      overflowY: 'auto',
    })
  }

  wrapperStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText({ zIndex: 41 })
  }

  closeClasses(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().flex({ align: 'center', justify: 'center' }),
      this.cm().w(10),
      this.cm().h(10),
      this.cm().shrink0,
      this.cm().roundedFull,
      this.cm().cursorPointer,
      this.cm().textMuted,
    )
  }

  navLabelText(): string {
    return (
      this.navLabel ??
      this.i18n.t('appShell.primaryNav', {}, { defaultValue: 'Primary navigation' })
    )
  }

  closeLabel(): string {
    return this.i18n.t('appShell.closeMenu', {}, { defaultValue: 'Close navigation menu' })
  }

  closeIcon(): ResolvedIcon {
    return resolveIcon('x-mark', this.cm().iconMd)
  }

  stopPropagation(event: MouseEvent): void {
    event.stopPropagation()
  }

  handleBackdropClick(event: MouseEvent): void {
    if (event.target === event.currentTarget) this.shell().closeDrawer()
  }

  ngAfterViewInit(): void {
    this.shell().registerDrawer(true)
    this.headerParent = this.headerSlot?.nativeElement.parentElement ?? null
    this.headerRowParent = this.headerRow?.nativeElement.parentElement ?? null
    this.footerParent = this.footerSlot?.nativeElement.parentElement ?? null
    this.headerPresent.set(hasNodes(this.headerSlot?.nativeElement))
    this.hasFooter = hasNodes(this.footerSlot?.nativeElement)
    this.syncWrappers()
    const open = this.shell().drawerOpen && !this.shell().isDesktop
    this.lastOpen = open
    if (open) this.openPanel()
    else this.detachPortal()
  }

  ngDoCheck(): void {
    this.hasFooter = hasNodes(this.footerSlot?.nativeElement)
    this.syncWrappers()
    this.syncOpen()
  }

  ngOnDestroy(): void {
    this.shell().registerDrawer(false)
    this.removeListeners()
    if (this.locked) {
      this.scrollLock.unlock()
      this.locked = false
    }
    const host = this.host.nativeElement
    if (this.attached) {
      const overlay = this.overlay?.nativeElement
      const wrapper = this.wrapper?.nativeElement
      if (overlay?.parentNode === document.body) host.appendChild(overlay)
      if (wrapper?.parentNode === document.body) host.appendChild(wrapper)
      this.attached = false
    }
  }

  private syncWrappers(): void {
    if (!this.attached) return
    this.sync.sync(
      this.headerRow?.nativeElement,
      this.headerPresent() || this.showCloseButton,
      this.headerRowParent ?? undefined,
    )
    this.sync.sync(
      this.headerSlot?.nativeElement,
      this.headerPresent(),
      this.headerParent ?? undefined,
    )
    this.sync.sync(this.footerSlot?.nativeElement, this.hasFooter, this.footerParent ?? undefined)
  }

  private syncOpen(): void {
    const open = this.shell().drawerOpen && !this.shell().isDesktop
    if (open === this.lastOpen) return
    this.lastOpen = open
    if (open) {
      this.openPanel()
    } else {
      this.removeListeners()
      this.detachPortal()
      if (this.locked) {
        this.scrollLock.unlock()
        this.locked = false
      }
      const toRestore = this.previouslyFocused
      if (toRestore && document.contains(toRestore)) toRestore.focus()
    }
  }

  private openPanel(): void {
    this.previouslyFocused = (document.activeElement as HTMLElement | null) ?? null
    this.attachPortal()
    this.syncWrappers()
    const panel = this.panel?.nativeElement
    if (panel) {
      const focusables = getFocusableElements(panel)
      ;(focusables[0] ?? panel).focus()
    }
    this.scrollLock.lock()
    this.locked = true
    this.addListeners()
  }

  private attachPortal(): void {
    const overlay = this.overlay?.nativeElement
    const wrapper = this.wrapper?.nativeElement
    if (!overlay || !wrapper) return
    document.body.appendChild(overlay)
    document.body.appendChild(wrapper)
    this.attached = true
  }
// … truncated — 4 more members: detachPortal, addListeners, removeListeners, handleKeyDown; the full declaration is in node_modules/@molecule/app-ui-angular/dist/components/responsive-app-shell.component.d.ts
```
