#### `ResponsiveAppShellContent`

The shell's `<main>` landmark.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'main[mol-responsive-app-shell-content]',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: ['className', 'style', 'dataMolId', 'testId'],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.data-mol-id]': 'dataMolId',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
  },
  template: `<ng-content></ng-content>`,
})
export class ResponsiveAppShellContent {
  /** Extra classes on the `<main>`. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline styles on the `<main>`. */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** `data-mol-id` (default `shell-content`). */
  dataMolId = 'shell-content'
  /** `data-testid`. */
  testId?: string

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * The main class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().flex1, this.className)
  }

  /**
   * The main's inline style (`min-width: 0` plus the caller's style).
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText({ minWidth: 0, ...this.style })
  }
}
```

#### `Select`

Select component.

The host is the `div.inputWrapper` React renders. React's select value is
written through the value PROPERTY (a `select` has no value attribute);
a controlled `value` is restored after a change exactly like React's
controlled-input state restore.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-select]',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [MolIconSvg],
  inputs: [
    'options',
    'value',
    'defaultValue',
    'size',
    'label',
    'placeholder',
    'error',
    'hint',
    'clearable',
    'className',
    'style',
    'testId',
    'disabled',
    'required',
    'name',
    'id',
  ],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'cm().inputWrapper',
  },
  template: `@if (label) {
      <label
        [attr.for]="selectId()"
        [class]="cm().cn(cm().label({ required: !!required }), cm().labelBlock)"
        >{{ label }}</label
      >
    }
    <div [class]="cm().inputInner">
      <select
        [attr.id]="selectId()"
        [attr.name]="name"
        [disabled]="disabled"
        [attr.required]="required ? '' : null"
        [attr.aria-invalid]="error || fieldContext()?.invalid ? 'true' : 'false'"
        [attr.aria-describedby]="mergedDescribedBy()"
        [class]="classes()"
        [attr.style]="hostStyle()"
        [attr.data-testid]="testId"
        (change)="handleChange($event)"
        #selectEl
      >
        @if (placeholder) {
          <option value="" disabled>{{ placeholder }}</option>
        }
        @if (clearable && !placeholder) {
          <option value="">{{ clearText() }}</option>
        }
        @for (entry of optionGroups(); track $index) {
          @if (entry.group === '') {
            @for (option of entry.options; track option.value) {
              <option [attr.value]="option.value" [attr.disabled]="option.disabled ? '' : null">
                {{ option.label }}
              </option>
            }
          } @else {
            <optgroup [attr.label]="entry.group">
              @for (option of entry.options; track option.value) {
                <option [attr.value]="option.value" [attr.disabled]="option.disabled ? '' : null">
                  {{ option.label }}
                </option>
              }
            </optgroup>
          }
        }
      </select>
      <div [class]="cm().inputRightElement" [attr.style]="'pointer-events: none'">
        <svg mol-icon [data]="chevron()" />
      </div>
    </div>
    @if (error) {
      <p [attr.id]="selectId() + '-error'" [class]="cm().formError">{{ error }}</p>
    }
    @if (hint && !error) {
      <p [attr.id]="selectId() + '-hint'" [class]="cm().formHint">{{ hint }}</p>
    }`,
})
export class Select implements AfterViewInit, AfterViewChecked {
  options?: SelectPropsOption[]
  value?: string
  defaultValue?: string
  size?: Size
  label?: string
  placeholder?: string
  error?: string
  hint?: string
  clearable?: boolean
  className?: string
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  testId?: string
  disabled?: boolean
  required?: boolean
  name?: string
  id?: string

  @Output() readonly change = new EventEmitter<Event>()
  @Output() readonly valueChange = new EventEmitter<string>()

  @ViewChild('selectEl') selectEl?: ElementRef<HTMLSelectElement>

  private readonly host = inject(ElementRef)

  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  private restDescribedBy: string | null = null
  private restDescribedByCaptured = false

  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  selectId(): string {
    return this.id || this.fieldContext()?.id || this.name || 'r0'
  }

  describedBy(): string | null {
    if (this.error) return `${this.selectId()}-error`
    if (this.hint) return `${this.selectId()}-hint`
    return null
  }
  fieldContext(): FormFieldContext | null {
    return fieldContext(this.host.nativeElement as HTMLElement)
  }

  mergedDescribedBy(): string | null {
    const target = this.fieldContext()?.describedBy
    if (target) return [this.restDescribedBy, target].filter(Boolean).join(' ')
    return this.restDescribedBy ?? this.describedBy()
  }

  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().select({ error: !!this.error, size: this.size ?? 'md' }),
      this.cm().selectNative,
      this.cm().inputPadRight,
      this.className,
    )
  }

  clearText(): string {
    return this.i18n.t('ui.select.clear', undefined, { defaultValue: '--' })
  }

  chevron(): ResolvedIcon {
    return resolveIcon('chevron-down', this.cm().cn(this.cm().iconSm, this.cm().textMuted))
  }

  optionGroups(): { group: string; options: SelectPropsOption[] }[] {
    const acc = new Map<string, SelectPropsOption[]>()
    for (const option of this.options ?? []) {
      const group = option.group || ''
      const list = acc.get(group)
      if (list) list.push(option as SelectPropsOption)
      else acc.set(group, [option as SelectPropsOption])
    }
    return [...acc.entries()].map(([group, options]) => ({ group, options }))
  }

  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }

  handleChange(event: Event): void {
    event.stopPropagation()
    this.change.emit(event)
    this.valueChange.emit((event.target as HTMLSelectElement).value)
    this.applyValue(false)
  }

  ngAfterViewInit(): void {
    if (!this.restDescribedByCaptured) {
      this.restDescribedBy = this.host.nativeElement.getAttribute('aria-describedby')
      this.restDescribedByCaptured = true
    }
    moveRestAttributes(this.host.nativeElement, this.selectEl?.nativeElement)
    this.applyValue(true)
  }

  ngAfterViewChecked(): void {
    this.applyValue(false)
  }

  private applyValue(initial: boolean): void {
    const select = this.selectEl?.nativeElement
    if (!select) return
    if (this.value !== undefined) {
      const next = String(this.value)
      if (select.value !== next && Array.from(select.options).some((o) => o.value === next))
        select.value = next
    } else if (initial) {
      if (this.defaultValue !== undefined) {
        const target = String(this.defaultValue)
        for (const option of Array.from(select.options)) {
          if (option.value === target) option.setAttribute('selected', '')
          else option.removeAttribute('selected')
        }
        if (select.value !== target) select.value = target
      } else if (select.multiple) {
        select.selectedIndex = -1
      }
    }
  }
}
```

_(Member comments are left out to fit; the full declaration is in `node_modules/@molecule/app-ui-angular/dist/components/select.component.d.ts`.)_

#### `Separator`

Separator component.

Decorative separators render `role="none"` and no orientation; a semantic
one (`decorative={false}`) renders `role="separator"` plus
`aria-orientation`.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-separator]',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: ['orientation', 'decorative', 'className', 'style', 'testId'],
  host: {
    '[attr.role]': 'decorative ? "none" : "separator"',
    '[attr.aria-orientation]': 'decorative ? null : orientation',
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
  },
  template: '',
})
export class Separator {
  /** Orientation of the separator. */
  orientation: 'horizontal' | 'vertical' = 'horizontal'
  /** Decorative separators are purely visual. */
  decorative = true
  /** Extra classes. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** `data-testid`. */
  testId?: string

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Computes the separator class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().separator({ orientation: this.orientation }), this.className)
  }

  /**
   * Serializes the inline style.
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }
}
```

#### `SidebarUserCard`

SidebarUserCard component.

React's root is a FRAGMENT (the trigger button plus a drawer), so the host
is unwrapped (`data-parity-unwrap`, `display: contents`) and the trigger
button is adopted beside it. Identity comes from `useAuth()` unless the
matching props override it.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'mol-sidebar-user-card',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [Modal, PanelCloseProvider],
  inputs: [
    'name',
    'secondaryLine',
    'avatarUrl',
    'ariaLabelKey',
    'ariaLabelDefault',
    'dataMolId',
    'className',
  ],
  host: {
    'data-parity-unwrap': '',
    style: 'display: contents',
  },
  template: `<button
      #trigger
      type="button"
      (click)="isOpen.set(true)"
      [attr.aria-label]="ariaLabel()"
      [attr.data-mol-id]="dataMolId"
      [class]="triggerClasses()"
    >
      @if (displayAvatar(); as avatar) {
        <img [src]="avatar" alt="" [class]="avatarClasses()" />
      } @else {
        <span aria-hidden="true" [class]="initialsClasses()" [style]="initialsStyle()">{{
          initials()
        }}</span>
      }
      <span [class]="cm().cn(cm().minW(0), cm().flex1)"
        ><span [class]="nameClasses()">{{ displayName() }}</span>
        @if (displaySecondary(); as secondary) {
          <span [class]="secondaryClasses()">{{ secondary }}</span>
        }
      </span></button
    ><mol-modal [open]="isOpen()" (close)="isOpen.set(false)" [className]="cm().drawer"
      ><mol-panel-close-provider [close]="closePanel"
        ><ng-content></ng-content></mol-panel-close-provider
    ></mol-modal>`,
})
export class SidebarUserCard implements AfterViewInit, OnDestroy {
  /** Display name override (falls back to the auth user, then the guest label). */
  name?: string
  /** Secondary line under the name (falls back to the auth user's email). */
  secondaryLine?: string
  /** Avatar image URL override. */
  avatarUrl?: string
  /** i18n key for the trigger's aria-label (default `sidebarUserCard.open`). */
  ariaLabelKey?: string
  /** Fallback aria-label (default `Open account menu`). */
  ariaLabelDefault?: string
  /** `data-mol-id` for the trigger (default `sidebar-user-card`). */
  dataMolId = 'sidebar-user-card'
  /** Extra classes for the trigger. */
  className?: string

  /** Whether the drawer is open. */
  readonly isOpen = signal(false)
  /** Auth state (identity defaults). */
  readonly auth = useAuth<AccountUserShape>()
  /** Router state: a route change closes the drawer. */
  readonly router = useRouter()

  /**
   * Reactive translation, read in the injection context. Its `t` reads the
   * locale, so the view re-renders the text on a locale switch (the static `t`
   * does not).
   */
  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  /** The trigger button (adopted beside the host). */
  @ViewChild('trigger') trigger?: ElementRef<HTMLButtonElement>

  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLElement> = inject(ElementRef)
  private adopted = false
  private movedRest = false
  private readonly routeEffect = effect(() => {
    const location = this.router.location()
    void `${location.pathname}${location.search}`
    this.isOpen.set(false)
  })

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /** The trigger's accessible label. */
  ariaLabel(): string {
    return this.i18n.t(
      this.ariaLabelKey ?? 'sidebarUserCard.open',
      {},
      {
        defaultValue: this.ariaLabelDefault ?? 'Open account menu',
      },
    )
  }

  /**
   * The resolved display name (React's fallback chain).
   * @returns The name.
   */
  displayName(): string {
    return (
      this.name ??
      this.auth.user()?.name ??
      this.auth.user()?.email ??
      this.i18n.t('sidebarUserCard.guest', {}, { defaultValue: 'Guest' })
    )
  }

  /**
   * The resolved secondary line (prop override, else the auth email).
   * @returns The line, or undefined.
   */
  displaySecondary(): string | undefined {
    return this.secondaryLine ?? this.auth.user()?.email ?? undefined
  }

  /**
   * The resolved avatar URL (prop override, else the auth user's).
   * @returns The URL, or undefined.
   */
  displayAvatar(): string | undefined {
    return this.avatarUrl ?? this.auth.user()?.avatarUrl ?? undefined
  }

  /** The name/email initials. */
  initials(): string {
    return getInitials(this.displayName())
  }

  /** The trigger's class list. */
  triggerClasses(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().flex({ align: 'center', gap: 'sm' }),
      this.cm().sp('px', 3),
      this.cm().sp('py', 3),
      this.cm().sidebarUserCardTrigger,
      this.className,
    )
  }

  /** The avatar image class list. */
  avatarClasses(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().w(10),
      this.cm().h(10),
      this.cm().roundedFull,
      this.cm().shrink0,
      this.cm().objectCover,
    )
  }

  /** The initials badge class list. */
  initialsClasses(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().w(10),
      this.cm().h(10),
      this.cm().roundedFull,
      this.cm().flex({ align: 'center', justify: 'center' }),
      this.cm().textSize('sm'),
      this.cm().fontWeight('bold'),
      this.cm().shrink0,
    )
  }

  /** The initials badge's inline style. */
  initialsStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText({
      backgroundColor: 'var(--color-primary)',
      color: 'var(--color-on-primary, #ffffff)',
    })
  }

  /** The display-name class list. */
  nameClasses(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().textSize('sm'),
      this.cm().fontWeight('semibold'),
      this.cm().displayBlock,
      this.cm().textTruncate,
    )
  }

  /** The secondary-line class list. */
  secondaryClasses(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().textSize('xs'),
      this.cm().displayBlock,
      this.cm().textTruncate,
      this.cm().textMuted,
    )
  }

  /** Closes the drawer (the panel-close context's callback). */
  closePanel = (): void => {
    this.isOpen.set(false)
  }

  /** Adopts the trigger beside the host and moves rest props onto it. */
  ngAfterViewInit(): void {
    const trigger = this.trigger?.nativeElement
    const host = this.host.nativeElement
    if (!trigger) return
    if (host.parentNode) {
      host.parentNode.insertBefore(trigger, host)
      this.adopted = true
    }
    if (!this.movedRest) {
      moveRestAttributes(host, trigger)
      this.movedRest = true
    }
  }

  /** Restores the adopted trigger (unmount). */
  ngOnDestroy(): void {
    if (this.adopted) this.host.nativeElement.appendChild(this.trigger?.nativeElement as Node)
  }
}
```

#### `Skeleton`

Skeleton loading placeholder.

The host is the skeleton `<div>`; width/height/borderRadius accept React's
number-or-string shapes (numbers become px), and a circle skeleton without
a height uses the width for both, exactly like React.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-skeleton]',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: [
    'width',
    'height',
    'circle',
    'borderRadius',
    'animation',
    'className',
    'style',
    'testId',
  ],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
  },
  template: '',
})
export class Skeleton {
  /** Width (number = px, or any CSS value). */
  width?: number | string
  /** Height (number = px, or any CSS value). */
  height?: number | string
  /** Whether the skeleton is a circle. */
  circle = false
  /** Border radius override (number = px). */
  borderRadius?: number | string
  /** Animation style. */
  animation: 'pulse' | 'wave' | 'none' = 'pulse'
  /** Extra classes. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** `data-testid`. */
  testId?: string

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Computes the skeleton class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().skeleton(),
      this.circle && this.cm().skeletonCircle,
      this.animation === 'none' && this.cm().skeletonNone,
      this.animation === 'wave' && this.cm().skeletonWave,
      this.className,
    )
  }

  /**
   * Serializes the merged inline style.
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    const computed: Record<string, unknown> = {
      ...this.style,
      width: typeof this.width === 'number' ? `${this.width}px` : this.width,
      height: typeof this.height === 'number' ? `${this.height}px` : this.height,
      borderRadius: this.circle
        ? '9999px'
        : typeof this.borderRadius === 'number'
          ? `${this.borderRadius}px`
          : this.borderRadius,
    }
    // If circle, make width and height equal.
    if (this.circle && this.width && !this.height) {
      computed.height = computed.width
    }
    return styleText(computed)
  }
}
```

#### `SkeletonText`

Skeleton text lines (a container with N skeleton bars; the last one is 60%).

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-skeleton-text]',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [Skeleton],
  inputs: ['lines', 'className'],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'classes()',
  },
  template: `@for (line of lineList; track $index) {
    <div mol-skeleton [height]="16" [width]="$index === lines - 1 ? '60%' : '100%'"></div>
  }`,
})
export class SkeletonText {
  /** Number of skeleton lines. */
  lines = 3
  /** Extra classes. */
  className?: string

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * An array as long as the line count (template iteration).
   * @returns Placeholder items.
   */
  get lineList(): number[] {
    return Array.from({ length: this.lines })
  }

  /**
   * Computes the container class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().skeletonTextContainer, this.className)
  }
}
```

#### `SkeletonCircle`

Skeleton circle (avatar placeholder).

React renders `<Skeleton circle width={size} height={size} className/>` —
a single div; the host IS that div (no wrapper).

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-skeleton-circle]',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: ['size', 'className'],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
  },
  template: '',
})
export class SkeletonCircle {
  /** Circle diameter in pixels. */
  size = 40
  /** Extra classes. */
  className?: string

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Computes the class list (a circle skeleton).
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().skeleton(), this.cm().skeletonCircle, this.className)
  }

  /**
   * Serializes the circle inline style.
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    const px = typeof this.size === 'number' ? `${this.size}px` : this.size
    return styleText({ width: px, height: px, borderRadius: '9999px' })
  }
}
```

#### `Spinner`

Spinner component.

The host element is the `<div role="status">` React renders. A raw CSS
color (not a known color name) is expressed as inline border colors, and
`data-*`/`aria-*` rest props land on the host (React's spread target).

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-spinner]',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: ['size', 'color', 'label', 'className', 'style', 'testId'],
  host: {
    '[attr.role]': '"status"',
    '[attr.aria-label]': 'ariaLabel()',
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
  },
  template: `@if (label) {
    <span [class]="cm().srOnly">{{ label }}</span>
  }`,
})
export class Spinner {
  /** Spinner size. */
  size: Size = 'md'
  /** Named color variant or a raw CSS color. */
  color?: string
  /** Accessible label; also renders a screen-reader-only text node. */
  label?: string
  /** Extra classes joined onto the spinner classes. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** `data-testid`. */
  testId?: string

  /**
   * Reactive translation, read in the injection context. Its `t` reads the
   * locale, so the view re-renders the text on a locale switch (the static `t`
   * does not).
   */
  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * The accessible label (localized default when not given).
   * @returns The aria-label text.
   */
  ariaLabel(): string {
    return this.label || this.i18n.t('ui.spinner.loading', undefined, { defaultValue: 'Loading' })
  }

  /**
   * Computes the spinner class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().spinner({ size: this.size }), this.className)
  }

  /**
   * Serializes the merged inline style (caller style plus a raw-color border
   * override when `color` is a CSS color rather than a named variant).
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    const colorStyle =
      this.color &&
      typeof this.color === 'string' &&
      !NAMED_COLORS.includes(this.color as (typeof NAMED_COLORS)[number])
        ? { borderColor: this.color, borderTopColor: 'transparent' }
        : undefined
    return styleText({ ...this.style, ...colorStyle })
  }
}
```

#### `Switch`

Switch component.

React's root is a fragment (hidden native checkbox + label); the host
element is unwrapped by the DOM normalizer (`data-parity-unwrap`) and
`display: contents` keeps real layout fragment-like. Clicking the visible
button clicks the hidden checkbox, whose GENUINE native `change` event is
forwarded as the `change` output.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'mol-switch',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: ['label', 'checked', 'size', 'color', 'className', 'style', 'testId', 'disabled'],
  host: {
    'data-parity-unwrap': '',
    style: 'display: contents',
  },
  template: `<input
      type="checkbox"
      [attr.checked]="checked ? '' : null"
      [disabled]="disabled"
      aria-hidden="true"
      tabindex="-1"
      [class]="cm().srOnly"
      (change)="handleChange($event)"
      #hiddenInput
    />
    <label [class]="labelClasses()">
      <button
        type="button"
        role="switch"
        [attr.aria-checked]="checked ? 'true' : 'false'"
        [disabled]="disabled"
        [attr.data-state]="state()"
        (click)="handleClick($event)"
        [class]="classes()"
        [attr.style]="hostStyle()"
        [attr.data-testid]="testId"
        #trigger
      >
        <span [attr.data-state]="state()" [class]="cm().switchThumb({ size: size ?? 'md' })"></span>
      </button>
      <span [class]="cm().controlText" #labelSlot
        >{{ label }}<ng-content select="[slot=label]"></ng-content
      ></span>
    </label>`,
})
export class Switch implements AfterViewChecked {
  /** Switch label (text, or projected `[slot=label]` nodes). */
  label?: unknown
  /** Whether the switch is on. */
  checked?: boolean
  /** Switch size. */
  size?: Size
  /** Switch color. */
  color?: ColorVariant
  /** Extra classes joined onto the switch classes. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** `data-testid` for the button. */
  testId?: string
  /** Disables the switch. */
  disabled?: boolean

  /** The genuine native `change` event of the hidden checkbox. */
  @Output() readonly change = new EventEmitter<Event>()

  /** The hidden native checkbox. */
  @ViewChild('hiddenInput') hiddenInput?: ElementRef<HTMLInputElement>
  /** The visible trigger button (React's `...rest` spread target). */
  @ViewChild('trigger') trigger?: ElementRef<HTMLButtonElement>
  /** The label text wrapper. */
  @ViewChild('labelSlot') labelSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLSpanElement>

  private readonly host = inject(ElementRef)
  private hasLabel = false
  private readonly sync = new SlotWrapper(inject(ElementRef), inject(Renderer2))

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * The data-state of the switch and thumb.
   * @returns `checked` or `unchecked`.
   */
  state(): 'checked' | 'unchecked' {
    return this.checked ? 'checked' : 'unchecked'
  }

  /**
   * Computes the switch button class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().switchBase({ size: this.size ?? 'md', color: this.color }),
      this.className,
    )
  }

  /**
   * Computes the label class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  labelClasses(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().controlLabel, this.disabled && this.cm().controlDisabled)
  }

  /**
   * Serializes the inline style for the button.
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }

  /**
   * Clicking the button clicks the hidden checkbox (unless disabled or the
   * event was vetoed), exactly like React's handler.
   *
   * @param event - The button click event.
   */
  handleClick(event: Event): void {
    if (!this.disabled && !event.defaultPrevented) this.hiddenInput?.nativeElement.click()
  }

  /**
   * Forwards the hidden checkbox's genuine native `change` event (React
   * forwards the raw browser event, not a React synthetic one) and then
   * restores the controlled value, like React's controlled-input state
   * restore (the input mirrors the `checked` prop, never free user state).
   * The event's propagation stops here so the host-level `change` binding
   * does not record it a second time.
   *
   * @param event - The native change event.
   */
  handleChange(event: Event): void {
    event.stopPropagation()
    this.change.emit(event)
    const input = this.hiddenInput?.nativeElement
    if (input) input.checked = !!this.checked
  }

  /**
   * Moves rest attributes onto the button and applies React's
   * `{!!label && <span>}` label condition after queries resolve.
   */
  ngAfterViewChecked(): void {
    moveRestAttributes(this.host.nativeElement, this.trigger?.nativeElement)
    this.hasLabel = !!this.label || hasNodes(this.labelSlot?.nativeElement)
    this.sync.sync(
      this.labelSlot?.nativeElement,
      this.hasLabel,
      this.labelSlot?.nativeElement.parentElement ?? undefined,
    )
  }
}
```

#### `Table`

Table component.

The host is the wrapper `<div>` (`div[mol-table]`), exactly React's root;
the `<table>` carries the className/style/testId/rest. Column render
functions are templates (with `a0`–`a2` = the render function's arguments)
or plain functions returning text.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-table]',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [MolIconSvg, MolTemplateOutlet, Spinner],
  inputs: [
    'data',
    'columns',
    'rowKey',
    'bordered',
    'striped',
    'hoverable',
    'size',
    'emptyContent',
    'loading',
    'sort',
    'onSort',
    'onRowClick',
    'className',
    'style',
    'testId',
  ],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'cm().tableWrapper',
  },
  template: `<span #emptySlot><ng-content select="[slot=emptyContent]"></ng-content></span>
    @if (loading) {
      <div [class]="cm().tableLoadingOverlay"><div mol-spinner [size]="'lg'"></div></div>
    }
    <table #tableEl [class]="tableClasses()" [style]="tableStyle()" [attr.data-testid]="testId">
      <thead [class]="cm().tableHeader">
        <tr [class]="cm().tableRow">
          @for (column of columns; track column.key) {
            <th
              [class]="headClasses(column)"
              [style]="columnStyle(column)"
              (click)="handleSort(column)"
              [attr.aria-sort]="ariaSort(column)"
            >
              <div [class]="cm().tableSortWrapper">
                @if (asTemplate(column.header); as headerTpl) {
                  <ng-container [molTemplateOutlet]="headerTpl"></ng-container>
                } @else {
                  {{ nodeText(column.header) }}
                }
                @if (column.sortable && sort?.key === column.key) {
                  <span [class]="cm().tableSortIcon"><svg mol-icon [data]="sortIcon()" /></span>
                }
              </div>
            </th>
          }
        </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody [class]="cm().tableBody">
        @if (data.length === 0) {
          <tr>
            <td [attr.colspan]="columns.length" [class]="cm().tableEmptyCell" #emptyCell>
              @if (!hasEmpty()) {
                {{ emptyText() }}
              }
            </td>
          </tr>
        } @else {
          @for (row of data; track rowKeyOf(row, $index); let rowIdx = $index) {
            <tr [class]="rowClasses($index)" (click)="handleRowClick(row, $index)">
              @for (column of columns; track column.key) {
                <td [class]="cellClasses(column)">
                  @if (column.render) {
                    @if (asTemplate(column.render); as cellTpl) {
                      <ng-container
                        [molTemplateOutlet]="cellTpl"
                        [molTemplateOutletContext]="cellContext(row, column, rowIdx)"
                      ></ng-container>
                    } @else {
                      {{ renderText(row, column, $index) }}
                    }
                  } @else {
                    {{ cellText(row, column) }}
                  }
                </td>
              }
            </tr>
          }
        }
      </tbody>
    </table>`,
})
export class Table implements AfterViewInit, DoCheck {
  data: Record<string, unknown>[] = []
  columns: TableColumn<Record<string, unknown>>[] = []
  rowKey?: string | ((row: Record<string, unknown>) => string | number)
  bordered?: boolean
  striped?: boolean
  hoverable = true
  size?: string
  emptyContent?: unknown
  loading?: boolean
  sort?: { key: string; direction: 'asc' | 'desc' }
  onSort?: (key: string, direction: 'asc' | 'desc') => void
  onRowClick?: (row: Record<string, unknown>, index: number) => void
  className?: string
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  testId?: string

  @ViewChild('emptySlot') emptySlot?: ElementRef<HTMLSpanElement>
  @ViewChild('emptyCell') emptyCell?: ElementRef<HTMLTableCellElement>

  readonly hasEmpty = signal(false)

  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLElement> = inject(ElementRef)

  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  private readonly renderer = inject(Renderer2)
  private readonly sync = new SlotWrapper(this.host, this.renderer)
  private emptyParent: HTMLElement | null = null
  private adoptedEmpty: Node[] = []

  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  tableClasses(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().table, this.bordered && this.cm().tableBordered, this.className)
  }

  tableStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }

  headClasses(column: TableColumn<Record<string, unknown>>): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().tableHead,
      column.align === 'center' && this.cm().textCenter,
      column.align === 'right' && this.cm().textRight,
      column.sortable && this.cm().tableHeadSortable,
    )
  }

  columnStyle(column: TableColumn<Record<string, unknown>>): string | null {
    return styleText({ width: column.width })
  }

  cellClasses(column: TableColumn<Record<string, unknown>>): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().tableCell,
      column.align === 'center' && this.cm().textCenter,
      column.align === 'right' && this.cm().textRight,
    )
  }

  rowClasses(index: number): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().tableRow,
      this.striped && index % 2 === 1 && this.cm().tableRowStriped,
      this.hoverable && this.cm().tableRowHoverable,
      !!this.onRowClick && this.cm().tableRowClickable,
    )
  }

  ariaSort(column: TableColumn<Record<string, unknown>>): string | null {
    if (!column.sortable) return null
    if (this.sort?.key !== column.key) return 'none'
    return this.sort.direction === 'asc' ? 'ascending' : 'descending'
  }

  sortIcon(): ResolvedIcon {
    return resolveIcon(
      this.sort?.direction === 'asc' ? 'chevron-up' : 'chevron-down',
      this.cm().iconSm,
    )
  }

  emptyText(): string {
    return this.i18n.t('ui.table.empty', undefined, { defaultValue: 'No data available' })
  }

  nodeText(value: unknown): string {
    return typeof value === 'string' ? value : ''
  }

  asTemplate(value: unknown): TemplateRef<unknown> | null {
    return value instanceof TemplateRef ? value : null
  }

  rowKeyOf(row: Record<string, unknown>, index: number): string | number {
    if (typeof this.rowKey === 'function') return this.rowKey(row)
    if (this.rowKey && row[this.rowKey] !== undefined) return row[this.rowKey] as string | number
    return index
  }

  cellContext(
    row: Record<string, unknown>,
    column: TableColumn<Record<string, unknown>>,
    index: number,
  ): Record<string, unknown> {
    const value = row[column.key as string]
    return { $implicit: value, a0: value, a1: row, a2: index }
  }

  renderText(
    row: Record<string, unknown>,
    column: TableColumn<Record<string, unknown>>,
    index: number,
  ): string {
    const render = column.render as unknown
    if (typeof render !== 'function') return ''
    const value = row[column.key as string]
    const result = (render as (v: unknown, r: unknown, i: number) => unknown)(value, row, index)
    return typeof result === 'string' ? result : ''
  }

  cellText(row: Record<string, unknown>, column: TableColumn<Record<string, unknown>>): string {
    const value = row[column.key as string]
    return value === undefined || value === null ? '' : String(value)
  }

  handleSort(column: TableColumn<Record<string, unknown>>): void {
    if (!column.sortable || !this.onSort) return
    const key = column.key as string
    const currentDirection = this.sort?.key === key ? this.sort.direction : undefined
    this.onSort(key, currentDirection === 'asc' ? 'desc' : 'asc')
  }

  handleRowClick(row: Record<string, unknown>, index: number): void {
    this.onRowClick?.(row, index)
  }

  ngAfterViewInit(): void {
    const slot = this.emptySlot?.nativeElement
    this.emptyParent = slot?.parentElement ?? null
    const present = hasNodes(slot)
    this.hasEmpty.set(present)
    const cell = this.emptyCell?.nativeElement
    if (slot && cell && present) {
      this.adoptedEmpty = Array.from(slot.childNodes)
      for (const node of this.adoptedEmpty) cell.appendChild(node)
    }
    this.sync.sync(slot, false, this.emptyParent ?? undefined)
  }
```

#### `Tabs`

Tabs component.

The host is the root `<div>` (`div[mol-tabs]`). Implements the WAI-ARIA APG
"automatic activation" tabs pattern: roving tabindex, Left/Right/Home/End
move both focus and the active tab, and disabled tabs are skipped. Item
`icon` and `content` are node values (templates); `label` is a string or a
node value.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-tabs]',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [MolTemplateOutlet],
  inputs: [
    'items',
    'value',
    'defaultValue',
    'variant',
    'size',
    'fitted',
    'className',
    'style',
    'testId',
  ],
  outputs: ['change'],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'className',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
  },
  template: `<div [class]="listClasses()" role="tablist" (keydown)="handleKeyDown($event)">
      @for (item of items; track item.value) {
        <button
          #tabBtn
          [id]="tabId(item.value)"
          type="button"
          role="tab"
          [attr.aria-selected]="item.value === activeValue() ? 'true' : 'false'"
          [attr.aria-controls]="panelId(item.value)"
          [attr.data-state]="item.value === activeValue() ? 'active' : 'inactive'"
          [attr.tabindex]="item.value === activeValue() ? 0 : -1"
          [disabled]="!!item.disabled"
          (click)="selectTab(item.value)"
          [class]="triggerClasses()"
        >
          @if (item.icon) {
            <span [class]="cm().tabTriggerIcon">
              @if (asTemplate(item.icon); as iconTpl) {
                <ng-container [molTemplateOutlet]="iconTpl"></ng-container>
              } @else {
                {{ nodeText(item.icon) }}
              }
            </span>
          }
          @if (asTemplate(item.label); as labelTpl) {
            <ng-container [molTemplateOutlet]="labelTpl"></ng-container>
          } @else {
            {{ nodeText(item.label) }}
          }
        </button>
      }
    </div>
    @if (hasContent()) {
      <div
        role="tabpanel"
        [id]="panelId(activeValue() ?? '')"
        [attr.aria-labelledby]="tabId(activeValue() ?? '')"
        [class]="contentClasses()"
      >
        @if (activeContent(); as contentTpl) {
          <ng-container [molTemplateOutlet]="contentTpl"></ng-container>
        } @else {
          {{ contentText() }}
        }
      </div>
    }`,
})
export class Tabs implements OnInit {
  /** The tabs. */
  items: TabItem<string>[] = []
  /** Controlled active value. */
  value?: string
  /** Initial active value for the uncontrolled form. */
  defaultValue?: string
  /** Tab-list visual variant. */
  variant?: 'line' | 'enclosed' | 'soft-rounded' | 'solid-rounded'
  /** Tab size. */
  size?: 'xs' | 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg' | 'xl'
  /** Whether tabs stretch to fill the list. */
  fitted?: boolean
  /** Extra classes for the root. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** `data-testid`. */
  testId?: string

  /** Fires with the selected tab's value (React's `onChange`). */
  @Output() readonly change = new EventEmitter<string>()

  /** The tab buttons (for focus movement). */
  @ViewChildren('tabBtn') tabButtons?: QueryList<ElementRef<HTMLButtonElement>>
  /** The per-instance id base (the normalizer renumbers ids). */
  readonly idBase = nextInstanceId()
  /** The uncontrolled active value. */
  private readonly internalValue = signal<string | undefined>(undefined)

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Seeds the uncontrolled value once, at mount, like React's
   * `useState(defaultValue || items[0]?.value)`: reordering the tabs later keeps
   * the active tab rather than activating whichever tab is now first.
   */
  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.internalValue.set(this.defaultValue || this.items[0]?.value)
  }

  /**
   * The active tab value: the controlled `value`, else the uncontrolled one.
   * @returns The active value.
   */
  activeValue(): string | undefined {
    if (this.value !== undefined) return this.value
    return this.internalValue()
  }

  /**
   * The tab button id for a value.
   * @param value - The tab value.
   * @returns The id.
   */
  tabId(value: string): string {
    return `${this.idBase}-tab-${value}`
  }

  /**
   * The tabpanel id for a value.
   * @param value - The tab value.
   * @returns The id.
   */
  panelId(value: string): string {
    return `${this.idBase}-tabpanel-${value}`
  }

  /**
   * The tab-list class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  listClasses(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().tabsList({ variant: this.variant, size: this.size }),
      this.fitted && this.cm().tabsFitted,
    )
  }

  /**
   * The tab-button class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  triggerClasses(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().tabsTrigger({ variant: this.variant, size: this.size }),
      this.fitted && this.cm().tabTriggerFitted,
    )
  }

  /**
   * The tabpanel class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  contentClasses(): string {
    return this.cm().tabsContent({ variant: this.variant, size: this.size })
  }

  /**
   * Serializes the root's inline style.
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }

  /**
   * Narrows a node-valued prop to a TemplateRef.
   *
   * @param value - The prop value.
   * @returns The template, or null.
   */
  asTemplate(value: unknown): TemplateRef<unknown> | null {
    return value instanceof TemplateRef ? value : null
  }

  /**
   * A plain-string node value's text.
   *
   * @param value - The prop value.
   * @returns The text, or empty.
   */
  nodeText(value: unknown): string {
    return typeof value === 'string' ? value : ''
  }

  /**
   * Whether the active tab has content (React renders no panel without it).
   * @returns True when content is given.
   */
  hasContent(): boolean {
    const active = this.items.find((item) => item.value === this.activeValue())
    return !!active?.content
  }

  /**
   * The active tab's content as a template.
   * @returns The template, or null for a plain string.
   */
  activeContent(): TemplateRef<unknown> | null {
    const active = this.items.find((item) => item.value === this.activeValue())
    return active ? this.asTemplate(active.content) : null
  }

  /**
   * The active tab's plain-string content.
   * @returns The text, or empty.
   */
  contentText(): string {
    const active = this.items.find((item) => item.value === this.activeValue())
    return this.nodeText(active?.content)
  }

  /**
   * Selects a tab and notifies the caller (React's `selectTab`).
   * @param tabValue - The tab value.
   */
  selectTab(tabValue: string): void {
    if (this.value === undefined) this.internalValue.set(tabValue)
    this.change.emit(tabValue)
  }

  /**
   * The APG keyboard model: Left/Right/Home/End move focus AND selection.
   * @param event - The key event.
   */
  handleKeyDown(event: KeyboardEvent): void {
    const enabled = this.items.filter((item) => !item.disabled)
    if (enabled.length === 0) return
    const active = this.activeValue()
    const currentIndex = enabled.findIndex((item) => item.value === active)
    let nextIndex: number
    switch (event.key) {
      case 'ArrowRight':
        nextIndex = currentIndex === -1 ? 0 : (currentIndex + 1) % enabled.length
        break
      case 'ArrowLeft':
        nextIndex = currentIndex === -1 ? 0 : (currentIndex - 1 + enabled.length) % enabled.length
        break
      case 'Home':
        nextIndex = 0
        break
      case 'End':
        nextIndex = enabled.length - 1
        break
      default:
        return
    }
    event.preventDefault()
    const nextValue = enabled[nextIndex].value
    this.selectTab(nextValue)
    this.focusTab(nextValue)
  }

  /**
   * Moves focus to the tab with the given value (React's `tabRefs.focus()`).
   * @param value - The tab value.
   */
  private focusTab(value: string): void {
    const match = this.tabButtons?.find((ref) => ref.nativeElement.id === this.tabId(value))
    match?.nativeElement.focus()
  }
}
```

#### `Textarea`

Textarea component.

The host is the `div.inputWrapper` React renders. React's textarea
`onChange` fires on `input` events; a controlled `value` is restored after
user input exactly like React's controlled-input state restore.
`autoResize` recomputes the inline height after every check and on real
`input` events (matching React's effect + native listener pair).

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-textarea]',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: [
    'size',
    'label',
    'error',
    'hint',
    'autoResize',
    'minRows',
    'maxRows',
    'className',
    'style',
    'testId',
    'disabled',
    'required',
    'value',
    'defaultValue',
    'placeholder',
    'name',
    'id',
    'rows',
  ],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'cm().inputWrapper',
  },
  template: `@if (label) {
      <label
        [attr.for]="textareaId()"
        [class]="cm().cn(cm().label({ required: !!required }), cm().labelBlock)"
        >{{ label }}</label
      >
    }
    <textarea
      [attr.id]="textareaId()"
      [attr.name]="name"
      [attr.placeholder]="placeholder"
      [disabled]="disabled"
      [attr.required]="required ? '' : null"
      [attr.rows]="rows || minRows || 3"
      [attr.aria-invalid]="error || fieldContext()?.invalid ? 'true' : 'false'"
      [attr.aria-describedby]="mergedDescribedBy()"
      [class]="classes()"
      [attr.style]="hostStyle()"
      [attr.data-testid]="testId"
      (input)="handleInput($event)"
      (change)="handleChange($event)"
      #textareaEl
    ></textarea>
    @if (error) {
      <p [attr.id]="textareaId() + '-error'" [class]="cm().formError">{{ error }}</p>
    }
    @if (hint && !error) {
      <p [attr.id]="textareaId() + '-hint'" [class]="cm().formHint">{{ hint }}</p>
    }`,
})
export class Textarea implements AfterViewInit, AfterViewChecked {
  /** Textarea size. */
  size?: Size
  /** Field label. */
  label?: string
  /** Error message (associates via aria-describedby). */
  error?: string
  /** Hint text (shown without an error). */
  hint?: string
  /** Whether the box resizes to its content. */
  autoResize?: boolean
  /** Minimum rendered rows (autoResize floor). */
  minRows?: number
  /** Maximum rendered rows (autoResize ceiling). */
  maxRows?: number
  /** Extra classes joined onto the textarea classes. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** `data-testid` for the textarea. */
  testId?: string
  /** Disables the textarea. */
  disabled?: boolean
  /** Marks the textarea required (label styling). */
  required?: boolean
  /** Controlled value. */
  value?: string
  /** Uncontrolled initial value. */
  defaultValue?: string
  /** Placeholder text. */
  placeholder?: string
  /** Form field name. */
  name?: string
  /** Explicit textarea id. */
  id?: string
  /** Explicit rows attribute. */
  rows?: number

  /** Fires the raw `input` event (React's textarea onChange). */
  @Output() readonly change = new EventEmitter<Event>()

  /** The native textarea (React's `...rest` spread target). */
  @ViewChild('textareaEl') textareaEl?: ElementRef<HTMLTextAreaElement>

  private readonly host = inject(ElementRef)
  /** The `aria-describedby` the caller spread onto the host (React's rest). */

  /** Whether the rest-describedby value was captured. */

  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /** The textarea id (`id`, `name`, or a stable generated id; the DOM normalizer renumbers generated ids in order of appearance, like React's `useId`). */
  textareaId(): string {

  }

  describedBy(): string | null {

    if (this.hint) return `${this.textareaId()}-hint`

  mergedDescribedBy(): string | null {
    const target = this.fieldContext()?.describedBy
    if (target) return [this.restDescribedBy, target].filter(Boolean).join(' ')
    return this.restDescribedBy ?? this.describedBy()
  }

  /** Computes the textarea class list. */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().textarea({ error: !!this.error, size: this.size }),
      this.className,
    )
  }

  /** Serializes the inline style for the textarea: `autoResize` neutralizes the bond's min-height class (`minHeight: 0`, `resize: none`) and the caller's style stil… */

  /** React's textarea onChange: the `input` event, with the controlled value restored afterwards and the autoResize height recomputed (React listens for native `inp… */
  handleInput(event: Event): void {

    event.stopPropagation()
  }

  /** Sizes the box to its content: React's `resize()` — height `auto`, then clamp `scrollHeight` between `minRows * lineHeight` and `maxRows * lineHeight` (`lineHei… */
  resize(): void {
    const element = this.textareaEl?.nativeElement
    if (!this.autoResize || !element) return
    element.style.height = 'auto'
    const lineHeight = parseInt(window.getComputedStyle(element).lineHeight) || 20
    const minHeight = (this.minRows ?? 3) * lineHeight
    const maxHeight = this.maxRows ? this.maxRows * lineHeight : Infinity

  /** Re-applies a controlled value and recomputes the autoResize height after every check (React re-runs the value effect whenever `value` changes; running here cov… */
  ngAfterViewChecked(): void {
    this.applyValue(false)
    this.resize()
  }

  /** Writes the value to the textarea's value property: the controlled `value` every check, the `defaultValue` only at mount (uncontrolled typing afterwards is the… */
  private applyValue(initial: boolean): void {
    const textarea = this.textareaEl?.nativeElement

      const next = String(this.value)

    } else if (initial && this.defaultValue !== undefined && textarea.value === '') {

}
```

#### `ThemeToggle`

ThemeToggle component.

Button that flips the wired theme bond between light and dark. The host is
the `<button>` itself (`button[mol-theme-toggle]`), matching React's root
exactly. Ships React's conventions out of the box: `data-mol-id`,
`data-mode`, `aria-pressed` and the `theme.toggle` i18n `aria-label`.
Extra attributes (`data-*`, `title`, …) land on the host — React's spread
target — and unknown props like `disabled` bind the host attribute
directly, so the browser suppresses the click exactly as React does.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'button[mol-theme-toggle]',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [Icon],
  inputs: ['ariaLabelKey', 'ariaLabelDefault', 'darkIcon', 'lightIcon', 'iconSize', 'className'],
  host: {
    type: 'button',
    'data-mol-id': 'theme-toggle',
    '[attr.data-mode]': 'mode()',
    '[attr.aria-pressed]': 'mode() === "dark" ? "true" : "false"',
    '[attr.aria-label]': 'labelText()',
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '(click)': 'theme.toggleTheme()',
  },
  template: `@if (mode() === 'dark') {
      <svg mol-icon-root [name]="darkIcon ?? 'moon'" [size]="iconSizePx()"></svg>
    } @else {
      <svg mol-icon-root [name]="lightIcon ?? 'sun'" [size]="iconSizePx()"></svg>
    }`,
})
export class ThemeToggle {
  /** i18n key for the `aria-label` (default `theme.toggle`). */
  ariaLabelKey?: string
  /** Fallback `aria-label` if the i18n key is missing. */
  ariaLabelDefault?: string
  /** Icon name rendered in dark mode (default `moon`). */
  darkIcon?: IconName
  /** Icon name rendered in light mode (default `sun`). */
  lightIcon?: IconName
  /** Pixel size for the rendered icon (default 20). */
  iconSize?: number
  /** Extra classes joined onto the toggle classes. */
  className?: string

  /** Theme state and actions, read in the component's injection context. */
  readonly theme = useTheme()

  /**
   * Reactive translation, read in the injection context. Its `t` reads the
   * locale, so the view re-renders the text on a locale switch (the static `t`
   * does not).
   */
  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  /**
   * The current theme mode.
   * @returns `'light'` or `'dark'`.
   */
  mode(): 'light' | 'dark' {
    return this.theme.mode()
  }

  /**
   * The localized `aria-label`.
   * @returns The translated label text.
   */
  labelText(): string {
    return this.i18n.t(
      this.ariaLabelKey ?? 'theme.toggle',
      {},
      {
        defaultValue: this.ariaLabelDefault ?? 'Toggle theme',
      },
    )
  }

  /**
   * Computes the toggle class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    const cm: UIClassMap = getClassMap()
    return cm.cn(cm.themeToggleButton, this.className)
  }

  /**
   * The rendered icon pixel size.
   * @returns The size (default 20 when a later-bound write left it unset).
   */
  iconSizePx(): number {
    return this.iconSize ?? 20
  }
}
```

#### `Toast`

Toast component.

The host is the toast `<div>` (`div[mol-toast]`), exactly React's root. The
auto-dismiss countdown pauses on hover AND focus (WCAG 2.2.1) and resumes
with the remaining time; the announced role follows `status`
(`warning`/`error` assertive, everything else polite).

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-toast]',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [MolIconSvg, MolTemplateOutlet],
  inputs: [
    'title',
    'description',
    'status',
    'duration',
    'dismissible',
    'closeLabel',
    'className',
    'style',
    'testId',
    'position',
  ],
  outputs: ['dismiss'],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
    '[attr.role]': 'role()',
    '[attr.data-state]': 'visible() ? "open" : null',
    '(mouseenter)': 'pauseTimer()',
    '(mouseleave)': 'resumeTimer()',
    '(focusin)': 'pauseTimer()',
    '(focusout)': 'resumeTimer()',
  },
  template: `@if (statusIcon(); as icon) {
      <span [class]="cm().toastIconWrapper"><svg mol-icon [data]="icon" /></span>
    }
    <div [class]="cm().toastContentWrapper">
      @if (title) {
        <div [class]="cm().toastTitle">{{ title }}</div>
      }
      <div #descriptionSlot [class]="cm().toastDescription">
        @if (descriptionText(); as descriptionTextValue) {
          {{ descriptionTextValue }}
        }
        <ng-container [molTemplateOutlet]="descriptionTemplate()"></ng-container
        ><span #childrenSlot><ng-content></ng-content></span>
      </div>
    </div>
    @if (dismissible) {
      <button
        type="button"
        [class]="cm().toastClose"
        [attr.aria-label]="closeText()"
        (click)="handleDismiss()"
      >
        <svg mol-icon [data]="closeIcon()" />
      </button>
    }`,
})
export class Toast implements AfterViewInit, DoCheck, OnDestroy {
  title?: string
  description?: unknown
  status: ColorVariant = 'info'
  duration = 5000
  dismissible = true
  closeLabel?: string
  className?: string
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  testId?: string
  position?: ToastPosition

  @Output() readonly dismiss = new EventEmitter<void>()

  @ViewChild('descriptionSlot') descriptionSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>
  @ViewChild('childrenSlot') childrenSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLSpanElement>

  readonly visible = signal(true)

  private readonly el = inject(ElementRef)

  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  private readonly renderer = inject(Renderer2)
  private readonly host: HTMLElement = this.el.nativeElement
  private readonly sync = new SlotWrapper(this.el, this.renderer)
  private remaining: number
  private startedAt: number | null = null
  private timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined
  private lastDuration: number
  private descriptionParent: HTMLElement | null = null
  private childrenParent: HTMLElement | null = null
  private hasChildren = false
  private adoptedChildren: Node[] = []
  private detachedParent: Node | null = null
  private detachedAnchor: Node | null = null

  constructor() {
    this.remaining = this.duration
    this.lastDuration = this.duration
  }

  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  variant(): 'default' | 'success' | 'warning' | 'error' | 'info' {
    return STATUS_VARIANT[this.status] || 'default'
  }

  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().toast({ variant: this.variant() }), this.className)
  }

  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }

  role(): 'alert' | 'status' {
    return this.variant() === 'warning' || this.variant() === 'error' ? 'alert' : 'status'
  }

  statusIcon(): ResolvedIcon | null {
    const name = STATUS_ICON[this.variant()]
    return name ? resolveIcon(name, this.cm().iconMd) : null
  }

  closeIcon(): ResolvedIcon {
    return resolveIcon('x-mark', this.cm().iconSm)
  }

  descriptionText(): string | null {
    return typeof this.description === 'string' ? this.description : null
  }

  descriptionTemplate(): TemplateRef<unknown> | null {
    return this.description instanceof TemplateRef ? this.description : null
  }

  closeText(): string {
    return this.closeLabel ?? this.i18n.t('ui.toast.close', undefined, { defaultValue: 'Close' })
  }

  handleDismiss(): void {
    this.visible.set(false)
    this.dismiss.emit()
  }

  pauseTimer(): void {
    if (this.startedAt === null) return
    const elapsed = Date.now() - this.startedAt
    this.remaining = Math.max(0, this.remaining - elapsed)
    this.clearTimer()
  }

  resumeTimer(): void {
    if (this.remaining > 0) this.startTimer(this.remaining)
  }

  ngAfterViewInit(): void {
    this.descriptionParent = this.descriptionSlot?.nativeElement.parentElement ?? null
    this.childrenParent = this.childrenSlot?.nativeElement.parentElement ?? null
    this.hasChildren = hasNodes(this.childrenSlot?.nativeElement)
    this.adoptChildren()
    this.restart()
  }

  ngDoCheck(): void {
    if (this.duration !== this.lastDuration) {
      this.lastDuration = this.duration
      this.restart()
    }
    this.syncDescription()
    this.syncHostVisibility()
  }

  ngOnDestroy(): void {
    this.clearTimer()
    this.adoptedChildren = []
    if (!this.host.isConnected && this.detachedParent) {
      this.detachedParent.insertBefore(this.host, this.detachedAnchor)
      this.detachedParent = null
      this.detachedAnchor = null
    }
  }

  private restart(): void {
    this.remaining = this.duration
    this.startTimer(this.duration)
  }

  private clearTimer(): void {
    if (this.timer) {
      clearTimeout(this.timer)
      this.timer = undefined
    }
    this.startedAt = null
  }

  private startTimer(ms: number): void {
    this.clearTimer()
    if (ms <= 0) return
    this.startedAt = Date.now()
    this.timer = setTimeout(() => {
      this.visible.set(false)
      this.dismiss.emit()
    }, ms)
  }

  private adoptChildren(): void {
    const span = this.childrenSlot?.nativeElement
    if (!span) return
    const wrapper = this.descriptionSlot?.nativeElement
    const descWins = this.descriptionText() !== null || this.descriptionTemplate() !== null
    if (wrapper && !descWins && this.hasChildren) {
      this.adoptedChildren = Array.from(span.childNodes)
      for (const node of this.adoptedChildren) wrapper.appendChild(node)
    }
    this.sync.sync(span, false, this.childrenParent ?? undefined)
    this.syncDescription()
  }

  private syncHostVisibility(): void {
    const host = this.host
    if (!this.visible()) {
      if (host.isConnected) {
        this.detachedParent = host.parentNode
        this.detachedAnchor = host.nextSibling
        host.parentNode?.removeChild(host)
      }
    } else if (this.detachedParent && !host.isConnected) {
      this.detachedParent.insertBefore(host, this.detachedAnchor)
      this.detachedParent = null
      this.detachedAnchor = null
    }
  }

  private syncDescription(): void {
    const wrapper = this.descriptionSlot?.nativeElement
    if (!wrapper) return
    const hasDescription = this.descriptionText() !== null || this.descriptionTemplate() !== null
    this.sync.sync(wrapper, hasDescription || this.hasChildren, this.descriptionParent ?? undefined)
  }
}
```
