#### `ToastContainer`

Toast container: positions toasts on screen via a body portal.

React's root is a portal, so the host is unwrapped
(`data-parity-unwrap`, `display: contents`) and the container `<div>` is
moved into `document.body`.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'mol-toast-container',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: ['position', 'className'],
  host: {
    'data-parity-unwrap': '',
    style: 'display: contents',
  },
  template: `<div #container [class]="classes()"><ng-content></ng-content></div>`,
})
export class ToastContainer implements AfterViewInit, OnDestroy {
  /** The container position (default `bottom-right`). */
  position: ToastPosition = 'bottom-right'
  /** Extra classes. */
  className?: string

  /** The container element (moved to `document.body`). */
  @ViewChild('container') container?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>

  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLElement> = inject(ElementRef)

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * The container class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().toastContainer({ position: this.position }), this.className)
  }

  /** Moves the container to `document.body` (React's portal). */
  ngAfterViewInit(): void {
    const el = this.container?.nativeElement
    if (el && typeof document !== 'undefined') document.body.appendChild(el)
  }

  /** Moves the container back under the host so Angular can remove it. */
  ngOnDestroy(): void {
    const el = this.container?.nativeElement
    if (el && this.host.nativeElement.parentNode) this.host.nativeElement.appendChild(el)
  }
}
```

#### `ToastProvider`

ToastProvider: manages global toast state and renders children plus a
positioned toast container (React's context provider + portal).

The context value is registered on the host element (React context flows
through the rendered tree, which Angular DI cannot reach for projected
content); `useToast()` reads it through the DOM ancestors.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'mol-toast-provider',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [Toast],
  inputs: ['position'],
  host: {
    'data-parity-unwrap': '',
    style: 'display: contents',
  },
  template: `<span #childrenCapture><ng-content></ng-content></span>
    <div #container [class]="containerClasses()">
      @for (toast of toasts(); track toast.id) {
        <div
          mol-toast
          [title]="toast.title"
          [description]="toast.description"
          [status]="toast.status ?? 'info'"
          [duration]="toast.duration ?? 5000"
          [dismissible]="toast.dismissible ?? true"
          [closeLabel]="toast.closeLabel"
          [className]="toast.className"
          [style]="toast.style"
          [testId]="toast.testId"
          (dismiss)="removeToast(toast.id)"
        ></div>
      }
    </div>`,
})
export class ToastProvider implements AfterViewInit, OnDestroy {
  /** The default position for toasts (default `bottom-right`). */
  position: ToastPosition = 'bottom-right'

  /** The children capture point (removed once children are adopted). */
  @ViewChild('childrenCapture') childrenCapture?: ElementRef<HTMLSpanElement>
  /** The toast container (moved to `document.body`). */
  @ViewChild('container') container?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>

  /** The current toasts. */
  readonly toasts = signal<(ToastProps & { id: string })[]>([])

  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLElement> = inject(ElementRef)
  private readonly renderer = inject(Renderer2)
  private readonly sync = new SlotWrapper(this.host, this.renderer)
  private adopted: Node[] = []

  /** Registers the toast context on the host (before any projected child resolves it). */
  constructor() {
    provideDomContext(this.host.nativeElement, new ToastContextImplementation(this))
  }

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * The container class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  containerClasses(): string {
    return this.cm().toastContainer({ position: this.position })
  }

  /**
   * Adds a toast and returns its generated id (React's `addToast`).
   * @param toast - The toast to add.
   * @returns The id.
   */
  addToast(toast: Omit<ToastProps, 'onDismiss'>): string {
    const id = `toast-${++toastCounter}`
    this.toasts.update((prev) => [...prev, { ...toast, id }])
    return id
  }

  /**
   * Removes a toast by id (React's `removeToast`).
   * @param id - The toast's id.
   */
  removeToast(id: string): void {
    this.toasts.update((prev) => prev.filter((toast) => toast.id !== id))
  }

  /**
   * Adopts the children bare into the host and moves the container to
   * `document.body` (React's portal).
   */
  ngAfterViewInit(): void {
    const capture = this.childrenCapture?.nativeElement
    const host = this.host.nativeElement
    if (capture) {
      this.adopted = Array.from(capture.childNodes)
      for (const node of this.adopted) host.appendChild(node)
      this.sync.sync(capture, false, host)
    }
    const container = this.container?.nativeElement
    if (container && typeof document !== 'undefined') document.body.appendChild(container)
  }

  /** Removes the context registration and restores the container (unmount). */
  ngOnDestroy(): void {
    removeDomContext(this.host.nativeElement)
    const container = this.container?.nativeElement
    if (container && this.host.nativeElement.parentNode)
      this.host.nativeElement.appendChild(container)
    this.adopted = []
  }
}
```

#### `Tooltip`

Tooltip component.

React's root is a FRAGMENT (a trigger wrapper `<div>` plus a portal), so
the host is unwrapped (`data-parity-unwrap`, `display: contents`); the
wrapper is adopted beside the host (so the rendered tree and focus paths
match React's) and the portal element is moved into `document.body` while
visible. A single-element trigger receives `aria-describedby` while the
tooltip shows; show/hide are coordinated across instances exactly like
React's module-level `activeTooltips` map.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'mol-tooltip',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: ['content', 'placement', 'delay', 'hasArrow', 'className', 'style', 'testId'],
  host: {
    'data-parity-unwrap': '',
    style: 'display: contents',
  },
  template: `<div
      #triggerWrap
      [class]="cm().tooltipTrigger"
      (mouseover)="show()"
      (mouseout)="scheduleHide()"
      (focusin)="handleFocus($event)"
      (focusout)="hide()"
      (keydown)="handleKeyDown($event)"
      (click)="hide()"
    >
      <span #childrenCapture><ng-content></ng-content></span>
    </div>
    <div #portalWrap [style]="portalStyleText()">
      <div
        #contentEl
        role="tooltip"
        [id]="tooltipId"
        [class]="contentClasses()"
        [style]="contentStyleText()"
        [attr.data-testid]="testId"
        (mouseover)="keepVisible()"
        (mouseout)="scheduleHide()"
      >
        <ng-content select="[slot=content]"></ng-content>
      </div>
      @if (hasArrow) {
        <span aria-hidden="true" [style]="arrowStyleText()"></span>
      }
    </div>`,
})
export class Tooltip implements AfterViewInit, DoCheck, OnDestroy {
  content?: unknown
  placement: TooltipPlacement = 'top'
  delay = 0
  hasArrow = false
  className?: string
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  testId?: string

  readonly tooltipId = nextInstanceId()
  readonly visible = signal(false)
  private readonly position = signal<Position>({ top: 0, left: 0 })

  @ViewChild('triggerWrap') triggerWrap?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>
  @ViewChild('childrenCapture') childrenCapture?: ElementRef<HTMLSpanElement>
  @ViewChild('portalWrap') portalWrap?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>
  @ViewChild('contentEl') contentEl?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>

  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLElement> = inject(ElementRef)
  private readonly cdr = inject(ChangeDetectorRef)
  private showTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined
  private hideTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined
  private readonly hideListener = (): void => this.hide()
  private readonly escapeListener = (event: KeyboardEvent): void => {
    if (event.key === 'Escape') this.hide()
  }
  private escapeBound = false
  private attached = false
  private lastVisible = false
  private associateEl: HTMLElement | null = null
  private hostParent: Node | null = null

  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  contentClasses(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().tooltipContent, this.className)
  }

  contentStyleText(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }

  portalStyleText(): string | null {
    return styleText({
      position: 'absolute',
      top: this.position().top,
      left: this.position().left,
      zIndex: 9999,
    })
  }

  arrowStyleText(): string | null {
    return styleText(arrowStyle(this.placement))
  }

  show(): void {
    if (typeof document !== 'undefined') {
      const previous = activeTooltips.get(document)
      if (previous !== this.hideListener) previous?.()
      activeTooltips.set(document, this.hideListener)
    }
    clearTimeout(this.hideTimer)
    clearTimeout(this.showTimer)
    if (this.delay > 0) {
      this.showTimer = setTimeout(() => {
        this.visible.set(true)
        this.cdr.markForCheck()
      }, this.delay)
    } else {
      this.visible.set(true)
      this.cdr.markForCheck()
    }
  }

  hide(): void {
    clearTimeout(this.showTimer)
    clearTimeout(this.hideTimer)
    if (typeof document !== 'undefined' && activeTooltips.get(document) === this.hideListener) {
      activeTooltips.delete(document)
    }
    this.visible.set(false)
    this.cdr.markForCheck()
  }

  scheduleHide(): void {
    clearTimeout(this.showTimer)
    clearTimeout(this.hideTimer)
    this.hideTimer = setTimeout(() => this.hide(), 150)
  }

  keepVisible(): void {
    clearTimeout(this.hideTimer)
  }

  handleFocus(event: FocusEvent): void {
    const target = event.target as HTMLElement
    try {
      if (typeof target.matches === 'function' && target.matches(':focus-visible')) this.show()
    } catch (_error) {}
  }

  handleKeyDown(event: KeyboardEvent): void {
    if (event.key === 'Escape') this.hide()
  }

  ngAfterViewInit(): void {
    const wrap = this.triggerWrap?.nativeElement
    const host = this.host.nativeElement
    const capture = this.childrenCapture?.nativeElement
    this.hostParent = host.parentNode
    if (wrap) {
      this.hostParent?.insertBefore(wrap, host)
    }
    if (capture && wrap) {
      const children = Array.from(capture.childNodes)
      for (const node of children) wrap.appendChild(node)
      const elements = children.filter((node) => node.nodeType === 1)
      const hasText = children.some(
        (node) => node.nodeType === 3 && (node.textContent ?? '').trim(),
      )
      this.associateEl = elements.length === 1 && !hasText ? (elements[0] as HTMLElement) : null
      capture.remove()
    }
    this.portalWrap?.nativeElement.remove()
    this.attached = false
    this.lastVisible = false
  }

  ngDoCheck(): void {
    const visible = this.visible()
    if (visible === this.lastVisible) return
    this.lastVisible = visible
    if (visible) this.attachPortal()
    else this.detachPortal()
  }

  ngOnDestroy(): void {
    clearTimeout(this.showTimer)
    clearTimeout(this.hideTimer)
    if (typeof document !== 'undefined' && activeTooltips.get(document) === this.hideListener) {
      activeTooltips.delete(document)
    }
    if (this.escapeBound) document.removeEventListener('keydown', this.escapeListener)
    const host = this.host.nativeElement
    const wrap = this.triggerWrap?.nativeElement
    if (wrap && wrap.parentElement !== host) host.appendChild(wrap)
    const portal = this.portalWrap?.nativeElement
    if (portal && portal.parentElement !== host) host.appendChild(portal)
    this.associateEl = null
  }

  private attachPortal(): void {
    const portal = this.portalWrap?.nativeElement
    if (!portal) return
    document.body.appendChild(portal)
    this.attached = true
    this.updatePosition()
    this.associateEl?.setAttribute('aria-describedby', this.tooltipId)
    if (!this.escapeBound) {
      document.addEventListener('keydown', this.escapeListener)
      this.escapeBound = true
    }
  }

  private detachPortal(): void {
    this.portalWrap?.nativeElement.remove()
    this.attached = false
    this.associateEl?.removeAttribute('aria-describedby')
    if (this.escapeBound) {
      document.removeEventListener('keydown', this.escapeListener)
      this.escapeBound = false
    }
  }

  private updatePosition(): void {
    if (typeof window === 'undefined' || typeof document === 'undefined') return
    const trigger = this.triggerWrap?.nativeElement
    const tooltip = this.contentEl?.nativeElement
    if (!trigger || !tooltip) return
    this.position.set(
      calculatePosition(
        trigger.getBoundingClientRect(),
        tooltip.getBoundingClientRect(),
        this.placement,
      ),
    )
  }
}
```

_(Member comments are left out to fit; the full declaration is in `node_modules/@molecule/app-ui-angular/dist/components/tooltip.component.d.ts`.)_

#### `UserMenuPopover`

UserMenuPopover root: owns the open state and the auto-dismiss behavior
(route change, outside click, Escape) and provides the identity context.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-user-menu-popover]',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: ['guestName', 'className'],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'classes()',
  },
  template: `<ng-content></ng-content>`,
})
export class UserMenuPopover implements AfterViewInit, DoCheck, OnDestroy {
  /** Label shown when there is no authenticated user (default `Account`). */
  guestName?: string
  /** Extra classes composed onto the relative-positioned container. */
  className?: string

  /** Whether the popover is open. */
  readonly isOpen = signal(false)
  /** The popover's shared id (the normalizer renumbers it). */
  readonly panelId = nextInstanceId()
  /** Auth state (identity defaults). */
  readonly auth = useAuth<AccountUser>()
  /**
   * Translation state. Its `t` reads the locale, so the views that render it
   * re-render on a locale switch (the static `t` does not follow a switch).
   */
  readonly i18n = useTranslation()
  /** Router state: a route change closes the popover. */
  readonly router = useRouter()

  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLElement> = inject(ElementRef)
  private readonly cdr = inject(ChangeDetectorRef)
  private listeners: (() => void)[] = []
  private openListener = false
  private lastOpen = false
  private lastRoute: string | null = null
  /** Closes the popover on any route change (React's location effect). */
  private readonly routeEffect = effect(() => {
    const location = this.router.location()
    const key = `${location.pathname}${location.search}${(location as { hash?: string }).hash ?? ''}`
    if (this.lastRoute === null) {
      this.lastRoute = key
      this.isOpen.set(false)
      return
    }
    if (key !== this.lastRoute) {
      this.lastRoute = key
      this.isOpen.set(false)
    }
  })

  /** Registers the context on the host before any projected child resolves it. */
  constructor() {
    provideDomContext(this.host.nativeElement, new UserMenuPopoverContextValue(this))
  }

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * The container class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().position('relative'), this.className)
  }

  /**
   * The resolved display name (auth name, then email, then the guest label).
   * @returns The name.
   */
  displayName(): string {
    const user = this.auth.user()
    return (
      user?.name ??
      user?.email ??
      this.guestName ??
      this.i18n.t('userMenuPopover.guest', {}, { defaultValue: 'Account' })
    )
  }

  /** Schedules a re-render of this component's view. */
  markForCheck(): void {
    this.cdr.markForCheck()
  }

  /** Closes the popover on the first pass and keeps the outside listeners in sync. */
  ngAfterViewInit(): void {
    // React's route effect runs on mount and closes the (closed) popover.
    this.isOpen.set(false)
    this.lastOpen = false
  }

  /** Reconciles the document listeners for the open state. */
  ngDoCheck(): void {
    const open = this.isOpen()
    if (open !== this.lastOpen) {
      this.lastOpen = open
      if (open) this.addDocumentListeners()
      else this.removeDocumentListeners()
    }
  }

  /** Removes listeners and the context registration (unmount). */
  ngOnDestroy(): void {
    this.removeDocumentListeners()
    removeDomContext(this.host.nativeElement)
  }

  /** Adds the outside-click and Escape dismiss listeners while open. */
  private addDocumentListeners(): void {
    this.removeDocumentListeners()
    if (this.openListener) return
    this.openListener = true
    const onMouseDown = (event: MouseEvent): void => {
      if (!this.host.nativeElement.contains(event.target as Node)) {
        this.isOpen.set(false)
        this.cdr.markForCheck()
      }
    }
    const onKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent): void => {
      if (event.key === 'Escape') {
        this.isOpen.set(false)
        this.cdr.markForCheck()
      }
    }
    document.addEventListener('mousedown', onMouseDown)
    document.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown)
    this.listeners.push(
      () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', onMouseDown),
      () => document.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown),
    )
  }

  /** Removes the document listeners. */
  private removeDocumentListeners(): void {
    for (const off of this.listeners) off()
    this.listeners = []
    this.openListener = false
  }
}
```

#### `UserMenuPopoverTrigger`

The trigger: an initials avatar plus the account name and email, toggling
the popover (the disclosure `aria-expanded` + `aria-controls` contract).

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'button[mol-user-menu-popover-trigger]',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: ['ariaLabelKey', 'ariaLabelDefault', 'dataMolId', 'className'],
  host: {
    type: 'button',
    '[attr.aria-expanded]': 'open() ? "true" : "false"',
    '[attr.aria-controls]': 'context()?.panelId',
    '[attr.aria-label]': 'ariaLabel()',
    '[attr.data-mol-id]': 'dataMolId',
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '(click)': 'toggle()',
  },
  template: `<span aria-hidden="true" [class]="initialsClasses()" [style]="initialsStyle()">{{
      initials()
    }}</span
    ><span [class]="cm().cn(cm().flex1, cm().minW(0))"
      ><span [class]="nameClasses()">{{ displayName() }}</span>
      @if (email(); as accountEmail) {
        <span [class]="secondaryClasses()">{{ accountEmail }}</span>
      }
    </span>`,
})
export class UserMenuPopoverTrigger {
  /** i18n key for the trigger's aria-label (default `userMenu.open`). */
  ariaLabelKey?: string
  /** Fallback aria-label (default `Open user menu`). */
  ariaLabelDefault?: string
  /** `data-mol-id` for the trigger (default `user-menu`). */
  dataMolId = 'user-menu'
  /** Extra classes for the trigger. */
  className?: string

  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLElement> = inject(ElementRef)

  /**
   * Reactive translation, read in the injection context. Its `t` reads the
   * locale, so the view re-renders the text on a locale switch (the static `t`
   * does not).
   */
  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * The nearest popover context (React's context value).
   * @returns The context, or null outside a popover.
   */
  context(): UserMenuPopoverContext | null {
    return findDomContext<UserMenuPopoverContext>(this.host.nativeElement) ?? null
  }

  /**
   * Whether the popover is open.
   * @returns The open state (false outside a popover).
   */
  open(): boolean {
    return this.context()?.open ?? false
  }

  /**
   * The trigger's accessible label.
   * @returns The label text.
   */
  ariaLabel(): string {
    return this.i18n.t(
      this.ariaLabelKey ?? 'userMenu.open',
      {},
      {
        defaultValue: this.ariaLabelDefault ?? 'Open user menu',
      },
    )
  }

  /**
   * The resolved display name.
   * @returns The name.
   */
  displayName(): string {
    return this.context()?.displayName ?? ''
  }

  /**
   * The resolved account email.
   * @returns The email, or undefined.
   */
  email(): string | undefined {
    return this.context()?.email
  }

  /** The initials shown in the avatar badge. */
  initials(): string {
    return getInitials(this.displayName())
  }

  /** The trigger's class list. */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().flex({ align: 'center', gap: 'sm' }),
      this.cm().sp('px', 3),
      this.cm().sp('py', 3),
      this.cm().userMenuPopoverTrigger,
      this.className,
    )
  }

  /** The initials badge class list. */
  initialsClasses(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().w(10),
      this.cm().h(10),
      this.cm().roundedFull,
      this.cm().flex({ align: 'center', justify: 'center' }),
      this.cm().textSize('sm'),
      this.cm().fontWeight('bold'),
      this.cm().shrink0,
    )
  }

  /** The initials badge's inline style. */
  initialsStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText({
      backgroundColor: 'var(--color-primary)',
      color: 'var(--color-on-primary, #ffffff)',
    })
  }

  /** The display-name class list. */
  nameClasses(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().textSize('sm'),
      this.cm().fontWeight('semibold'),
      this.cm().displayBlock,
      this.cm().textTruncate,
    )
  }

  /** The email class list. */
  secondaryClasses(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().textSize('xs'),
      this.cm().displayBlock,
      this.cm().textTruncate,
      this.cm().textMuted,
    )
  }

  /** Toggles the popover (React's `setOpen(!open)`). */
  toggle(): void {
    this.context()?.setOpen(!this.open())
  }
}
```

#### `UserMenuPopoverPanel`

The popover panel: an absolutely-positioned card with an identity header
and a `<nav>` wrapping the caller's items. Renders nothing while closed.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-user-menu-popover-panel]',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: ['ariaLabelKey', 'ariaLabelDefault', 'dataMolId', 'className'],
  host: {
    'data-parity-unwrap': '',
    '[attr.data-open]': 'open() ? "true" : null',
    style: 'display: contents',
  },
  template: `<div #panel [id]="panelId()" [attr.data-mol-id]="dataMolId" [class]="classes()">
    <header [class]="cm().cn(cm().sp('pb', 4), cm().userMenuPopoverHeader)">
      <p [class]="cm().cn(cm().textSize('sm'), cm().fontWeight('semibold'))">{{ displayName() }}</p>
      @if (email(); as accountEmail) {
        <p [class]="cm().cn(cm().textSize('xs'), cm().textMuted)">{{ accountEmail }}</p>
      }
    </header>
    <nav [class]="cm().cn(cm().sp('mt', 4), cm().stack(1))" [attr.aria-label]="navLabel()">
      <ng-content></ng-content>
    </nav>
  </div>`,
})
export class UserMenuPopoverPanel implements AfterViewInit, DoCheck {
  /** i18n key for the panel's aria-label (default `userMenu.panelLabel`). */
  ariaLabelKey?: string
  /** Fallback aria-label (default `Account menu`). */
  ariaLabelDefault?: string
  /** `data-mol-id` for the panel (default `user-menu-panel`). */
  dataMolId = 'user-menu-panel'
  /** Extra classes for the panel. */
  className?: string

  /** The panel element (removed from the DOM while closed). */
  @ViewChild('panel') panel?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>

  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLElement> = inject(ElementRef)

  /**
   * Reactive translation, read in the injection context. Its `t` reads the
   * locale, so the view re-renders the text on a locale switch (the static `t`
   * does not).
   */
  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  private panelParent: Node | null = null
  private panelAnchor: Node | null = null
  private lastOpen = false

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * The nearest popover context.
   * @returns The context, or null outside a popover.
   */
  context(): UserMenuPopoverContext | null {
    return findDomContext<UserMenuPopoverContext>(this.host.nativeElement) ?? null
  }

  /**
   * Whether the popover is open.
   * @returns The open state.
   */
  open(): boolean {
    return this.context()?.open ?? false
  }

  /** The panel's shared id. */
  panelId(): string | null {
    return this.context()?.panelId ?? null
  }

  /** The resolved display name. */
  displayName(): string {
    return this.context()?.displayName ?? ''
  }

  /** The resolved account email. */
  email(): string | undefined {
    return this.context()?.email
  }

  /**
   * The nav's accessible label.
   * @returns The label text.
   */
  navLabel(): string {
    return this.i18n.t(
      this.ariaLabelKey ?? 'userMenu.panelLabel',
      {},
      {
        defaultValue: this.ariaLabelDefault ?? 'Account menu',
      },
    )
  }

  /**
   * The panel card class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().userMenuPopoverPanel, this.className)
  }

  /** Records where the panel lives and applies the initial open state. */
  ngAfterViewInit(): void {
    const panel = this.panel?.nativeElement
    if (!panel) return
    this.panelParent = panel.parentNode
    this.panelAnchor = panel.nextSibling
    this.lastOpen = this.open()
    if (!this.lastOpen) panel.remove()
  }

  /** Attaches or detaches the panel to match the open state (React renders null). */
  ngDoCheck(): void {
    const open = this.open()
    if (open === this.lastOpen) return
    this.lastOpen = open
    const panel = this.panel?.nativeElement
    if (!panel || !this.panelParent) return
    if (open) this.panelParent.insertBefore(panel, this.panelAnchor)
    else panel.remove()
  }
}
```

#### `UserMenuPopoverSignOut`

The sign-out nav item: closes the popover and signs out.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'button[mol-user-menu-popover-sign-out]',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: ['labelKey', 'labelDefault', 'dataMolId', 'className'],
  host: {
    type: 'button',
    '[attr.data-mol-id]': 'dataMolId',
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '(click)': 'signOut()',
  },
  template: `{{ label() }}`,
})
export class UserMenuPopoverSignOut {
  /** i18n key for the button label (default `userMenu.signOut`). */
  labelKey?: string
  /** Fallback label (default `Sign out`). */
  labelDefault?: string
  /** `data-mol-id` for the button (default `user-menu-sign-out`). */
  dataMolId = 'user-menu-sign-out'
  /** Extra classes for the button. */
  className?: string

  /** Auth state (the logout action). */
  readonly auth = useAuth()

  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLElement> = inject(ElementRef)

  /**
   * Reactive translation, read in the injection context. Its `t` reads the
   * locale, so the view re-renders the text on a locale switch (the static `t`
   * does not).
   */
  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * The nearest popover context.
   * @returns The context, or null outside a popover.
   */
  context(): UserMenuPopoverContext | null {
    return findDomContext<UserMenuPopoverContext>(this.host.nativeElement) ?? null
  }

  /**
   * The button label.
   * @returns The label text.
   */
  label(): string {
    return this.i18n.t(
      this.labelKey ?? 'userMenu.signOut',
      {},
      {
        defaultValue: this.labelDefault ?? 'Sign out',
      },
    )
  }

  /**
   * The button class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().sp('px', 3),
      this.cm().sp('py', 2),
      this.cm().textSize('sm'),
      this.cm().fontWeight('semibold'),
      this.cm().displayBlock,
      this.cm().w('full'),
      this.cm().textError,
      this.cm().userMenuPopoverSignOut,
      this.className,
    )
  }

  /** Closes the popover and signs out (React's click handler). */
  async signOut(): Promise<void> {
    this.context()?.setOpen(false)
    try {
      await this.auth.logout()
    } catch (_error) {
      // A logout failure should not crash the panel — the user is already
      // treated as signed out from the UI's perspective.
    }
  }
}
```

#### `UserMenu`

UserMenu component.

React's root is a FRAGMENT (the trigger button plus a drawer), so the host
is unwrapped (`data-parity-unwrap`, `display: contents`) and the trigger
button is adopted beside it — the rendered tree and focus paths match
React's (a route change must restore focus to the trigger). The panel
content is projected inside the drawer through `PanelCloseProvider`.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'mol-user-menu',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [Button, Icon, Modal, PanelCloseProvider],
  inputs: [
    'ariaLabelKey',
    'ariaLabelDefault',
    'triggerIcon',
    'triggerIconSize',
    'dataMolId',
    'className',
    'disabled',
    'onClick',
    'side',
  ],
  host: {
    'data-parity-unwrap': '',
    style: 'display: contents',
  },
  template: `<button
      #trigger
      mol-button
      [variant]="'ghost'"
      [size]="'sm'"
      [className]="triggerClasses()"
      [disabled]="!!disabled"
      (click)="handleTriggerClick($event)"
      [attr.aria-label]="ariaLabel()"
      [attr.data-mol-id]="dataMolId"
    >
      <svg
        mol-icon-root
        [name]="triggerIcon ?? 'user'"
        [size]="triggerIconSize ?? 20"
      ></svg></button
    ><mol-modal
      [open]="isOpen()"
      (close)="isOpen.set(false)"
      [className]="drawerClasses()"
      [attr.aria-label]="panelLabel()"
      ><mol-panel-close-provider [close]="closePanel"
        ><ng-content></ng-content></mol-panel-close-provider
    ></mol-modal>`,
})
export class UserMenu implements AfterViewInit, OnDestroy {
  /** i18n key for the trigger's aria-label (default `userMenu.open`). */
  ariaLabelKey?: string
  /** Fallback aria-label (default `Open user menu`). */
  ariaLabelDefault?: string
  /** Icon name for the trigger (default `user`). */
  triggerIcon?: IconName
  /** Pixel size for the trigger icon (default 20). */
  triggerIconSize?: number
  /** `data-mol-id` for the trigger (default `user-menu`). */
  dataMolId = 'user-menu'
  /** Extra classes composed onto the trigger. */
  className?: string
  /** Whether the trigger is disabled. */
  disabled?: boolean
  /** Overrides the trigger click (called instead of opening the panel). */
  onClick?: () => void
  /** Which side the panel opens from (default `right`). */
  side: 'left' | 'right' = 'right'

  /** Whether the drawer is open. */
  readonly isOpen = signal(false)
  /** Router state: a route change closes the drawer (React's effect). */
  readonly router = useRouter()

  /**
   * Reactive translation, read in the injection context. Its `t` reads the
   * locale, so the view re-renders the text on a locale switch (the static `t`
   * does not).
   */
  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  /** The trigger button (adopted beside the host). */
  @ViewChild('trigger', { read: ElementRef }) trigger?: ElementRef<HTMLButtonElement>

  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLElement> = inject(ElementRef)
  private adopted = false
  private readonly routeEffect = effect(() => {
    const location = this.router.location()
    void `${location.pathname}${location.search}`
    // React's effect runs on mount too; closing an already-closed drawer is a no-op.
    this.isOpen.set(false)
  })

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * The trigger's accessible label.
   * @returns The label text.
   */
  ariaLabel(): string {
    return this.i18n.t(
      this.ariaLabelKey ?? 'userMenu.open',
      {},
      {
        defaultValue: this.ariaLabelDefault ?? 'Open user menu',
      },
    )
  }

  /**
   * The drawer panel's accessible label.
   * @returns The label text.
   */
  panelLabel(): string {
    return this.i18n.t('userMenu.panelLabel', {}, { defaultValue: 'Account menu' })
  }

  /**
   * The trigger's class list (a 40x40 minimum target plus the caller's classes).
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  triggerClasses(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().minH(10), this.cm().minW(10), this.className)
  }

  /**
   * The drawer's class list (side-aware).
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  drawerClasses(): string {
    return this.side === 'left'
      ? this.cm().cn(this.cm().drawer, this.cm().drawerLeft)
      : this.cm().drawer
  }

  /** Closes the drawer (the panel-close context's callback). */
  closePanel = (): void => {
    this.isOpen.set(false)
  }

  /**
   * Opens the drawer unless a custom click handler intercepts (React passes
   * the handler straight to the button, so it receives the click event).
   *
   * @param event - The click event.
   */
  handleTriggerClick(event: MouseEvent): void {
    if (this.onClick) (this.onClick as (e: MouseEvent) => void)(event)
    else this.isOpen.set(true)
  }

  /** Adopts the trigger beside the host (React's fragment root). */
  ngAfterViewInit(): void {
    const trigger = this.trigger?.nativeElement
    const host = this.host.nativeElement
    if (trigger && host.parentNode) {
      host.parentNode.insertBefore(trigger, host)
      this.adopted = true
    }
  }

  /** Restores the adopted trigger (unmount). */
  ngOnDestroy(): void {
    if (this.adopted) this.host.nativeElement.appendChild(this.trigger?.nativeElement as Node)
  }
}
```

#### `MolIconSvg`

Internal SVG renderer used by every React-parity component where React
renders through `renderIcon` / `Icon`.

The component's selector is an attribute on the `<svg>` itself
(`<svg mol-icon …>`), so the host IS the svg element and no wrapper
element exists. Attribute parity with React's `renderIcon`:
`xmlns`, `aria-hidden="true"`, `focusable="false"`, `class`, `viewBox`,
`fill` (or the stroke group when the icon is stroked) and `<path>` children.
`icon.svg`-backed glyphs inject the bond's raw inner markup (the bond is
the trust boundary, exactly as React's `dangerouslySetInnerHTML`).

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'svg[mol-icon]',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: ['data'],
  host: {
    xmlns: 'http://www.w3.org/2000/svg',
    '[attr.aria-hidden]': '"true"',
    '[attr.focusable]': '"false"',
    '[class]': 'data?.className',
    '[attr.viewBox]': 'data?.viewBox',
    '[attr.fill]': 'fill',
    '[attr.stroke]': 'strokeAttrs.stroke',
    '[attr.stroke-width]': 'strokeAttrs.strokeWidth',
    '[attr.stroke-linecap]': 'strokeAttrs.strokeLinecap',
    '[attr.stroke-linejoin]': 'strokeAttrs.strokeLinejoin',
  },
  template: `@for (p of paths; track $index) {
    <svg:path
      [attr.d]="p.d"
      [attr.fill]="p.fill"
      [attr.fill-rule]="p.fillRule"
      [attr.clip-rule]="p.clipRule"
      [attr.opacity]="p.opacity"
    />
  }`,
})
export class MolIconSvg implements OnChanges {
  /** The resolved icon data (plain field input, JIT-visible). */
  data?: ResolvedIcon

  /**
   * The fill attribute value: `none` for stroked icons, the icon fill or
   * `currentColor` otherwise.
   * @returns The fill value.
   */
  get fill(): string {
    return this.data?.stroked ? 'none' : (this.data?.icon.fill ?? 'currentColor') || 'currentColor'
  }

  /**
   * Stroke attribute values (null when the icon is not stroked).
   * @returns The stroke attribute bag.
   */
  get strokeAttrs(): {
    stroke: string | null
    strokeWidth: number | null
    strokeLinecap: string | null
    strokeLinejoin: string | null
  } {
    if (!this.data?.stroked) {
      return { stroke: null, strokeWidth: null, strokeLinecap: null, strokeLinejoin: null }
    }
    const icon = this.data.icon
    return {
      stroke: icon.stroke ?? null,
      strokeWidth: icon.strokeWidth ?? null,
      strokeLinecap: icon.strokeLinecap ?? null,
      strokeLinejoin: icon.strokeLinejoin ?? null,
    }
  }

  /**
   * The paths to render (empty for `icon.svg`-backed glyphs).
   * @returns The path list.
   */
  get paths(): IconData['paths'] {
    return this.data?.icon.svg ? [] : (this.data?.icon.paths ?? [])
  }

  /**
   * Injects raw `icon.svg` markup into the svg host when the icon carries it.
   *
   * @param changes - The changed inputs.
   */
  ngOnChanges(changes: { data?: { currentValue: ResolvedIcon } }): void {
    if (!changes.data) return
    const svg = changes.data.currentValue.icon.svg
    if (svg) (this.host.nativeElement as SVGSVGElement).innerHTML = svg
  }

  private readonly host: ElementRef<SVGSVGElement> = inject(ElementRef)
}
```

### Functions

#### `hasNodes(el)`

Whether an ng-content wrapper captured any element or text node.

```typescript
function hasNodes(el: Element | undefined | null): boolean
```

- `el` — The wrapper element.

**Returns:** True when at least one projected node exists.

#### `getModalWrapperStyle(centered)`

Get the inline style for the modal centering wrapper. `dialogWrapper` has
no dedicated ClassMap resolver option (it's a fixed token, not a
`{ centered }`-parameterized one), so a top-anchored (non-centered) layout
is expressed via the one inline-style exception the workspace styling rule
allows for values ClassMap genuinely cannot express.

A standalone function (rather than a class member) so it's unit-testable
without constructing `MoleculeModal` — the class can't be `new`'d outside
an Angular injection context (`sanitizer = inject(DomSanitizer)` runs at
field-initializer time).

```typescript
function getModalWrapperStyle(centered: boolean): Record<string, string> | undefined
```

- `centered` — Whether the modal is vertically centered.

**Returns:** An inline style object, or `undefined` when centered.

#### `getTooltipArrowStyle(placement)`

Returns the arrow's inline position style for a given placement. The arrow
has no dedicated ClassMap resolver (`tooltip()` takes no options), so its
shape/position is expressed via a small inline style — the sanctioned
exception for values ClassMap cannot express. Its color is NOT hardcoded:
`var(--color-surface)` / `var(--color-border)` are the same CSS custom
properties the `bg-surface`/`border` classes in `tooltipContent` resolve
to, so the arrow always matches the tooltip body in both themes.

A standalone function (rather than a class member) so it's unit-testable
in isolation, matching `getModalWrapperStyle()` in `modal.component.ts`.

```typescript
function getTooltipArrowStyle(placement: TooltipPlacement): Record<string, string>
```

- `placement` — The tooltip's resolved placement.

**Returns:** Inline style positioning the rotated-square arrow for that placement.

#### `usePanelClose()`

Returns the callback that dismisses the drawer the current panel is
mounted in. Safe to call anywhere — a no-op outside a provider.

Must be called in an injection context (a component field initializer or
constructor).

```typescript
function usePanelClose(): () => void
```

**Returns:** A function that closes the enclosing drawer, or a no-op.

#### `useToast()`

Accesses the toast context for adding and removing toasts.

Must be called in an injection context (a component field initializer or
constructor).

```typescript
function useToast(): ToastContextValue
```

**Returns:** The toast context value.

#### `useUserMenuPopoverClose()`

Returns a callback that closes the enclosing `UserMenuPopover` (a no-op
outside one).

Must be called in an injection context (a component field initializer or
constructor).

```typescript
function useUserMenuPopoverClose(): () => void
```

**Returns:** A function that closes the popover.

#### `useIsDesktop(query)`

Whether the viewport is at least 768px wide (the fleet's `md` breakpoint).

Reads `window.matchMedia` synchronously when the value is created, so the
first render is already correct (no mobile-then-desktop swap frame), then
follows `change` events. Guarded for non-browser environments (SSR/node):
`false` (the mobile shell) when no `window.matchMedia` exists.

Must be called in an injection context (a component field initializer or
constructor).

```typescript
function useIsDesktop(query?: string): Signal<boolean>
```

- `query` — Override the media query. Defaults to `(min-width: 768px)`.

**Returns:** A signal of whether the desktop shell should mount.

#### `provideDomContext(host, value)`

Registers a context value on a provider's host element.

```typescript
function provideDomContext(host: Element, value: unknown): void
```

- `host` — The provider component's host element.
- `value` — The context value.

#### `removeDomContext(host)`

Removes a registration (on provider destroy).

```typescript
function removeDomContext(host: Element): void
```

- `host` — The provider component's host element.

#### `findDomContext(start)`

Finds the nearest enclosing context value for a consumer's element.

```typescript
function findDomContext<T>(start: Element | null): T | undefined
```

- `start` — The consumer's element (its own registrations are ignored — a provider's children see the provider, the provider does not see itself).

**Returns:** The nearest registered value, or `undefined` when there is none.

#### `findDomContextIncludingSelf(start)`

Finds the nearest enclosing context INCLUDING the element itself.

```typescript
function findDomContextIncludingSelf<T>(start: Element | null): T | undefined
```

- `start` — The element to start from.

**Returns:** The nearest registered value, or `undefined` when there is none.

#### `getFocusableElements(container)`

Returns the tab-stop elements inside a container, in DOM (tab) order.

```typescript
function getFocusableElements(container: HTMLElement): HTMLElement[]
```

- `container` — The element to search within.

**Returns:** The focusable descendants, in document order.

#### `createScrollLock()`

Reference-counted body scroll lock. Two dialogs can be open at once (a
drawer with a confirm above it); a shared counter means closing either one
only restores `overflow` when the last one closes.

One counter per call site family — React keeps Modal's and the responsive
shell drawer's counters in separate module scopes, so each `createScrollLock()`
result is independent, matching that behavior.

```typescript
function createScrollLock(): {
  /** Locks body scroll on the first caller; later callers just increment the count. */
  lock: () => void
  /** Decrements the count; restores the original overflow only once it reaches zero. */
  unlock: () => void
}
```

**Returns:** Lock/unlock functions with a shared reference count.

#### `fieldContext(host)`

Finds the nearest enclosing `FormField` context for a field's host element.

Resolved on every read: the field's own view is created before `FormField`
moves its projected children into place, so a value captured once at
construction could be stale.

```typescript
function fieldContext(host: Element | null): FormFieldContext | null
```

- `host` — The field component's host element.

**Returns:** The context, or null outside a `FormField`.

#### `formSubmitErrorMessages(error, fallback)`

Reads auth/HTTP response bodies, field-error dictionaries and ordinary
failures. Server text is rendered as text by Form; malformed or empty
bodies use the fallback.

```typescript
function formSubmitErrorMessages(error: unknown, fallback: string): string[]
```

- `error` — A rejected submission or a controlled server error.
- `fallback` — The already translated generic failure message.

**Returns:** Nonempty messages suitable for a visible error list.

#### `getIconSvg(name, className)` _(deprecated)_

Generates SVG markup string from an icon name.

```typescript
function getIconSvg(name: IconName, className: string): string
```

- `name` — Icon name from the icon set
- `className` — CSS class for sizing

**Returns:** SVG markup string

#### `nextInstanceId()`

Mints a unique DOM id. React's `useId()` produces per-render-instance ids
that the conformance normalizer renumbers by document order, so the exact
format does not matter — only uniqueness and stable per-instance identity
(one call per logical id, kept in a field).

```typescript
function nextInstanceId(): string
```

**Returns:** A unique id string.

#### `getInitials(value)`

Compute up-to-two-letter initials from a display name or email.

```typescript
function getInitials(value: string | null | undefined): string
```

- `value` — The name or email to derive initials from.

**Returns:** The uppercased initials, or `'?'` when no usable input is given.

#### `asTemplate(value)`

Whether a node-valued prop arrived as a TemplateRef.

```typescript
function asTemplate(value: unknown): TemplateRef<unknown> | null
```

- `value` — The prop value.

**Returns:** The TemplateRef, or null for any other value.

#### `nodeText(value)`

Renders a plain-string node value (React renders strings as text).

```typescript
function nodeText(value: unknown): string
```

- `value` — The prop value.

**Returns:** The text, or empty.

#### `isGiven(value)`

Whether a node-valued prop was passed at all (presence check for
conditional wrappers).

```typescript
function isGiven(value: unknown): boolean
```

- `value` — The prop value.

**Returns:** True when given.

#### `shallowEqual(a, b)`

Shallow equality (React's `@molecule/app-state` semantics).

```typescript
function shallowEqual(a: unknown, b: unknown): boolean
```

- `a` — One value.
- `b` — The other value.

**Returns:** Whether the values are shallowly equal.

#### `createOptimisticValue(options)`

Creates the optimistic-value controller.

```typescript
function createOptimisticValue<T>(options: OptimisticValueOptions<T>): OptimisticValue<T>
```

- `options` — Initial value, commit and callbacks.

**Returns:** The controller.

#### `resolveIcon(name, className)`

Resolves an icon from the bonded set, throwing on unknown names exactly
like `@molecule/app-icons`'s `getIcon` (an unknown name is a runtime error,
not a blank).

```typescript
function resolveIcon(name: IconName, className?: string): ResolvedIcon
```

- `name` — The icon name.
- `className` — The class for the `<svg>` (a `cm.*` token).

**Returns:** The resolved icon data.

#### `moveRestAttributes(host, target)`

Moves every rest attribute (`aria-*`, `data-*`, `id`, …) from the host
element onto the element React spreads `{...rest}` onto.

Adapter bindings write each attribute once (case props are static), so a
move at first view init is stable.

```typescript
function moveRestAttributes(host: Element, target: Element | undefined | null): string[]
```

- `host` — The component host element.
- `target` — The element React's `...rest` targets.

**Returns:** The moved attribute names.

#### `kebab(key)`

Converts a camelCase CSS property name to kebab-case.

```typescript
function kebab(key: string): string
```

- `key` — The camelCase property name.

**Returns:** The kebab-case CSS property name.

#### `styleText(style)`

Serializes a React-style style object the way React writes it to the DOM:
camelCase keys become kebab-case, numbers gain `px` unless the property is
unitless, and declaration order follows object key order (the conformance
normalizer sorts declarations anyway).

```typescript
function styleText(style: Record<string, unknown> | undefined | null): string | null
```

- `style` — The style object (or undefined).

**Returns:** The `style` attribute text, or null when there is nothing to set.

#### `cn(inputs)`

Merge class names, filtering out falsy values.

```typescript
function cn(...inputs: (string | undefined | null | false)[]): string
```

- `inputs` — The inputs.

**Returns:** The resulting string.

### Constants

#### `FOCUSABLE_SELECTOR`

Elements the WAI-ARIA APG dialog pattern treats as tab-stops for the focus trap.

```typescript
const FOCUSABLE_SELECTOR: 'a[href], button:not([disabled]), textarea:not([disabled]), input:not([disabled]), select:not([disabled]), [tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])'
```

## Injection Notes

### Requirements

Peer dependencies:

- `@angular/common` ^22.0.0
- `@angular/core` ^22.0.0
- `@angular/platform-browser` ^22.0.0
- `@molecule/app-angular` ^3.4.0
- `@molecule/app-i18n` ^1.0.1
- `@molecule/app-icons` ^1.0.1
- `@molecule/app-ui` ^1.0.1

### Runtime Dependencies

- `@angular/common`
- `@angular/core`
- `@angular/platform-browser`
- `@molecule/app-angular`
- `@molecule/app-i18n`
- `@molecule/app-icons`
- `@molecule/app-ui`

- **`setClassMap()` must run before any component renders** — every component resolves its
  classes through `getClassMap()`, which THROWS until a ClassMap bond (e.g.
  `@molecule/app-ui-tailwind`) is set. Call it in `main.ts` before `bootstrapApplication`.
- Components are standalone — add them to the `imports` array of the consuming component or
  route; there is no NgModule.
- A component's selector names the native element React renders at its root (an attribute
  selector such as `button[mol-button]`), so the host IS the root element. Components whose
  React root is a fragment or portal use an element selector whose host is
  `display: contents`.
- Hooks (`useToast`, `usePanelClose`, `useIsDesktop`, `useUserMenuPopoverClose`) are called in
  an injection context (a component field initializer or constructor).
- The original first-pass components remain exported under their `Molecule*` names
  (`MoleculeButton`, `MoleculeInput`, `MoleculeModal`, …) as deprecated aliases.

## Translations

Translation strings are provided by `@molecule/app-locales-ui`.
